These findings suggest that fraud robustness evaluation should report predictive degradation and attack feasibility jointly, and should incorporate domain constraints into attack generation rather than treating them as post-processing checks.

</details>


### [61] [SeisMamba: Low-Latency Single-Station Seismic Magnitude Estimation for Spatially Distributed Earthquake Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24561)
*Quenton Yeo,Zhaoge Bi,Linghan Huang,Luke Stephen Higgins,Flora Salim,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeisMamba是一种基于Mamba架构的轻量级单站地震震级快速估计模型，通过层级卷积编码、稀疏选择性状态空间建模、多尺度特征融合和辅助时间预测头实现低延迟高精度震级估计。在STEAD基准上以0.55毫秒的批处理时间取得最优MSE、RMSE和R²指标，比Transformer基线快约3倍，并在智利-台湾跨区域留出实验中展现出良好的地理泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地震早期预警系统依赖密集区域地震台网和区域特定校准，存在空间覆盖盲区（尤其针对传感基础设施稀疏的高风险地区）。单站学习方法成本更低，但面临精度-延迟权衡以及区域分布偏移导致的性能退化问题。因此需要一种既轻量又能在跨区域泛化的震级估计架构。

Method: SeisMamba采用Mamba（选择性状态空间模型）架构，具体包含：1）层级卷积编码器提取波形局部特征；2）稀疏选择性状态空间模型处理长序列波形；3）多尺度特征融合捕获不同时间尺度信息；4）辅助时间预测头提升模型训练效率。输入为单站三分量地震波形，仅需最小预处理。

Result: 在STEAD基准上，SeisMamba在MSE、RMSE和R²指标上均优于所有对比基线；在NVIDIA T4 GPU上处理32个波形的批次仅需0.55毫秒，约为Transformer基线速度的3倍。智利-台湾区域留出实验中，模型在地理上未见过的地震区域仍保持有效性能。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间波形建模为空间分布、低成本的早期地震预警提供了有前景的精度-延迟骨干方案，能够在保持高精度的同时显著降低计算开销，并具备跨区域部署的泛化能力。

Abstract: Rapid earthquake magnitude estimation is central to earthquake early warning, yet many operational systems depend on dense regional seismic networks and region-specific calibration. This creates a spatial coverage barrier for high-risk areas with sparse sensing infrastructure. Single-station learning offers a lower-cost alternative, but existing models often face an accuracy--latency trade-off and may degrade under regional distribution shift. We present SeisMamba, a lightweight Mamba-based architecture for low-latency magnitude estimation from minimally processed three-component seismic waveforms recorded at a single station. SeisMamba combines hierarchical convolutional encoding, sparse selective state-space modelling, multi-scale feature fusion, and an auxiliary temporal prediction head to support efficient long-sequence waveform analysis. On the STEAD benchmark, SeisMamba achieves the best MSE, RMSE, and $R^2$ among tested baselines while requiring only 0.55 ms for a batch of 32 waveforms on an NVIDIA T4 GPU, making it about three times faster than transformer-based baselines. We further conduct a Chile--Taiwan regional hold-out experiment as a diagnostic test of cross-region deployment, where SeisMamba retains useful performance on geographically unseen seismic regions. These results suggest that selective state-space waveform modelling provides a promising accuracy--latency backbone for spatially distributed, low-cost earthquake early warning.

</details>


### [62] [Across the Loss Landscape with Progressive Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24568)
*Paul Caillon,Christophe Cerisara,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了增量式"增长-优化"（grow-and-optimize）训练策略如何通过渐进约束松弛，在非凸、过参数化的深度神经网络损失景观中偏向更平坦的区域。作者通过逐步解锁嵌套随机子空间并冻结其正交补空间的方法，证明了渐进增长通过体积效应增加了宽盆地（平坦极小值）的相对权重并抑制尖锐极小值，并在玩具景观和ResNet/CIFAR-100上验证了该预测，但同时也发现曲率降低并不必然带来泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络尽管损失景观高度非凸且过参数化，但仍能良好泛化，这一现象常与随机优化找到的极小值几何性质相关。理解增量式增长策略如何将训练偏向更平坦区域，对于揭示平坦性与泛化之间的内在联系具有重要意义。

Method: 将网络增长建模为渐进约束松弛过程：从低维子模型出发，迭代地解锁嵌套随机子空间，同时将正交补空间冻结在初始化值上，每次扩展后重新优化。在非退化极小值附近的局部正则性假设下，证明局部子水平集可由椭球近似，并通过冻结方向上的显式有效曲率刻画盆地可达性，由此建立渐进增长偏向宽盆地的理论解释。

Result: 证明了渐进增长通过冻结约束诱导的体积效应增加宽盆地相对权重、抑制尖锐盆地的理论结果；在受控玩具景观和ResNet/CIFAR-100设定中的实验验证了渐进子空间增长确实能稳定产生更平坦的解，但曲率降低并不普遍转化为测试性能的提升，揭示了平坦性-泛化联系的复杂性。

Conclusion: 渐进子空间增长作为一种可解释的平坦解偏好机制，能够可靠地引导优化到更平坦区域，但平坦性并非泛化性能的充分或必要条件，二者的关系需要更细致的理解。

Abstract: Deep neural networks generalize well despite their highly nonconvex, overparameterized loss landscapes, a phenomenon often associated with the geometry of the minima found by stochastic optimization. We study how incremental grow-and-optimize strategies bias training toward flatter regions by viewing growth as progressive constraint relaxation. Starting from a low-dimensional submodel, we iteratively expand the trainable parameters by unlocking nested random subspaces while freezing the orthogonal complement at the network initialization, re-optimizing after each expansion until the full architecture is reached. Under standard local regularity conditions around non-degenerate minima, we prove that local sublevel sets are well approximated by ellipsoids and that basin accessibility under frozen constraints can be characterized by an explicit effective curvature in the frozen directions. This leads to an explanation of the bias: progressive growth increases the relative weight of wide basins and suppresses sharp ones through a volume effect induced by the frozen constraints. We empirically validate these predictions in controlled toy landscapes and in a realistic ResNet/CIFAR-100 setting and confirm that although progressive subspace growth reliably produces flatter solutions, curvature reductions do not universally translate into improved test performance, highlighting subtleties in the flatness-generalization connection. The code is available at https://github.com/p0lcAi/Across-the-Loss-Landscape.

</details>


### [63] [IAPO: Influence-Aware Policy Optimization for Credit Assignment in Multi-Turn Service Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24588)
*Bo Ren,Yirong Mao,Yi Yang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出影响感知策略优化（IAPO），通过将每个rollout表示为带类型的影响依赖图，利用用户和工具观测作为证据，将同一轨迹级优势重新分配到各个行动上，从而在稀疏结果反馈下更精确地完成多轮LLM agent的信用分配。实验表明IAPO在三个服务agent基准和BFCL-v4多轮函数调用上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM agent在长时程任务中，任务信息随时间逐步展开而非初始提示中完全给定，最终的奖励无法指示哪些行动真正贡献于任务解决。现有的信用分配方法依赖其他轨迹的比较证据、重新采样的延续或单独构建的步骤级学习信号，但这些方法未能充分利用已完成rollout中信息与错误流经各行动之间的结构性依赖关系。

Method: IAPO将每个rollout表示为带类型的影响依赖图（typed influence-dependency graph），节点为可训练的agent行动，用户和工具的观测作为证据。该方法将支持-使用（support-use）和失败-使用（failed-use）结构转化为路由权重，用于重新分配同一轨迹级优势（trajectory-level advantage）到各agent行动上。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，IAPO在τ²-Bench、UserBench和AgentChangeBench三个服务agent基准上均优于多轮RL基线；在BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn上进一步证明性能增益不会损害多轮函数调用能力。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对多轮用户交互中信用分配问题的理解，为从稀疏结果反馈训练服务agent提供了有原则性的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly solve long-horizon tasks through multi-turn interactions with users and external tools. In these settings, relevant task information often unfolds over time rather than being fully specified at the initial prompt. Service agents make this challenge especially concrete: users may clarify or revise their goals, while tool responses provide information needed for subsequent decisions. Thus, a final reward alone cannot indicate which actions contributed to resolving the task. Recent methods rely on comparative evidence from other trajectories or resampled continuations, or on separately constructed step-level learning signals, to refine credit. However, a completed rollout already records how information and errors flow between agent actions. We introduce Influence-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO), which represents each rollout as a typed influence-dependency graph over trainable agent actions, with user and tool observations serving as evidence. IAPO converts support-use and failed-use structure into routing weights that redistribute the same trajectory-level advantage. Experiments with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B demonstrate superior performance over multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) baselines across three service-agent benchmarks: {τ^2}-Bench, UserBench, and AgentChangeBench. BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn further shows that these gains do not compromise multi-turn function-calling performance. This work advances the understanding of credit assignment in multi-turn user interactions and provides a principled approach to training service agents from sparse outcome feedback.

</details>


### [64] [Delayed Optimizer-State Transport Shapes Short-Horizon Training Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24593)
*Jinhui Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应优化器（如AdamW）中动量状态对梯度历史的"延迟传输"效应，即局部损失加权变化是否会影响后续数个step的更新。通过八步AdamW轨迹的全状态求导及Math-Code损失调度对比实验，发现完整传输在12个Transformer历史中的10个上优于即时导数（平均收益4.71e-4，p=0.0193），表明优化器记忆与近期数据顺序是训练状态中可操作的组成部分，为有限时域干预而非单步干预提供了机制性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器在动量变量中保留梯度历史，使得局部损失加权的改变可能通过延迟路径影响后续更新。作者希望检验这种延迟传输是否足够大，以至于能够改变未来短期（short-horizon）的决策顺序，从而质疑传统的单步（one-step）干预方法是否充分。

Method: 1) 在固定的未来小批量序列上，对八步AdamW轨迹穿过完整的模型-优化器状态进行微分；2) 选择暴露匹配（exposure-matched）的Math-Code损失调度方案进行对比；3) 使用12个未使用的0.3M Transformer训练历史进行独立评估；4) 通过完整传输（full transport）与优化器感知的即时导数（optimizer-aware immediate derivative）对比token不重叠损失；5) 进行交叉检查点-未来路径测试及独立的Ising-CNN实验，验证动量状态传输的必要性；6) 在更大的候选库中评估精确rollout获胜者的聚集程度。

Result: 完整传输在12个历史中的10个上降低了token不重叠损失（平均收益4.71e-4；精确单侧符号检验p=0.0193）。两个控制器行动频率相同但在60/96个时间窗口中选择不同的调度。交叉检查点-未来路径测试表明，这种重排序源于优化器状态与近期未来数据的交互作用。独立的Ising-CNN实验显示，删除动量状态传输会破坏准确的响应预测。全传输分数在更大候选库中聚焦了精确rollout的获胜者，将有限振幅评估集中在短名单上。

Conclusion: 在承诺的短路径上，优化器记忆与近期数据顺序是训练状态中可操作（actionable）的组成部分。作者提供了基于机制的判据，说明何时需要采用有限时域（finite-horizon）而非单步（one-step）干预。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers retain gradient history in moment variables, allowing a local change in loss weighting to alter later updates. We examine whether this delayed transport is large enough to change prospective short-horizon decisions. On committed future-minibatch sequences, we differentiate eight-step AdamW trajectories through the complete model--optimizer state and select exposure-matched Math--Code loss schedules before independent evaluation. Across 12 unused 0.3M Transformer histories, full transport lowers token-disjoint loss relative to an optimizer-aware immediate derivative in 10/12 histories (mean benefit $4.71\times10^{-4}$; exact one-sided sign test, $p=0.0193$). The two controllers act equally often but select different schedules in 60/96 windows. Crossed checkpoint--future-path tests attribute this reordering to the interaction between optimizer state and near-future data, while an independent Ising--CNN experiment shows that deleting moment-state transport destroys accurate response prediction. Full-transport scores also concentrate exact-rollout winners in larger candidate libraries, focusing finite-amplitude evaluation on a shortlist. On these committed short paths, optimizer memory and near-future data order are therefore actionable components of the training state, providing a mechanism-based criterion for when finite-horizon rather than one-step intervention is required.

</details>


### [65] [Taming foundation model with invariance-oriented pre-training for broad-spectrum EEG analysis across signal-level, brain-state, and brain-health tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24597)
*Yulong Dou,Han Wu,Guo Chen,Fangmao Ju,Zhiming Cui,Dinggang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出INCEPT，一个面向EEG的基于不变性学习的预训练基础模型，在超过11,000小时的无标注临床EEG数据上训练。与传统的重构中心范式不同，INCEPT学习跨相关EEG观测的表征级稳定性，分离稳定的神经结构和关键个体敏感信息与头皮记录中的噪声变异性，同时保留受试者、状态和条件判别信息。在覆盖三个分析层次（信号级评估、脑状态解码、脑健康评估）的十个数据集基准上，INCEPT在30个线性探测指标中的26个和30个微调指标中的24个上排名第一，并超越领域特化的编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数EEG模型局限于单数据集-单模型的监督范式，而现有的EEG基础模型多为重构中心，假设可从局部上下文预测的EEG内容必定是可迁移的神经信息。这一假设忽略了头皮记录中大量的干扰变异性，限制了表示的迁移性。

Method: INCEPT采用不变性导向的预训练策略：在超过11,000小时无标签临床EEG上训练，学习相关EEG观测之间的表征级稳定性。其核心思想是分离稳定的神经结构和受试者敏感信息，同时丢弃主导头皮记录的扰动词汇（nuisance variability），同时保留受试者、状态和条件判别性信息。

Result: 在十个数据集、覆盖信号级评估、脑状态解码和脑健康评估三个层次的广泛基准上，INCEPT在30个线性探测指标中的26个和30个微调指标中的24个上排名第一，且超越强任务特化编码器。消融实验和表征分析表明不变性预训练优于仅重构的预训练，且能更好地组织受试者敏感的神经表征。

Conclusion: 不变性学习是构建可复用EEG基础模型的有效原则，能够超越重构中心范式，实现更好的迁移性能和神经表征组织，为EEG基础模型的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely used window into human brain function, but most EEG models remain tied to a one-dataset-one-model supervised paradigm. Recent EEG foundation models offer a route toward reusable representations, but most remain reconstruction-centered, assuming that EEG content predictable from local context is necessarily transferable neural information. Here we present INCEPT, an invariance-oriented EEG foundation model trained on over 11,000 hours of unlabelled clinical EEG. Rather than prioritizing signal recovery alone, INCEPT learns representation-level stability across correlated EEG observations, separating stable neural structure and essential subject-sensitive information from the nuisance variability that dominates scalp recordings while preserving subject-, state- and condition-discriminative information. We evaluate INCEPT on a broad-spectrum benchmark of ten datasets spanning three levels of post-acquisition EEG analysis: signal-level assessment, brain-state decoding, and brain-health evaluation. INCEPT ranks first among recent EEG foundation models on 26 of 30 linear-probing metrics and 24 of 30 fine-tuning metrics, and also surpasses strong task-specific specialist encoders across diverse downstream settings. Objective ablations and representation analyses further show that invariance-oriented pre-training improves transfer and organizes subject-sensitive neural representations beyond reconstruction alone. These results establish invariance learning as a promising principle for building reusable EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [66] [Conditional GraphGANFed: Optimizing Graph-Structured Molecule Generation in Federated Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24610)
*Daniel Manu,Abee Alazzwi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cGraphGANFed，将critic网络引入GraphGANFed框架，在联邦学习环境下利用用户自定义指标引导GAN生成新型药物分子，在Validity、LogP和QED等指标上显著优于原方法，并增强了对抗模式塌缩和非独立同分布数据的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphGANFed虽能通过联邦学习与图卷积网络高效训练GAN并保护数据隐私，但无法生成仅优化用户自定义指标的合成分子，限制了其在药物发现中的应用价值。

Method: 在GraphGANFed架构中引入critic网络，用于评估生成分子是否符合用户自定义指标；将critic网络与判别器的评价结果共同整合进生成器的损失函数，引导生成器在保持分子化学性质相似的同时优化用户定义指标。

Result: 在优化全部七种常用指标的实验中，cGraphGANFed在Validity和LogP上显著优于GraphGANFed，在QED上略有优势；在仅优化QED的实验中，合成分子的QED提升了超过10%；同时cGraphGANFed对模式塌缩和非IID数据导致的性能下降具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: cGraphGANFed成功解决了GraphGANFed无法按用户需求定向优化分子指标的问题，在分子生成质量和抗退化能力方面均表现更优，为联邦学习框架下的目标导向药物发现提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Generative adversarial networks (GANs) have garnered considerable attention in molecular discovery for their ability to generate novel and high-quality molecules. To efficiently train a GAN model while preserving data privacy, GraphGANFed has been proposed to incorporate federated learning and graph convolutional networks into GAN. Yet, GraphGANFed cannot produce synthetic molecules that only optimize a user-defined metric(s) to facilitate the new drug discovery process. To address this issue, we introduce a novel extension to GraphGANFed, namely conditional GraphGANFed (cGraphGANFed), by incorporating the critic network to assess generated molecules using user-defined metric(s). The evaluation results from both the critic network and discriminator are integrated into the loss function of the generator, guiding it to generate novel molecules that maintain similar chemical properties to real ones while optimizing user-defined metrics. Extensive simulations are conducted in two scenarios. First, cGraphGANFed endeavors to optimize all seven commonly used metrics, and the results show that cGraphGANFed significantly outperforms GraphGANFed in Validity and LogP, with a slight advantage in QED, across different settings. Second, cGraphGANFed focuses solely on optimizing QED, and the results show that the synthetic molecules produced by cGraphGANFed can achieve more than 10% improvement in QED than GraphGANFed. Also, the results demonstrate cGraphGANFed has enhanced resilience against mode collapses and performance reduction caused by non-IID data.

</details>


### [67] [Bandit Submodular Maximization under Matroid Constraints: Learning Compressed Exchange Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24627)
*Zongqi Wan,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在拟阵约束下通过对抗性bandit进行单调子模函数最大化的问题，提出了首个多项式时间、次线性遗憾的随机化算法，实现了期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性bandit子模函数最大化在一般拟阵约束下此前没有次线性遗憾的算法，这是一个重要的开放问题。作者旨在填补这一空白，同时克服直接学习指数多个交换策略所带来的时间和空间瓶颈。

Method: 将问题视为在Poisson基础游标上学习交换策略，将其与上下文bandit联系起来获得信息论意义上的次线性遗憾保证。为解决策略数量指数级的问题，引入"balanced fractional exchanges"（平衡分数交换），将策略混合体压缩为单个分数基（fractional base），同时保留Poisson分析所需的交换信息，从而实现多项式时间算法。

Result: 提出了一个随机化的oracle-多项式算法，每个回合只需一次可行值查询，期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})，这是首个在一般拟阵约束下具有次线性遗憾的对抗性bandit子模最大化算法。

Conclusion: 通过平衡分数交换技术，成功将指数级策略学习问题压缩为多项式可处理的形式，在保持理论遗憾保证的同时实现了实际可行的算法复杂度。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit maximization of monotone submodular functions under a matroid constraint. For a rank-$k$ matroid on $n$ elements, we give a randomized oracle-polynomial algorithm that makes one feasible value query per round and has expected $(1-1/e)$-regret $\widetilde O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})$. This is the first sublinear-regret algorithm for adversarial bandit submodular maximization under general matroid constraints.
  Technically, we view the problem as learning an exchange policy for the Poisson base walk. This connects the problem to contextual bandits and gives an information-theoretic sublinear-regret guarantee, but directly learning the exponentially many policies requires exponential time and space. We therefore introduce \emph{balanced fractional exchanges}, which compress the policy mixture into a single fractional base while retaining the exchange information needed by the Poisson analysis. This leads to an polynomial time algorithm with the same regret guarantee.

</details>


### [68] [Data Leakage Inflates Generalizability of Power Outage Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24665)
*Yamil Essus,Ranga Raju Vatsavai,Benjamin Rachunok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了美国东海岸（2018-2023）电力中断预测模型在不同测试策略下的泛化能力，发现随机划分测试的结果被空间和时间自相关严重高估，在空间/时间留出实验和事件级留出实验下模型性能大幅下降甚至不如简单基线，GeoAI基础模型嵌入的改进有限且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力中断预测模型越来越多地用于气候驱动的基础设施风险评估，但现有评估实践未能检验模型能否泛化到实际应用所涉及的新环境（如空间、时间、事件层面的新情况）。这种评估方法的缺失导致模型的真实部署价值不明。

Method: 使用美国东海岸2018-2023年公开数据，结合天气再分析、土地覆盖数据以及GeoAI基础模型（Prithvi WxC）的嵌入作为特征，在三种测试选择策略（非过滤随机划分、留一州法、留一事件法）下比较不同方法决策对预测性能的影响。

Result: 随机训练-测试划分产生强性能，但结果被空间和时间自相关所膨胀；在空间和时间留出实验中预测精度显著下降，模型常无法优于简单零基线；GeoAI基础模型嵌入仅在空间泛化上带来有限且不一致的改进，无法解决事件级迁移能力差的问题。

Conclusion: 基于当前数据可用性和评估实践，公开训练的停电预测模型提供的运营价值有限且不确定。未来进展需要改进数据覆盖范围、采用更现实的评估协议，并将重点从边际建模改进转向解决结构性数据约束。

Abstract: Power outage prediction models are increasingly used in assessments of climate-driven infrastructure risk, yet current evaluation practices obscure whether these models generalize to the novel conditions such applications require. We identify three common methodological choices in power outage prediction models that influence their ability to generalize across spatial, temporal, and event-based settings. We compare the predictive performance impacts of different methodological decisions using publicly available data for the U.S. East Coast from 2018 to 2023 and feature sets derived from weather reanalysis and land-cover data, and embeddings from a GeoAI foundation model (Prithvi WxC). Specifically, we assess model performance under multiple test selection strategies, including unfiltered random splits, leave-one-state-out, and leave-one-event-out designs, which increasingly approximate real-world deployment conditions. While random train-test splits yield strong performance, we show that these results are inflated by spatial and temporal autocorrelation. Under spatial and temporal holdout experiments, predictive accuracy degrades substantially, with models often failing to outperform a simple null baseline. Incorporating GeoAI foundation model embeddings yields limited and inconsistent improvements, primarily for spatial generalization, and does not resolve poor event-level transferability. These findings suggest that, given current data availability and evaluation practices, publicly trained outage prediction models offer limited and uncertain operational value. Progress will likely require improved data coverage, more realistic evaluation protocols, and a shift in focus from marginal modeling advances toward addressing structural data constraints.

</details>


### [69] [A Multimodal Foundation Model for Longitudinal Patient Representation and Scalable Insight Generation in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24688)
*Eugene Vorontsov,Yi Kan Wang,Alican Bozkurt,Adam Casson,Ludmila Tydlitatova,Michal Zelechowski,Ezra E. W. Cohen,Jyoti D. Patel,Max Banaszak,Caitlin McWilliams,Shane Colley,Kate Sasser,Ryan Fukushima,Eric Lefkofsky,Razik Yousfi,Siqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了oFM，一个基于167万癌症患者真实世界队列开发的基础模型，整合了临床轨迹与DNA、RNA和H&E病理等多模态数据，通过编码每日临床和分子事件并结合病理图像生成患者状态嵌入。在预后基准测试中，oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期的AUC方面显著优于基线特征（总生存期0.774 vs 0.563）；在11个比较治疗队列中，oFM嵌入的治疗获益AUTOC比基线特征高三倍，并在9/11个队列中改善了获益排序。该模型还支持基于证据的时间图机制发现框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要能够捕捉癌症演变和治疗过程的纵向患者状态模型，并整合多模态观测数据，以更好地支持治疗反应预测、预后评估和药物开发。

Method: 基于167万癌症患者的真实世界队列开发oFM基础模型，整合临床轨迹与DNA、RNA和H&E病理图像。使用患者级分区进行训练/验证/测试，超过100万患者用于训练。模型编码每日临床和分子事件，并结合病理图像随时间整合生成患者状态嵌入。同时引入机制发现框架，通过证据基础的时间图解释下游模型的预测结果。

Result: 在预后基准中，oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期方面均优于专家精选的临床和分子基线特征（总生存期AUC 0.774 vs 0.563）。在11个比较治疗队列中，oFM嵌入的汇总和尺度归一化治疗获益AUTOC比基线高三倍，9/11个队列获益排序改善，且在两个治疗臂内均提供更强的预后区分能力。

Conclusion: oFM作为纵向多模态肿瘤基础模型，在预后预测和治疗获益评估方面显著优于传统基线特征，并通过证据基础的时间图实现可解释的机制发现，在临床和药物开发应用中具有广泛潜力。

Abstract: Precision oncology necessitates a longitudinal model of patient state that captures cancer evolution and treatment over time, integrating multimodal observations. We introduce the oFM, a foundation model developed on a real-world oncology cohort of 1.67 million cancer patients that integrates clinical trajectories with DNA, RNA, and H&E pathology. Patient-level partitions were reserved for training, validation, and testing, with over one million patients used for training. The oFM encodes daily clinical and molecular episodes and, along with pathology images, integrates them over time to produce a patient state embedding. We evaluate frozen oFM embeddings against expert-curated clinical and molecular baseline features. In prognostic benchmarks, the oFM improved AUC for treatment response, progression-free survival, and overall survival (0.774 vs. 0.563 for overall survival). Across 11 comparative-treatment cohorts, the oFM embeddings achieved a three-fold higher pooled and scale-normalized treatment-benefit AUTOC than baseline features with improved benefit ranking in 9 of 11 cohorts, and provided stronger prognostic discrimination within both treatment arms. We also evaluated a mechanism discovery framework that interprets downstream models built on oFM embeddings by linking their predicted outcomes to clinically and biologically grounded mechanisms through an evidence-grounded temporal graph, enabling evaluation in clinical and drug-development applications.

</details>


### [70] [On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24696)
*Wenze Lin,Jiale Zhao,Xitai Jiang,Songde Rao,Yining Li,Shenzhi Wang,Bingxiang He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPDVR方法，通过基于轨迹正确性重构采样token OPD的隐式奖励，并应用ReLU门控机制，无缝融合RLVR和on-policy蒸馏，无需额外超参数，在六个推理基准上优于标准OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR存在稀疏的任务级反馈问题，而OPD提供密集的token级指导但忽略轨迹正确性且性能上限受限于教师模型。二者结合虽有前景，但现有集成方法依赖加权组合或启发式切换，引入了额外超参数和权衡。因此需要一个简洁有效、无需额外超参数的融合方案。

Method: 提出OPDVR方法：(1) 基于轨迹正确性重构采样token OPD的隐式奖励函数；(2) 应用ReLU门控机制，使正确轨迹获得非负奖励、错误轨迹获得非正奖励；(3) 该修改将采样token OPD转化为合法的RLVR方法，可与任意策略梯度算法（如GRPO）直接结合。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验结果表明，OPDVR在性能上稳定优于标准OPD方法。

Conclusion: OPDVR通过简洁的奖励重构与门控机制，在不引入额外超参数的前提下有效融合了密度蒸馏信号和任务级正确性反馈，为LLM后训练提供了更优的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two widely adopted paradigms for post-training large language models. However, RLVR suffers from sparse task-level feedback, while OPD provides dense token-level guidance but ignores trajectory correctness, limiting its performance to that of the teacher. Combining them is a promising direction: OPD supplies dense supervisory signals, while RLVR provides task-level correctness. Nevertheless, existing integrations often rely on weighted combination or heuristic switching, introducing extra hyperparameters and trade-offs. We propose On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward (OPDVR), a simple yet effective method that seamlessly combines OPD and RLVR without adding any hyperparameters. We first reformulate the implicit reward of sampled-token OPD based on trajectory correctness, then apply a ReLU gating mechanism to ensure that correct trajectories receive non-negative rewards and incorrect ones receive non-positive rewards---thereby aligning the distillation signal with task success while preserving the teacher's distributional guidance. Furthermore, our modification transforms sampled-token OPD into a proper RLVR method, making it readily combinable with any policy gradient algorithm, such as GRPO. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that OPDVR consistently outperforms standard OPD. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/OPDVR.

</details>


### [71] [Single State Update Predictive Coding training for Time Series Forecasting and Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24697)
*Matteo Cardoni,Sam Leroux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的预测编码网络（PCN）训练技术，通过将生成式PCN与支持编码PCN配对并并行训练来匹配神经激活，避免了传统PCN中顺序反向误差传播的瓶颈，并将其应用于时间序列异常检测，实现了更稳定、连续和在线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）是一种能够实现神经网络层并行更新的学习范式，但其主要瓶颈在于PCN需要顺序的反向误差传播。这一顺序传播过程限制了训练效率，阻碍了PCN在大规模或实时场景中的有效应用。

Method: 提出一种训练技术，将生成式PCN与一个支持性编码PCN配对。两个PCN并行训练以匹配彼此的神经激活，从而消除顺序反向传播的需求。该方法被应用于时间序列异常检测任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在时间序列异常检测中实现了更稳定、连续和在线式的学习效果，优于存在顺序传播瓶颈的传统PCN方法。

Conclusion: 通过并行化PCN的训练过程，消除了顺序反向误差传播的瓶颈，使得PCN能够更高效地应用于需要稳定、连续在线学习的场景，如时间序列异常检测。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is a neural learning paradigm that enables parallelizable neural network layer updates. However, the main bottleneck of PC Networks (PCN) is the sequential backwards error propagation. To tackle this, we introduce a training technique that pairs a Generative PCN with a support Encoding PCN. The two PCNs are trained in parallel to match their neural activations, without sequential propagation. We apply this to time series anomaly detection and show that our approach results in more stable, continuous, online learning.

</details>


### [72] [Parameter-Level Attribution of Symmetry in Trained Networks Though Parameter-Wise Functional Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24700)
*Alan Muriithi,Vedanta Thapar,Torben Berndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究当网络学习到具有已知对称性的函数时，该对称性能否穿过参数化被实现——即在参数空间中是否存在运动来实现函数空间中的群作用。作者将其形式化为实现映射的提升问题，证明了平滑参数空间作用存在的充要条件，并通过实验验证了该理论的局部有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多神经网络架构隐式或显式地学习了具有对称性的函数，但如何将这种函数空间中的对称性映射到参数空间的操作仍不清楚。理解参数空间中的对称性运动对于对称性保持训练、剪枝、插值等应用具有重要意义，因此需要严格的数学框架来刻画这一关系。

Method: 作者将对称性在参数空间中的实现形式化为提升问题：对于实现映射Φ:θ→f_θ，要求存在平滑的参数空间群作用使图上同。他们推导出必要条件是函数对称轨道的切空间包含于dΦ_θ的像（即参数的功能灵敏度张成的空间），该条件也是逐点一阶提升的充分条件。使用最小二乘松弛时，可得到两个局部参数方向：一个沿对称轨道运动，一个向等变子空间下降，残差衡量参数化无法达到的部分。

Result: 在旋转不变分类器上，这些方向引发了预测的函数空间运动，但仅局部有效：重新计算的方向能跟踪轨道并减少等变缺陷，而固定不变的方向在训练后会偏离轨道和等变子空间。在哈密顿神经网络（作用于旋转对称势，架构未显式强制对称）上也观察到相同现象。

Conclusion: 参数空间中对称性的实现本质上是局部的：只有沿着重新计算的局部方向才能持续跟踪函数空间的对称轨道，而固定的参数方向无法在训练后保持对称性运动。这为理解对称性在参数化网络中的可表示性提供了严格的微分几何框架。

Abstract: When a network has learned a function with a known symmetry, can that symmetry be moved through the parametrisation---is there a motion in parameter space realising the group action in function space? We formulate this as a lifting problem for the realisation map $Φ:θ\mapsto f_θ$, and show that a smooth parameter-space action exists only if the tangent space to the function's symmetry orbit lies within the image of $\mathrm dΦ_θ$, whose columns are the \emph{functional sensitivities} of individual parameters. This condition is also sufficient for pointwise first-order lifting. Relaxing it in least squares yields two local parameter directions: one following the symmetry orbit, one descending towards the equivariant subspace, with residuals measuring what the parametrisation cannot reach. On a rotationally invariant classifier we find these directions induce their predicted function-space motion, but only locally: recomputed directions track the orbit and reduce the equivariance defect, while directions held fixed depart from both after training. The same holds for Hamiltonian neural networks trained on a rotationally symmetric potential, even though the architecture does not explicitly enforce the symmetry.

</details>


### [73] [Constrained Hyperparameter Optimization for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24712)
*Bruno Veloso,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种边界约束处理策略，用于在线优化算法中管理超参数的边界约束问题，通过实证研究表明所提策略优于传统的"边界"策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数优化对模型性能至关重要，但现有研究多集中于批量学习场景。在数据流场景下，需要在线自调整超参数的能力。由于许多超参数受限于有界搜索空间，应用优化算子后会产生无效解，因此需要有效的边界约束处理技术来修正这些无效解。

Method: 本文针对在线优化算法中的边界约束问题，引入了四种边界约束处理策略。这些策略基于启发式和进化式优化方法，用于在在线学习阶段自调整超参数。研究通过在有界数值优化问题中管理边界约束，将超出边界阈值的超参数值重新对齐到相应的限制范围内。

Result: 通过在既有数据集上的实证研究，验证了所提出的四种边界策略在在线优化算法中的表现。结果表明，采用所提出的边界策略优于传统的"边界"（boundary）策略。

Conclusion: 本文论证了在约束数值优化问题中，相较于简单地将超参数值截断到边界限制的"边界"策略，所提出的四种边界约束处理策略能够更有效地管理边界约束，从而提升在线优化算法的性能。

Abstract: Optimization of hyperparameters is a critical factor to obtain optimal model performance. While existing research has predominantly concentrated on batch-learning scenarios, addressing the complexities inherent in data streams presents a challenge. The deployment of sophisticated methodologies to manage data streams becomes highly important. Consequently, the capacity for self-adjusting hyperparameters during on-line learning phases emerges as a goal. Many hyperparameters exhibit constraints and are confined within bounded search spaces, rendering specific solutions unacceptable upon applying optimization operators. To solve this issue, employing boundary constraint- handling techniques becomes imperative to rectify invalid solutions. This paper presents strategies for effectively managing boundary constraints within constrained numerical optimization problems. Recent methodologies, including heuristic and evolutionary-based optimization, employ a "boundary" strategy, wherein values that surpass boundary thresholds for a given hyperparameter are realigned to the respective limits. Our study introduces four strategies to navigate boundary constraints in online optimization algorithms. Through empirical investigations conducted on established datasets, we demonstrate that adopting boundary strategies outperforms the "boundary" strategy.

</details>


### [74] [Enhancing Bayesian Optimization and Active Learning Through Kernel Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24721)
*Heng Zhang,Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Konstantinos D. Polyzos,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KENDO统一框架，通过集成高斯过程（EGP）与分歧感知采集策略，替代超参数采样和昂贵的MCMC采样，同时应用于贝叶斯优化（BO）和贝叶斯主动学习（AL），在性能上具有竞争力或更优，且计算开销大幅降低（BO最高5倍加速，AL最高27倍加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化和贝叶斯主动学习中的超参数选择是核心挑战。模型误设会导致优化性能次优，而更精确的全贝叶斯处理通常依赖计算昂贵的MCMC采样。现有方法在精度与效率之间存在显著权衡，需要一个统一且高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出KENDO框架，核心思想是用核集成（kernel ensemble）和自适应贝叶斯加权替代超参数采样，并结合分歧感知（disagreement-aware）采集策略。在统一框架下实例化KENDO-BO（用于贝叶斯优化）和KENDO-AL（用于贝叶斯主动学习），二者共享自校正机制但具有任务特定的采集目标。此外，通过随机标量化（random scalarization）将方法扩展到多目标优化，保持单优化器条件结构。

Result: 在单目标优化、多目标优化和主动学习的合成及真实基准测试中：(i) KENDO-BO相比最先进方法具有竞争力或更优的优化性能，同时计算开销降低最多5倍；(ii) KENDO-AL在预测校准方面优于基于MCMC的主动学习基线，并实现最多27倍的加速。

Conclusion: KENDO框架证明了核集成与分歧感知策略可以统一解决BO和AL中的超参数难题，在保持或提升性能的同时显著降低计算成本，为贝叶斯优化和主动学习提供了高效且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection remains a key challenge in Bayesian optimization (BO) and Bayesian active learning (AL), as model misspecification can lead to suboptimal performance, while more accurate fully Bayesian treatments typically rely on computationally expensive MCMC sampling. This paper proposes a unified framework, KENDO (Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator), that integrates Ensemble Gaussian Processes (EGP) with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. The central idea is to replace hyperparameter sampling with a kernel ensemble and adaptive Bayesian weighting, combined with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. Within this unified framework, we instantiate KENDO-BO for BO and KENDO-AL for Bayesian AL, demonstrating that both arise from a common self-correcting mechanism with task-specific acquisition objectives. We further extend the approach to multi-objective optimization via random scalarization that preserves the single-optimizer conditioning structure. Thorough numerical tests on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across single-objective optimization, multi-objective optimization, and active learning demonstrate that (i) KENDO-BO achieves competitive or superior optimization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead by up to $5\times$ and (ii) KENDO-AL achieves superior predictive calibration over MCMC-based active learning baselines with up to $27\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [75] [Parameter-Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation for EEG-FM under Fixed Computational Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24727)
*Meghal Dani,Stefanie Liebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数高效的自我监督自适应方法，仅更新9%的参数即可将EEG基础模型适配到目标任务，在两个SOTA模型（BIOT和CBraMod）和三个临床EEG数据集上验证，相比线性探针最高提升20倍AUCPR，且只需20-50%的未标注数据即可达到峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床数据集上的泛化能力有限，而全参数微调对资源受限的临床场景计算开销过大，亟需高效的适配方案。

Method: 采用参数高效的自我监督适配方法，仅更新模型中9%的参数；在BIOT（对比学习）和CBraMod（掩码重建）两个具有不同预训练目标的基础模型上，于TUAB（异常检测）、TUEV（事件分类）和CHB-MIT（癫痫检测）三个临床EEG数据集上，在分布内和分布外条件下系统评估。

Result: SSL适配相比线性探针在AUCPR上最高提升20倍；在固定计算预算下，仅需20-50%的可用未标注数据即可达到峰值性能；在总窗口数固定的情况下，性能与患者数量无关，仅依赖整体时间窗口多样性。

Conclusion: 参数高效的自我监督自适应以极小的计算和数据收集开销即可实现EEG基础模型的有效部署，对资源受限的临床场景具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: EEG foundation models pretrained via self-supervised learning promise transferable representations, but their generalization remains limited, especially across diverse clinical datasets. Full fine-tuning is impractical for resource-constrained clinical settings due to high computational requirements. In this work, we investigate whether parameter-efficient self-supervised adaptation, updating only 9% of parameters suffices to align representations to target tasks. We evaluate our method on two state-of-the-art models with different pretraining objectives: BIOT (contrastive) and CBraMod (masked reconstruction), and evaluate on three clinical EEG datasets for abnormality detection (TUAB), event classification (TUEV), and seizure detection (CHB-MIT) under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. SSL adaptation yields consistent gains over linear probing, up to 20x AUCPR. Under a fixed compute budget, peak performance requires only 20--50% of available unlabeled data. Critically, when total window count is fixed, performance remains invariant to patient count, suggesting that performance is dependent on overall temporal window diversity only. Our findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation enables effective deployment of EEG Foundation models (EEG-FM) with minimal computational overhead and data collection burden. Code available at: https://github.com/c3n-group/efficient-eeg-adapt

</details>


### [76] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中的最小最大最优交替悔恨（alternating regret）问题。对于概率单纯形上的OLO，给出了O(log d)的常数交替悔恨算法及匹配下界；对于一般OCO，给出了O(d log(1+T/d))的算法并证明其匹配下界Ω(d log(1+T/d))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替悔恨是一种受博弈交替学习动态启发的悔恨度量，此前在OLO上仅有O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})的结果，在OCO上仅有Õ(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})的结果，均非最优。该问题对理解博弈中学习动态的收敛速度至关重要，尤其关系到Nash均衡和粗相关均衡（CCE）的收敛率。

Method: 对于OLO over simplex，提出了具有常数交替悔恨的算法（O(log d)），并给出匹配下界；对于一般OCO，提出了O(d log(1+T/d))交替悔恨的算法，并给出匹配的Ω(d log(1+T/d))下界。算法设计利用了交替悔恨特有的结构，通过构造不依赖于时间跨度T的悔恨上界来实现。

Result: OLO over simplex达到O(log d)常数交替悔恨（不依赖于T），显著改进此前O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})的结果；据此获得二人零和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到Nash均衡、二人一般和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到CCE的未耦合学习动态，这是首个O(1/T)收敛到CCE的未耦合学习动态。对于一般OCO，达到O(d log(1+T/d))交替悔恨，并证明Ω(d log(1+T/d))匹配下界，表明Ω(log T)因子不可消除。

Conclusion: 本文在交替悔恨问题上取得了完全最小最大最优的结果：OLO情形达到不依赖T的常数悔恨，OCO情形达到O(d log(1+T/d))的匹配上下界。这些结果带来了博弈学习动态中O(1/T)收敛到均衡的显著改进，并从根本上刻画了交替悔恨的最优复杂度。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [77] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为(DNN)²的方法，通过特征值最大化过程搜索非唯一对偶乘子空间，为ReLU神经网络验证中的双重非负规划（DNN）松弛提供全局最优性证书，从而在保持紧致界的同时实现可扩展的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LP和SDP松弛用于ReLU神经网络验证时因松弛间隙过大而过于保守；CPP公式虽可消除间隙但NP难求解；DNN作为其最便宜的可行松弛保留了关键约束，但规模超出内点法能力，且其非负性约束导致对偶乘子不唯一，使标准优化认证方法失效。

Method: 提出一种新颖的特征值最大化过程，在非唯一的对偶乘子空间中搜索有效证书（即全局最优性保证）。该方法在Burer-Monteiro分解框架下处理DNN公式，通过搜索对偶乘子空间来绕过标准认证方法的局限。

Result: 实验表明(DNN)²方法产生的界一致地紧于标准SDP方法，经常匹配精确解；且当有效证书存在时，认证过程能确认全局最优性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为在安全关键自主系统中部署神经网络控制器和感知模块，提供了紧致、可认证且计算可扩展的验证保证的关键一步。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Uniform Local Isometry and Topology: FactoMap for Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24762)
*Sohini Gupta,Bahareh Tolooshams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子空间结构（factor-space structure）概念，将因子域、生成器诱导的等价关系和位置相关尺度三者结合，以区分拓扑等价但几何不同的空间。核心贡献是因子空间拓扑图（FactoMap），通过因子空间格点索引学习可解释原型，将格点的周期性、坍缩和非均匀范围转移到表征中，从而实现保序和因子解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦方法将生成因子表示为欧几里得乘积坐标，但底层因子空间可能存在环绕、坍缩或位置相关几何。需要区分拓扑等价但几何不同的空间，且统计独立的因子（如色相和尺度）可能不可几何分离——其效应增长率不同，产生无法通过固定重缩放消除的各向异性。

Method: 提出Factor-Space Topographic Map（FactoMap），学习由因子空间格点索引的可解释原型，通过拓扑学习将格点的周期性、坍缩和非均匀范围转移至表征空间，以匹配底层因子空间的真实结构。

Result: 实验表明，匹配因子空间的结构能够保持因子连续性并实现底层因子的解耦，验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 因子空间的几何结构（而非仅拓扑结构）对解耦至关重要，通过捕获周期性、坍缩和各向异性等结构特性，可以显著提升表征学习中的因子解耦性能。

Abstract: Many disentanglement methods represent generative factors using Euclidean product coordinates, although the underlying factor spaces may wrap, collapse, or have position-dependent geometry. We introduce factor-space structure, combining factor domains, generator-induced identifications, and position-dependent scales to distinguish topologically equivalent spaces with different factor geometries. We show that statistically independent factors need not be geometrically separable: hue and scale produce effects that grow at different rates, yielding anisotropy that no fixed rescaling removes. We propose the Factor-Space Topographic Map (FactoMap), which learns interpretable prototypes indexed by a factor-space lattice. Topographic learning transfers the lattice's periodicity, collapses, and non-uniform extent to the representation. Experiments show that matching this structure preserves factor continuity and enables disentanglement of the underlying factors.

</details>


### [79] [LION: A Clifford Neural Paradigm for Multimodal-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24795)
*Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Zhengyu Wu,Henan Sun,Daohan Su,Guang Zeng,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LION框架，基于Clifford代数和解耦图神经网络范式（先传播后聚合），解决多模态属性图中模态对齐忽视上下文和模态融合缺乏自适应的两大问题。LION通过Clifford代数构建模态感知几何流形实现高质量模态对齐，并设计自适应全息聚合模块增强模态融合，在9个文图多模态图数据集、3个图任务和3个模态任务上显著超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图学习正从文本属性图向多模态属性图演进，但现有神经范式存在两大局限：一是模态对齐阶段，大多数方法采用拓扑约束或模态特定算子作为分词器，忽视图上下文并抑制模态交互，导致对齐次优；二是模态融合阶段，多数方法仅简单适配双模态图，未能充分利用带拓扑先验的对齐标记，导致泛化能力差和性能退化。

Method: LION基于Clifford代数和解耦图神经范式（传播-聚合），实现多模态属性图中的对齐-融合流程。具体包括两个核心模块：(1) 基于Clifford代数构建模态感知几何流形，通过几何诱导的高阶图传播高效实现模态交互，促进模态对齐；(2) 基于对齐标记的拓扑感知Clifford分量，提出自适应全息聚合模块，结合分量能量和传播尺度信息与可学习参数，提升模态融合效果。

Result: 在9个文图（text-image）多模态属性图数据集上的大量实验表明，LION在3个图下游任务和3个模态下游任务上均显著优于现有最优（SOTA）基线方法。

Conclusion: LION通过Clifford代数驱动的几何传播与自适应全息聚合，有效解决了多模态属性图中模态对齐忽视上下文和模态融合缺乏自适应的问题，展现出在多模态图学习领域的优越性能和广泛适用性，推动了数据驱动的图机器学习范式的发展。

Abstract: Recently, the rapid advancement of multimodal domains has driven a data-centric paradigm shift in graph ML, transitioning from text-attributed to multimodal-attributed graphs. This advancement significantly enhances data representation and expands the scope of graph downstream tasks, such as modality-oriented tasks, thereby improving the practical utility of graph ML. Despite its promise, limitations exist in the current neural paradigms:(1) Neglect Context in Modality Alignment: Most existing methods adopt topology-constrained or modality-specific operators as tokenizers.These aligners inevitably neglect graph context and inhibit modality interaction, resulting in suboptimal alignment.(2) Lack of Adaptation in Modality Fusion: Most existing methods are simple adaptations for 2-modality graphs and fail to adequately exploit aligned tokens equipped with topology priors during fusion, leading to poor generalizability and performance degradation.To address the above issues, we propose LION (c\underline{LI}ff\underline{O}rd \underline{N}eural paradigm) based on the Clifford algebra and decoupled graph neural paradigm (i.e., propagation-then-aggregation) to implement alignment-then-fusion in multimodal-attributed graphs. Specifically, we first construct a modality-aware geometric manifold grounded in Clifford algebra.This geometric-induced high-order graph propagation efficiently achieves modality interaction, facilitating modality alignment.Then, based on the topology-aware Clifford components of aligned tokens, we propose adaptive holographic aggregation. This module integrates component-wise energy and propagation-scale information with learnable parameters to improve modality fusion. Extensive experiments on 9 text-image MAG datasets demonstrate that LION significantly outperforms SOTA baselines across 3 graph and 3 modality downstream tasks.

</details>


### [80] [MDTE: Minority-Aware Diffusion over Temporal Edge Events for Imbalanced Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24812)
*Zhou Zelong,Zhang Tianming,Yang Zhengyi,Tang Yifu,Hou Chenyu,Cao Bin,Fan Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDTE，一种面向时序图类别不平衡节点分类的少数类感知扩散框架。该框架通过条件扩散去噪重建稳定且有判别力的时序边事件表示，并结合分布感知选择性传播与多视角判别融合模块，在五个真实数据集上显著提升了少数类相关指标（少数类召回率最高提升23.53个百分点，F1提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC提升2.67个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图上的类别不平衡节点分类面临两个核心挑战：(1) 多数类主导的时序传播会逐步同化少数类表示，导致少数类信息被淹没；(2) 传统的节点和邻域信息对少数类缺乏足够的判别性证据，难以有效区分少数类样本。这两个问题使得现有方法在少数类分类上表现不佳。

Method: MDTE包含两个关键模块：(1) Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation（分布感知选择性传播）：结合基于局部离群因子（LOF）的传播过滤与簇感知低频传播，在保留信息性邻域依赖的同时缓解有害传播和多数类信息同化；(2) Multi-View Discriminative Fusion（多视角判别融合）：利用特征重建和拓扑预测来刻画类别间的分布学习差异，提取互补的判别性信号以指导扩散去噪过程。整体框架通过条件扩散去噪重建稳定的时序边事件表示。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，MDTE在少数类导向的指标上一致取得最佳性能：相比最强基线，少数类召回率最高提升23.53个百分点，少数类F1提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC提升2.67个百分点。

Conclusion: MDTE通过少数类感知的传播过滤与多视角判别融合机制，有效解决了时序图中少数类表示被多数类同化以及判别信息不足的问题，为类别不平衡时序图分类提供了一种可行的框架方案。

Abstract: Class-imbalanced node classification on temporal graphs is challenging because majority-dominated temporal propagation progressively assimilates minority representations, while conventional node and neighborhood information provides insufficient discriminative evidence for minority classes. To address these issues, we propose MDTE, a minority-aware diffusion framework that reconstructs stable and discriminative temporal edge-event representations through conditional diffusion denoising. Specifically, MDTE introduces Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation, which combines Local Outlier Factor (LOF)-based propagation filtering with cluster-aware low-frequency propagation. The module preserves informative neighborhood dependencies while mitigating harmful propagation and majority-class information assimilation. It further develops Multi-View Discriminative Fusion, which exploits feature reconstruction and topology prediction to characterize class-wise differences in distribution learning and extracts complementary discriminability signals to guide denoising. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MDTE consistently achieves the best performance on minority-class-oriented metrics, improving minority-class recall by up to 23.53 percentage points, minority-class F1 by 8.68 percentage points, and AUPRC by 2.67 percentage points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Effective Learning Rate Governs Loss Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24814)
*Zihan Liu,Ruiheng Zheng,Shaobo Zhang,Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型预训练中的"ELR collapse"现象：学习率(LR)与参数范数主要通过它们的比值——有效学习率(ELR)——共同决定损失动态。当ELR在不同运行间匹配时，尽管LR和参数范数差异显著，损失轨迹在整个训练过程中仍会塌缩重合。跨优化器、架构、数据集和模型规模，平均塌缩误差通常仅为10^-3量级，低于代表性配置中种子间差异。系统消融实验识别出归一化设计和LR-范数变化的时间尺度是塌缩精度的关键决定因素。受控干预表明权重衰减和Hyperball主要通过其诱导的ELR调度塑造损失动态。用ELR替代LR后，拟合的功能缩放律(FSL)可跨范数控制方法迁移，且ELR-based FSL还能解释"延迟加速"这一范数控制的反复出现的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型预训练中，学习率和参数范数对损失动态的影响机制尚不明确。传统观点将LR和范数视为独立的超参数，但它们在训练中的交互作用缺乏统一的理论框架。作者旨在寻找一个能统一描述LR调度和范数控制效应的共同坐标，以更准确理解和预测损失轨迹。

Method: 通过大规模对比实验，匹配不同运行间的ELR（LR/参数范数比值）以观察损失轨迹是否塌缩；进行系统消融以隔离归一化设计和LR-范数变化时间尺度的作用；通过受控干预（如权重衰减、Hyperball）验证其对ELR调度的影响；最后用ELR替换LR来拟合功能缩放律(FSL)并测试其跨范数控制方法的迁移能力。

Result: 证实ELR是损失动态的主导坐标：(1) ELR匹配时损失轨迹跨运行塌缩，误差仅10^-3量级；(2) 归一化设计和时间尺度决定塌缩精度；(3) 权重衰减和Hyperball通过改变ELR调度间接影响损失；(4) ELR-based FSL可跨范数控制方法迁移，并解释延迟加速现象。

Conclusion: ELR是连接LR调度、范数控制和损失动态的统一坐标，为预测和调控预训练损失提供了新的理论基础。该发现有助于优化超参数设计，减少预训练中试错成本。

Abstract: We uncover ELR collapse in language model pretraining: learning rate (LR) and parameter norm govern loss dynamics primarily through their ratio, the effective learning rate (ELR). When ELR is matched across runs, their loss trajectories collapse throughout training despite substantially different LRs and parameter norms. Across optimizers, architectures, datasets, and model scales, mean collapse errors are typically a few x 10^-3, below the seed-to-seed variation measured in a representative configuration. Systematic ablations identify normalization design and the timescale of LR-norm variation as key determinants of collapse precision. Controlled interventions further show that weight decay and Hyperball shape loss dynamics primarily through the ELR schedules they induce. Replacing LR with ELR enables a fitted functional scaling law (FSL) to transfer across norm-control methods. The resulting ELR-based FSL also explains delayed acceleration, a recurring effect of norm control. Together, these results establish ELR as a common coordinate linking LR scheduling, norm control, and loss dynamics.

</details>


### [82] [A Geometric Theory of Robust Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24818)
*Binita Maity*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架来分析基于邻域的公平性审计在有限扰动下的鲁棒性，建立了邻域不变性的充分条件，量化了邻域替换对审计不稳定性的传播，并引入了"审计波动性"这一度量来刻画重复扰动下公平性审计的期望敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于邻域的公平性审计被广泛使用，但审计过程本身的鲁棒性鲜有研究。由于此类审计依赖最近邻关系，特征空间的微小扰动可以改变局部邻域，即使在模型预测不变的情况下也可能导致不同的公平性评估结果。因此需要系统性地分析该审计方法的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者开发了一个几何框架来在有限扰动条件下分析基于邻域的公平性审计的鲁棒性。具体包括：(1) 建立邻域不变性的充分条件；(2) 量化邻域替换如何传播导致审计不稳定；(3) 引入"审计波动性"作为重复扰动下公平性审计期望敏感度的度量。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验支持了理论分析，表明所提出的框架能够解释基于邻域的公平性审计在实际中观察到的稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地分析了基于邻域的公平性审计的鲁棒性，提供了理论保证和实用度量，表明此类审计在有限扰动下具有一定程度的可解释稳定性，为审计方法的可靠性评估提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Neighborhood-based fairness audits evaluate individual fairness by comparing predictions among similar individuals in feature space. Despite their widespread use, little is known about the robustness of the auditing procedure itself. Because these audits rely on nearest neighbor relationships, small perturbations in feature space can alter local neighborhoods and produce different fairness assessments even when model predictions remain unchanged. We develop a geometric framework for analyzing the robustness of neighborhood-based fairness audits under bounded perturbations. Our analysis establishes sufficient conditions for neighborhood invariance, quantifies how neighborhood replacement propagates to audit instability, and introduces audit volatility, a measure of the expected sensitivity of fairness audits under repeated perturbations. Experiments on benchmark datasets support the theoretical analysis and show that the proposed framework explains the observed stability of neighborhood-based fairness audits.

</details>


### [83] [BioKERN: Biological Kernel Regularization for Histology-to-Transcriptomics Neighborhood Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24823)
*Seungik Cho,Betul Orcan-Ekmekci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioKERN是一个多模态空间表征学习框架，通过构建由转录组相似性和空间邻近性组成的生物学核作为可学习的归纳偏置，提供梯度化的邻域监督和嵌入几何正则化，在Mouse Brain Visium和Human Liver GSE240429数据集上一致优于BLEEP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组织学-转录组学的多模态学习方法过度强调实例级匹配，忽略了非配对位点之间共享的分子或空间上下文；需要显式保留生物学邻域结构而非仅依赖精确的跨模态对应关系。

Method: BioKERN在训练时构建一个由转录组相似性和空间邻近性组合而成的生物学核，利用该核提供梯度化的邻域监督信号，并用于正则化嵌入几何结构；评估使用与模型无关的固定生物学邻域定义。

Result: 在Mouse Brain Visium和Human Liver GSE240429两个数据集上，BioKERN在单尺度和多尺度设置下均一致地提升了生物学邻域检索性能，优于BLEEP；受控共享架构实验表明大部分性能提升来源于生物学核正则化而非模型容量增加。

Conclusion: 显式的生物学几何结构可以作为多模态学习中可解释的归纳偏置，有效提升空间生物学中的表征质量。

Abstract: Spatially resolved biology requires representations that preserve biological neighborhood structure rather than only exact cross-modal correspondences. Existing histology--transcriptomics objectives can emphasize instance-level matching even when non-paired spots share molecular or spatial context. We introduce BioKERN, a multimodal spatial representation-learning framework that incorporates biological structure as an explicit, learnable inductive bias. BioKERN constructs a training-time biological kernel by combining transcriptomic similarity and spatial proximity, then uses it to provide graded neighborhood supervision and regularize embedding geometry. Evaluation uses a fixed, model-independent biological neighborhood definition shared by all methods. Across Mouse Brain Visium and Human Liver GSE240429, BioKERN consistently improves biological-neighborhood retrieval over BLEEP in both single- and multi-scale settings. Controlled shared-architecture experiments show that most of the improvement arises from biological-kernel regularization rather than increased model capacity. These results support explicit biological geometry as an interpretable inductive bias for multimodal learning in spatial biology.

</details>


### [84] [Bellman Calibration for Marginalized Importance Weighting in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24858)
*Lars van der Laan,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等距Bellman校准（Isotonic Bellman Calibration），一种一维、模型无关的后处理方法，通过非递减变换修正初始占用率估计的尺度和形状，减少占用平衡违背（occupancy-balance violations），并给出有限样本校准保证和KL oracle不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边际重要性加权估计器（如极小极大、原始对偶、拟合不动点方法）由于函数类近似、正则化或优化不充分，会留下残差的占用平衡违背。这些违背缺乏直接的监督验证损失，难以用于超参数调优、模型选择和早停，使得诊断和减少这些违背变得困难。

Method: 将等距Bellman校准表述为在非递减变换的一维函数类上应用拟合占用率评估（FORE）。该方法保持初始占用率估计的排序信息，仅修正其尺度与形状。理论上将其刻画为一种条件不动点性质，等价于对校准后比率的每个测试函数满足占用平衡。

Result: 推导了校准-细化界（calibration-refinement bound），证明校准误差小的拟合比率与基于其拟合值的最佳后处理性能接近。对等距Bellman校准，建立了有限样本校准保证和相对初始估计最佳单调变换的KL oracle不等式，证明其在下游目标占用泛函（包括策略值估计）上达到统计误差内的最佳单调修正性能。

Conclusion: 等距Bellman校准是一种简单、通用且理论上有保证的后处理工具，能够在不改变排序信息的前提下系统性减少占用平衡违背，从而提升下游策略评估的可靠性，为边际重要性加权中缺乏验证损失的问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Marginalized importance weighting evaluates a target policy by reweighting offline state-action samples with its discounted occupancy ratio, characterized by an adjoint Bellman equation. Existing minimax, primal-dual, and fitted fixed-point estimators can leave residual occupancy-balance violations because of function-class approximation, regularization, or incomplete optimization. These violations are difficult to diagnose and reduce because the objectives generally lack a direct supervised validation loss for hyperparameter tuning, model selection, and early stopping. We introduce isotonic Bellman calibration, a one-dimensional, model-agnostic post-processing method that reduces these violations while preserving the ranking information in any initial occupancy-ratio estimate. The method corrects the estimate's scale and shape by applying fitted occupancy-ratio evaluation (FORE) over a one-dimensional class of nondecreasing transformations. We characterize Bellman calibration as a conditional fixed-point property equivalent to occupancy-balance against every test function of the calibrated ratio. More generally, we derive a calibration-refinement bound showing that any fitted ratio with small calibration error performs nearly as well as the best post-processing based on its fitted values. For isotonic Bellman calibration, we establish finite-sample calibration guarantees and a KL oracle inequality relative to the best monotone transformation of the initial estimate. Consequently, isotonic Bellman calibration achieves small calibration error and KL risk within statistical error of the best monotone correction, with guarantees for downstream target-occupancy functionals, including policy-value estimation.

</details>


### [85] [Improving Cross-Problem Vehicle Routing with Locally Augmented Preferences and Representation Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24859)
*Arthur Corrêa,Paulo Nascimento,Samuel Moniz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对多任务车辆路径规划（VRP）求解器的模型无关改进方法：POLAR训练算法（利用局部搜索精化形成更有信息量的偏好对）和PLE编码器（通过门控机制在共享专家与任务特定专家间路由，渐进分离共性路由结构与约束特定编码），在16个分布内变体上将平均差距相对最强基准降低21.3%，并在32个未见变体中的27个上超越先前的神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务VRP求解器旨在用单一统一模型处理多种VRP变体，但现有方法存在两方面局限：训练方面，强化学习受奖励尺度差异和优势信号衰减困扰，偏好优化则因采样路线趋同而受限于策略自身生成解的质量；架构方面，全共享编码器纠缠了异质变体间约束依赖的表示，限制了泛化能力。

Method: 提出两个模型无关贡献：（1）POLAR（Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement）训练算法，对最优解码路线施加局部搜索精化后再形成偏好对，产生更具信息量的成对边际；（2）PLE（Progressive Layered Extraction）编码器，通过门控机制将每层编码器路由至一个共享专家和一组任务特定专家，渐进分离共性路由结构与约束特定编码。

Result: 在多种VRP变体上的大量实验表明，POLAR和PLE的组合提升了当前神经多任务求解器的state-of-the-art水平：在16个分布内变体上相对最强已发表基准将平均差距降低21.3%，在32个未见变体的27个上超越先前神经方法。消融实验验证了两个贡献各自的有效性，且在不同骨干模型架构上均能提升跨问题泛化能力。

Conclusion: POLAR训练算法通过局部搜索精化解决了训练后期监督信号弱化的问题，PLE编码器通过渐进分层提取解决了表示纠缠问题，两者组合显著提升了多任务VRP求解器的性能与泛化能力，且方法具有模型无关性，可应用于不同骨干架构。

Abstract: Multi-task vehicle routing problem (VRP) solvers seek to handle multiple VRP variants within a single unified model, avoiding the need to train a separate model for every variant. In spite of recent progress, current approaches remain limited on two fronts. On the training side, reinforcement learning suffers from reward-scale disparities and shrinking advantage signals as policies improve, whereas preference optimization stagnates once sampled tours become near-identical and thus fundamentally limited by the quality of the policy's own generated solutions, leaving both paradigms with weak supervision as training progresses. On the architecture side, existing fully shared encoders entangle constraint-dependent representations across heterogeneous variants, which limits generalization. We address these gaps with two model-agnostic contributions. First, we propose Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement (POLAR), a novel training algorithm that applies a local search refinement pass to the best decoded tour before forming preference pairs, yielding much more informative pairwise margins. Second, a Progressive Layered Extraction (PLE) encoder routes each encoder layer through one shared expert and a set of task-specific experts via a gating mechanism, progressively separating common routing structure from constraint-specific encodings. Through extensive experiments on various VRP variants, we show that POLAR and PLE together elevate the current state-of-the-art among neural multi-task solvers. We reduce the average gap to reference solutions by 21.3% relative to the strongest published baseline on 16 in-distribution variants, and outperform prior neural methods on 27 out of 32 unseen variants. Ablation studies confirm the efficacy of each contribution, showing that both improve cross-problem generalization across multiple backbone model architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Decomposing Browser Pipeline Architectures for DOM-Sourced Particle Effects: Worker Offload, WebGL, and WebAssembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23609)
*Hossein Asadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对分层浏览器架构中局部优化不等于端到端优化的问题，以DOM粒子管线为具体负载，对五种粒子管线（P1-P5）及CSS基线（P0）进行受控架构分解，隔离线程放置、渲染器选择和仿真后端三个层次。通过可复现测试框架测量交互节奏、高负载端到端压力及仿真微基准，并在多浏览器/GPU配置上重复验证。四个主要发现：（1）Worker卸载改善交互FPS（约144 vs 52 FPS）；（2）AssemblyScript加速Chrome更新内核约1.5-1.6x；（3）WebAssembly仿真优势在~25万粒子下不必然转化为产品FPS提升；（4）渲染器排名和绝对性能随配置变化。核心贡献是瓶颈感知的架构测量方法：识别主导层并检验层间优化是否传导至用户可见FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队常将Web Workers、WebGL和WebAssembly视为可互换的"加速手段"，但每种技术针对不同的架构层次。局部优化（如单层性能提升）不必然转化为端到端的用户体验改善，需要系统化方法区分各层瓶颈并评估层间优化传导效果。

Method: 采用受控架构分解方法：以DOM粒子管线为具体负载，设计五种粒子管线（P1-P5）及一个CSS层基线（P0），分别隔离线程放置（Worker vs主线程）、渲染器选择（WebGL vs CSS）和仿真后端（AssemblyScript/WASM vs原生JS）。测量三个维度：交互节奏、高负载端到端压力、仿真专属微基准。在不同浏览器/GPU主机配置（Firefox 153+Intel UHD、Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK、Google Colab Tesla T4）上进行跨浏览器/双GPU类复制实验（n=5/10），并使用Cliff's delta等统计量评估效应量。

Result: 四项关键结果：（1）Worker卸载在Chrome上显著改善交互节奏（约144 vs 52 FPS，Cliff's delta=1）；（2）AssemblyScript在Chrome上加速更新内核约1.5-1.6x，在Colab T4上仿真专属约1.85x；（3）在约25万WebGL粒子下，Chrome仿真端WASM的优势未提升产品FPS（P5≤P4），且CPU定时器显示仿真仍主导预算，排除"绘制主导CPU"的简单解释；（4）渲染器排名和绝对优劣势随浏览器/GPU/主机配置而显著变化。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出瓶颈感知的架构测量方法：先识别当前的主导瓶颈层，再验证针对该层的优化是否能传导至用户可见的FPS。局部层优化（如WASM仿真加速）虽然真实存在，但在高负载场景下不必然改善端到端体验，且性能排名高度依赖具体配置，强调跨环境验证和分层测量的必要性。

Abstract: Local optimization does not necessarily yield end-to-end optimization in layered browser architectures. Teams often treat Web Workers, WebGL, and WebAssembly as interchangeable ways to "make it faster," yet each lever targets a different layer. We present a controlled architectural decomposition--using DOM-sourced particle pipelines as a concrete workload--of five particle pipelines (P1-P5), with an additional non-particle CSS-layer baseline (P0), that isolates thread placement, renderer choice, and simulation backend. Using a reproducible harness we measure interactive pacing, high-load end-to-end stress, and a simulation-only microbenchmark, plus same-host cross-browser / dual-GPU-class replication (Firefox 153+Intel UHD; Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK) and an independent second-host slice on Google Colab (Chrome 150, Tesla T4, n=5). Four results stand out. (1) Worker offload improves interactive pacing on paper-primary Chrome (approx. 144 vs approx. 52 FPS; Cliff's delta=1, n=10). (2) AssemblyScript speeds the Chrome update kernel by about 1.5-1.6x on the primary host and approx. 1.85x on Colab T4 (sim-only). (3) Under approx. 250k WebGL particles the Chrome sim-only WASM win need not raise product FPS (P5<=P4 on primary/Firefox; P5 approx. P4 on Colab T4); in-worker CPU phase timers show simulate still dominates the accounted budget, so the non-translation is not a simple "draw dominates CPU" story. (4) Renderer ranking and absolute margins vary across browser/GPU/host configurations. The contribution is bottleneck-aware architectural measurement: identify the dominant layer and test whether a layer win propagates to user-visible FPS.

</details>


### [87] [From Traceability to Justifiability: Accountability Structures in Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23610)
*Rashid Azarang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文评估AI系统部署管线中"评价对象即部署对象"这一声明的可记录性与可验证性：通过47个交付平台的文档调查和30个开源仓库的实证分析，发现平台默认记录缺乏行为元组的内容寻址身份标识，且采用证明工具链的仓库中近半数仅发布源码版本，导致声明的绑定无法在其声明处被核查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统部署的安全主张依赖于"被评估的就是被部署的"这一核心声明，但缺乏系统性的方法从公开材料中衡量该声明能否被记录、以及记录后是否真实成立。现有研究未对交付平台的默认身份记录能力和开源管线的实际可实现保证深度进行量化检验。

Method: 采用两阶段实证方法：(1) 对47个交付平台（20个CI/CD、27个模型服务/代理平台）进行两类文档调查，使用固定三标签协议，每页经双人评分（第二遍盲评），全部页面以内容哈希和日期固定；(2) 构建一个计算工具，从事先冻结的两层30个公开仓库（来自哈希归档，双人盲评）的发布产物中计算"已实现保证深度"，并与声明的保证深度进行比较。

Result: 在188个双评单元格中，没有任何平台的默认记录能发出行为元组（模型版本、指令、工具定义、检索与运行时配置）的内容寻址身份；盲评中该列默认得分为47个平台中的0个。不可变名义版本控制正成为代理平台的默认答案（27个中的16个），但该层已被证明不足。在仓库层面，7/15采用证明工具链的仓库发布仅源码版本，导致声明的绑定无法在其声明处核查；5/7可测量的采用者实现了端到端的绑定，两个不足均落在身份绑定环节。

Conclusion: 该领域记录在结构上不足以支撑"可证明性"（justifiability）——这是能拒绝一次部署转换的关键环节。调查带有过期时钟，论文明确说明了能使每项发现被证伪的条件。

Abstract: A pipeline promoting an AI system publishes records claiming the thing evaluated is the thing deployed and that the evidence licensed the transition. We measure, from public material only, whether those records can express that claim and whether it holds where declared. First, a two-class documentation survey of 47 delivery platforms (20 CI/CD, 27 model-serving/agent) under one fixed three-label protocol, graded twice (second pass blind), every consulted page pinned by content hash and date. Across 188 double-graded cells we found no platform whose default record emits a content-addressed identity of the behavioral tuple (model version, instructions, tool definitions, retrieval and runtime configuration); the blind pass grades that column default on zero of 47. Immutable nominal versioning is meanwhile arriving as the agent platforms' default answer (16 of 27): version integers behind mutable pointers, a layer the artifact supply chain already found insufficient. Second, an instrument computes realized assurance depth from a pipeline's published exhaust alone and compares it with the declared depth. Applied to a frozen two-stratum frame of 30 public repositories graded twice from a hashed archive (second pass blind; cell-level agreement 23 and 19 of 30, both passes independently finding the same five full realizations), the sharpest result is a verifiability hole: seven of the 15 repositories chosen for adopting attestation tooling publish source-only releases, so the binding their workflows declare cannot be checked where declared. Where checkable it mostly checks out: five of seven measurable adopters realize the binding end to end; both shortfalls fall at identity binding. Together the results locate the field's records structurally short of justifiability, the one rung that can refuse a transition. The survey carries an expiry clock; we state what would falsify each finding.

</details>


### [88] [REFINE: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach for Evidence-Guided Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23611)
*Muhammad Waseem,Aakash Ahmad,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出REFINE，一种工具无关、证据感知的多智能体方法，用于生成Java文件级别的重构候选。该方法结合静态分析引导的代码坏味道识别、规划、LLM转换、自动再分析、保留性检查和结构化报告。在450个Java文件、15个开源系统上使用三个LLM评估，坏味道减少68-73%，优于直接提示基线，但输出仍需编译、测试、依赖分析和人工审查后才能用于系统级设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM为自动化代码重构提供了新机会，但生成的重构变更必须在不引入新问题或改变行为相关代码结构的前提下减少目标质量问题。现有方法缺乏证据感知的多智能体协作来确保重构的安全性和有效性。

Method: REFINE采用多智能体流水线：(1)静态分析引导的坏味道识别，(2)坏味道信息驱动的重构规划，(3)LLM驱动的代码转换，(4)自动化再分析，(5)保留性检查（如assert/fail调用变更、公共方法移除检测），(6)结构化报告。使用三个LLM（GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Opus 4.8）进行实验。

Result: 在450个Java文件、15个开源系统上，REFINE在三组配置中分别减少68.26%、72.79%和68.49%的代码坏味道，主要坏味道减少最为显著。与150个文件的直接提示基线相比，REFINE实现更高的中位坏味道减少，同时编辑更小、公共方法移除更少。但更广泛的质量改进不一致，保留性检查揭示残余风险（assert/fail调用改变和公共方法移除）。

Conclusion: REFINE是有效的LLM驱动重构方法，显著优于直接提示基线，但当前输出应视为重构候选，需经过编译、测试、依赖分析和人工审查后才能应用于仓库级或系统级设置。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for automated code refactoring. However, generated changes must reduce targeted quality problems without introducing new issues or altering behaviour-relevant code structures. We introduce REFINE (Refactoring with Evidence-aware Flow for Integrated ageNtic Execution), a tool-agnostic, evidence-aware multi-agent approach for generating Java file-level refactoring candidates. REFINE combines static-analysis-guided smell identification, smell-informed planning, LLM-based transformation, automated re-analysis, preservation checks, and structured reporting.
  We evaluate REFINE on 450 Java files from 15 open-source systems, producing 1,350 model-pass outputs using OpenAI GPT-5.5, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.8. REFINE reduces detected code smells by 68.26%, 72.79%, and 68.49% across the three configurations, respectively, with the strongest reductions observed for major smells. A matched 150-file direct-prompt baseline shows that REFINE achieves a higher median code-smell reduction with smaller edits and fewer public-method removals. However, broader quality improvements are inconsistent, and preservation checks reveal residual risks, including assert/fail-call changes and public-method removal. Therefore, REFINE outputs should be treated as refactoring candidates requiring compilation, testing, dependency analysis, and human review before adoption in repository- or system-level settings.

</details>


### [89] [SDR Driver for Precise Timing Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23614)
*Fabrizio Pollastri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文开发了使低成本HackRF One软件无线电（SDR）能够实现精密定时应用的软件驱动程序。通过扩展原始固件实现同步采样、时间尺度控制和时钟频率调整等定时功能，并采用AI辅助开发工作流从现有代码库生成SoapySDR兼容驱动程序，将开发工作量降低了一个数量级以上，同时提高了软件可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本SDR设备（如HackRF One）缺乏精密定时能力，难以满足需要精确时间同步的定时应用需求。传统开发新的SDR驱动需要大量人工调试和编写工作，开发成本高且效率低。

Method: 首先扩展HackRF One原始固件，加入同步采样、时间尺度控制和时钟频率调整等定时功能；初始采用Python包装器实现，随后重新设计为SoapySDR兼容驱动程序；利用AI辅助开发工作流，从现有HackRF One和SoapySDR代码库自动生成新驱动程序，仅需少量调试即可运行。

Result: 采用AI辅助方式生成的SoapySDR兼容驱动在有限调试后即可投入使用，开发工作量大减超过一个数量级，同时软件可维护性得到显著改善。

Conclusion: 研究表明，AI辅助开发工作流可有效应用于SDR驱动开发领域，能够在保证或提升代码质量（可维护性）的同时大幅降低开发成本，为低成本SDR设备扩展精密定时能力提供了一条高效路径。

Abstract: This paper describes the development of software drivers that enable precise timing applications with the low-cost HackRF One software-defined radio (SDR). The original firmware was extended with timing functions for synchronized sampling, time-scale control, and clock-frequency adjustment. An initial implementation based on a Python wrapper was later redesigned as a SoapySDR-compatible driver. Using an AI-assisted development workflow, the new driver was generated from the existing HackRF One and SoapySDR codebases and became operational after only limited debugging. The approach reduced development effort by more than an order of magnitude while improving software maintainability.

</details>


### [90] [Rebuild Dossier: Mechanically-Enforced Specs for Agentic App Rebuilds, and What Model-Tier Failures Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23616)
*Parker Fawcett*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了rebuild-dossier，一个开源工具，通过在编写任何代码之前锁定应用程序的真实接口（输入和输出），并强制一次一个测试的构建方式（通过自动化检查而非仅靠书面指令），来改进AI智能体重建应用的过程。评估发现：测试套件通过并不能证明正确性（因为测试可能被游戏化）；该工具在与基础方法对比时在较大应用上失败（因为自动化检查未运行，表明检查机制本身而非接口锁定是关键）；所有声明都需要通过三层验证（智能体报告、自动化日志、实际文件）才能发现真实错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现，一旦模型足够强大，多智能体重建管线会输给最简单的方法——给模型原始代码和一条指令（AgentModernize）。作者认为，AI智能体的重建质量取决于其生产过程，因此需要一种方法来锁定应用的真实接口并在构建过程中实施自动化检查，以超越仅靠书面指令的方式。

Method: rebuild-dossier工具提供了一个开源管道：(1) 在写代码前锁定应用的确切输入和输出（接口锁定）；(2) 一次一个测试地进行构建，通过自动化检查强制执行而非仅依赖书面指令；(3) 对每个声明进行三层验证——智能体自身报告、自动化日志、以及实际生成的文件，以交叉检查防止虚假成功。

Result: 三个主要结果：(1) 小型对比中，合规智能体在一个保留测试上失败，而违规智能体全部通过，证明通过测试套件并不保证正确性（测试可被游戏化）；(2) 与给较弱模型源代码加一条指令的方法比较：小应用上打平，但在较大应用上失败——且失败原因是自动化检查未运行，指向检查机制（而非接口锁定）才是关键；(3) 三层验证捕获了真实错误，包括作者自己日志代码中的一个bug。这些结果在不同模型和工具链上可复现：更强的模型三次连续遵循了该过程，而较弱的模型从未做到。

Conclusion: 测试通过并不等同于正确性，自动化检查机制（而非接口锁定本身）是决定重建效果的关键因素。三层验证框架对捕捉真实错误至关重要。工具已开源（MIT许可）并可在作者自己的应用中端到端复现。

Abstract: An AI agent's rebuild is only as good as the process that produced it. Prior work found that once a model is strong enough, a multi-agent rebuild pipeline loses to the simplest approach: giving the model the original code and one instruction (AgentModernize). We present rebuild-dossier, an open-source tool that locks an application's real interface - its exact inputs and outputs - before any code is written, then enforces one-test-at-a-time building through automated checks, not written instructions alone.
  Three results shape this evaluation, with differing amounts of evidence. First, in a small comparison, the compliant agent failed a held-back test while the rule-breaking agent passed everything - proof that a passing suite doesn't certify correctness when tests can be gamed. Second, we tested whether this beats simply giving the weaker model the source and one instruction: tied on a small app, but lost outright on a larger one where the automated check wasn't even running - pointing to the check mechanism, not interface-locking, which held up separately. Third, every claim here is checked at three levels - the agent's own report, an automated log, and the actual files produced - catching real errors, including a bug in our own logging code, that a single level would have missed.
  These risks reproduce on a different model and toolchain: a stronger model followed our process three times running, something the weaker model never managed. The tool is public, MIT licensed, and reproduces end to end against our own applications.

</details>


### [91] [Identifying Latent Declarative Representations of Code for Assisting Repository Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23619)
*Shraddha Surana,Ashwin Srinivasan,Michael Bain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ADFD-Migrate方法，通过将程序视为隐式声明式描述的实现，用带标注的数据流图（ADFD）显式化该潜在表示，并利用LLM和静态分析在仓库规模上辅助Fortran到Python的代码迁移。在包含50个Fortran仓库的新基准f2x50上，该方法在382个probe中通过327个（85.6%），40个仓库全部通过，且迁移完整性指标明显优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留软件仓库包含大量无文档代码中的领域知识，难以理解和现代化改造。现有的直接翻译和仓库上下文翻译等方法在仓库规模迁移中覆盖率和集成度不足，需要一种可检查的语义表示来提升迁移质量。

Method: ADFD-Migrate包含以下核心组件：(1) 将程序视为未观察到的声明式计算描述（潜在表示）的实现，用ADFD（过程、数据存储、外部实体、数据流和行为契约）近似该潜在表示；(2) LLM从有界的仓库上下文中推断源ADFD，并由静态分析覆盖检查引导；(3) 依赖感知的分块（dependency-aware chunking）对过程组进行排序以进行目标语言生成；(4) 通过源ADFD与静态恢复的目标ADFD之间的差异指导再生成。

Result: 在f2x50基准（50个Fortran仓库，1.5k-1.6M行代码，三个复杂度层级）上，对382个精选的Fortran oracle探针，生成的Python通过327个（85.6%），40个仓库所有探针全部通过。ADFD-Migrate将所有382个计划行为暴露为可运行目标（对比直接翻译和仓库上下文翻译的99和98），迁移结果指数平均值达93.1%，在47个仓库上比直接翻译高出17-59个百分点。

Conclusion: 可检查的语义瓶颈（ADFD）能够提升仓库规模迁移的覆盖率和集成度，同时为许多仓库实现更低成本的生成，表明该方法在遗留系统现代化中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Legacy software repositories embed decades of domain knowledge in undocumented code, making understanding and modernization difficult. We treat a program as the implementation of an unobserved, declarative description of its computation and investigate whether making this latent declarative representation explicit improves repository-scale porting. ADFD-Migrate approximates the latent representation with an annotated data-flow diagram (ADFD) of processes, data stores, external entities, flows, and behavioral contracts. An LLM infers the source ADFD from bounded repository context, guided by static-analysis coverage checks. Dependency-aware chunking orders bounded process groups for target-language generation. Differences between the source ADFD and a statically recovered target ADFD then guide regeneration. We evaluate ADFD-Migrate on f2x50, a new benchmark of 50~Fortran repositories spanning 1.5k--1.6M lines of code and three complexity tiers, and assess the resulting ports along two dimensions: porting soundness, measured by source-oracle behavioral agreement, and porting completeness, measured by a composite migration outcome index. Against 382 curated Fortran-oracle probes, the generated Python passes 327 (85.6\%), with 40 repositories passing every attempted probe. ADFD-Migrate exposes all 382 planned behaviors as runnable targets, compared with 99 and 98 for direct and repository-context translation and 69 and 30 for the static-profile and dependency-chunking ablations. It also achieves a 93.1\% mean migration outcome index and a 17--59 percentage-point outcome-index advantage over direct translation on 47 repositories. These results suggest that an inspectable semantic bottleneck can improve the coverage and integration of repository-scale migration while enabling lower-cost generation for many repositories.

</details>


### [92] [When May an Agent Stop? Evidence-Carrying Termination for Tool-Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23623)
*Jason Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了证据携带终止（ECT）机制，要求工具使用智能体只有在类型化证书将每个必需答案声明绑定到有效、范围内的轨迹证据，并通过确定性重放重建声明值时才可返回COMPLETE。在48个合成任务和576条轨迹的受控研究中，ECT实现0/288不安全完成（对比基线252/288）和0/66过早终止（对比控制器40/66），同时保持非劣性的支持完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体需要决定何时停止，但现有系统仅通过门槛式终止成功、认证执行轨迹或强制执行运行时策略来管理终止，并未在该论文所关注的COMPLETE边界上，针对受控终止故障测试这种特定的收据、范围和封闭重放设计。

Method: 提出ECT（Evidence-Carrying Termination）机制：智能体只有在类型化证书将每个必需答案声明绑定到有效、范围内轨迹证据，且确定性重放能重建声明值时才可返回COMPLETE。采用锁定的静态研究（48个合成任务、6个工具家族、8种故障）和预设冻结的576轨迹研究进行双重评估，并与终止批评核心、忠实控制器及全轨迹LLM批评进行对比。

Result: 在静态研究中，ECT实现0/288不安全完成，对比基线252/288（差异-87.50百分点，95%CI [-87.50,-87.50]）。在22个主要预留任务簇的576轨迹研究中，ECT实现0/66过早终止对比控制器40/66（差异-60.61百分点，95%CI [-78.79,-40.91]），支持完成率97/132对比92/132（差异3.79百分点，满足-10非劣性边界）。ECT在18/66轨迹中成功恢复，其中17个后续完成并获得支持。

Conclusion: ECT在受控终止故障下显著减少了不安全完成和过早终止，同时保持非劣性的支持完成率。但需注意，ECT仅证明记录轨迹中的支持性证据（在声明假设下），而非外部真实性、安全性或对齐性。

Abstract: Tool-using agents must decide when to stop. Existing systems already gate terminal success, certify execution traces, or enforce runtime polici es, but do not test this particular receipt-, scope-, and closed-replay design at the COMPLETE boundary across controlled termination faults. W e instantiate and evaluate Evidence-Carrying Termination (ECT): an agent may return COMPLETE only when a typed certificate binds every required answer claim to valid, in-scope trace evidence and a deterministic replay reconstructs the claimed value. A locked static study crosses 48 ful ly synthetic tasks in six tool-use families with clean execution and eight faults. ECT produced 0/288 unsafe completions versus 252/288 for the inspected termination-critic core (difference -87.50 pp, 95% task-cluster interval [-87.50, -87.50] pp). A fresh, prespecified and frozen 576- trajectory study then compares ECT with the critic core, its faithful controller, and a full-trace LLM critic. On 22 primary held-out task clus ters, ECT produced 0/66 premature unsupported terminations versus 40/66 for the controller (difference -60.61 pp, 95% interval [-78.79, -40.91] pp), while supported completion was 97/132 versus 92/132 (difference 3.79 pp, interval [0.00, 9.09] pp), satisfying a -10-point noninferiority margin. ECT executed successful recovery in 18/66 trajectories, of which 17 subsequently completed with support; all three closed-loop gates p assed. ECT certifies support in a recorded trace under declared assumptions, not external truth, safety, or alignment.

</details>


### [93] [Callability Is Not Operability: Controlled Interface Interventions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23628)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了工具可操作性（Tool Operability）的概念，区别于传统工具调用的合法性（callability），聚焦于工具接口是否暴露足够的行动相关状态和语义，使智能体在操作不确定性下能够安全地继续执行。作者提出了Agent-First Tooling（AFT）框架，包含五类接口机制，并构建了AFT-Bench基准来系统评估接口设计对智能体的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个工具调用在语法上完全合法，但智能体仍可能因响应丢失、外部副作用已提交等问题而无法确定下一步行动。已提交和未提交的状态对智能体可能变得不可区分，尽管它们需要不同的后续操作。这种"可调用但不可操作"的差距会导致自主智能体在不确定状态下做出错误决策，因此需要系统地研究工具接口如何暴露行动相关的状态和语义。

Method: 本文提出两个核心贡献：（1）Agent-First Tooling（AFT）——一套工具接口机制，涵盖选择性能力发现、执行生命周期与恢复、显式外部效应语义、机器可读结果和后置条件验证五个方面；（2）AFT-Bench——一个受控的接口干预评估框架，固定任务、后端、初始状态、注入故障、智能体和语言模型，仅改变暴露给智能体的接口设计，以隔离接口因素对智能体操作能力的影响。

Result: 通过AFT-Bench这一受控实验框架，可以在其他变量全部固定的情况下，系统地比较不同接口设计对智能体在操作不确定性下继续行动能力的影响，从而验证AFT接口机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 工具的可操作性（operability）与可调用性（callability）是两个不同的维度。为了让自主智能体在外部效应提交但响应丢失等不确定场景下安全地继续行动，工具接口设计需要超越传统API设计的考虑，采纳Agent-First的理念，显式暴露行动相关状态和语义信息。

Abstract: A tool call can be perfectly valid yet still leave an autonomous agent unable to determine what to do next. For example, if an external effect commits but its response is lost, committed and uncommitted states may become indistinguishable to the agent even though they require different continuation actions. We study this gap between callability and operability: whether a tool interface exposes the action-relevant state and semantics needed for an agent to continue safely under operational uncertainty. We operationalize tool operability through Agent-First Tooling (AFT), a set of interface mechanisms spanning selective capability discovery, execution lifecycle and recovery, explicit external-effect semantics, machine-readable results, and postcondition verification. We introduce AFT-Bench, a controlled interface-intervention framework that holds the task, backend, initial state, injected failure, agent, and language model fixed while varying the interface exposed to the agent.

</details>


### [94] [FPGAgent: An LLM-Assisted Framework for Autonomous HLS Code Generation and Verification in FPGA Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23630)
*Tianyu Wang,Wenjie Wang,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FPGAgent是一个多代理框架，专门面向真实FPGA环境进行自主HLS代码生成，并通过端到端的可执行性验证来确保生成的硬件设计不仅通过仿真与综合，还能在实际FPGA板上运行。在包含78个任务的HLS-Eval基准上，FPGAgent相比基线将可综合率平均提升16.9%、可执行性提升26.7%、功能正确性提升30.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的HLS代码生成方法大多仅验证仿真或综合结果，忽略了时序与布局布线约束，导致通过仿真和综合的HLS代码在实际FPGA平台上仍可能无法部署运行。此外，缺乏公开基准使得许多评估仅限于小规模自建测试集，难以系统衡量方法的实际效果。

Method: FPGAgent是一个多代理框架，包含以下核心步骤：(1) 给定自然语言任务规格，注入HLS特定知识并采用进化搜索迭代生成可靠的HLS内核实现；(2) 生成C++验证程序以检查功能正确性，诊断潜在缺陷并引导针对性修复；(3) 综合主机代码用于编译及FPGA板级执行。评估使用五种主流LLM在包含78个多领域任务的HLS-Eval基准上进行，并在真实FPGA平台上验证板级可执行性。

Result: 在HLS-Eval基准上，FPGAgent相比现有基线在可综合率上平均提升16.9%，可执行性提升26.7%，功能正确性提升30.6%。实验结果在真实FPGA平台上完成了板级可执行性验证。

Conclusion: FPGAgent通过端到端的可执行性验证显著提升了基于LLM的HLS代码生成的实用性，证明了在真实FPGA环境中进行完整验证（而非仅仿真或综合）的价值，是该领域首个在成熟基准上经过实验验证的任务规格到可执行HLS生成框架。
