Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown substantial promise for high-level synthesis (HLS) code generation, but most existing approaches validate only simulation or synthesis results. Because of timing and place-and-route constraints, \emph{HLS code that passes simulation and synthesis may still fail to produce deployable, runnable designs on real FPGA platforms}. Moreover, the lack of public benchmarks has limited many evaluations to small, self-curated test suites. We propose FPGAgent, a multi-agent framework tailored to real FPGA environments for autonomous HLS coding with end-to-end executability validation. To the best of our knowledge, FPGAgent is \emph{the first task-specification-to-executable HLS generation framework experimentally validated on a well-established benchmark}. Given a natural-language task specification, FPGAgent injects HLS-specific knowledge and employs evolutionary search to iteratively derive reliable HLS kernel implementations. It then generates a C++ validation program to verify functional correctness, diagnoses potential defects, and guides targeted repairs. Finally, it synthesizes host code for compilation and board-level execution on FPGA hardware. We comprehensively evaluate FPGAgent with five established LLMs on HLS-Eval, a benchmark containing 78 tasks across multiple domains, and verify board-level executability on a real FPGA platform. Compared with existing baselines, FPGAgent improves the synthesizable rate by 16.9% on average, executability by 26.7%, and functional correctness by 30.6%. These results show that FPGAgent substantially improves the practical usability of LLM-based HLS generation and demonstrates the value of end-to-end validation.

</details>


### [95] [ToolRobustBench: Stage-Wise Perturbation Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23635)
*YiShan Zheng,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ToolRobustBench，一个分阶段的工具调用智能体诊断基准，从接口、意图、输出、运行时四个扰动族系统定位工具调用链路的鲁棒性故障，发现输出/观察扰动是主要瓶颈且混合扰动呈非加性失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端（E2E）成功率无法定位工具调用失败的具体来源及传播路径，需要分阶段诊断以区分工具选择、结构接地、参数绑定与反馈处理等环节的鲁棒性差异。

Method: 将工具调用过程划分为多个阶段，构建四类扰动族（工具接口、用户意图、工具输出/观察、运行时环境），并在15,456个单族实例、7个模型、16个本地工具、14个子类型上进行诊断实验，同时开展混合族实验以考察级联失效模式。

Result: 模型在干净场景下性能高但不均匀，鲁棒性显著下降；工具输出/观察扰动是最大瓶颈；混合族实验呈现无法由单族结果解释的非加性失效模式。

Conclusion: ToolRobustBench提供了确定性且具备级联感知能力的基准，可超越干净的调用准确率来诊断工具调用智能体的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on tool calling as a fundamental agent capability, enabling them to invoke external systems and complete tasks beyond text generation. However, clean end-to-end (E2E) success cannot identify where a tool-use failure originates or how it propagates through a call. We introduce ToolRobustBench, a stage-wise diagnostic benchmark for tool-calling agents, where a tool-calling agent is an LLM system that selects a tool, supplies structured arguments, and interprets its returned feedback. ToolRobustBench aligns four perturbation families with the tool-use pipeline: tool-interface, user-intent, tool-output/observation, and runtime-environment perturbations. It attributes failures to tool selection, schema grounding, argument binding, tool-output/runtime-feedback handling, and E2E task success. Experiments on 15,456 single-family instances across 7 models, 16 sampled local tools, 4 perturbation families, and 14 subtypes show high but non-uniform clean performance and substantial robustness degradation, with tool-output/observation perturbation the dominant bottleneck. Mixed-family experiments reveal non-additive failure patterns that are not explained by isolated single-family results. Thus, ToolRobustBench provides a deterministic and cascade-aware benchmark for diagnosing robustness beyond clean tool-calling accuracy;

</details>


### [96] [Feedback That Backfires: Why Small Language Model Agents Repeat the Call They Just Watched Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23651)
*Esmail Gumaan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，在智能体工具箱（harness）中记录失败的工具调用及其错误信息，并不能起到纠正作用，反而会显著降低模型重新输出该失败动作的概率（正确性增益为负）。该反直觉效应在6个指令微调模型、两个环境中普遍成立（90%-100%的个体上），可归结为失败调用的表面形式（占83%的损害）而非错误信息的语义标记。基于这一归因，替换措辞描述和使失败字符串在解码器不可达能有效缓解，而"禁止重复"指令和删除失败上下文（标准做法）无效甚至更糟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统普遍在转录中记录失败的工具调用和错误信息，并假设错误是纠正性信息，让模型继续。作者想验证这一核心假设是否成立，即失败记录是否真的帮助模型避免重复错误。

Method: 定义"纠正增益"为重新输出刚失败动作的log-概率变化量，在模拟工具调用和MBPP程序修复两个环境中，对6个检查点（135M-1.7B，4个家族）进行测量。通过反事实配对（同一调用配失败、成功或中性确认消息）分离表面形式与语义贡献；并测试多种补救措施（替换措辞、解码器屏蔽、显式禁止指令、删除失败上下文）。实验在CPU上端到端运行。

Result: 纠正增益对所有测试模型均为负，归一化后约-1.03 nats/动作token（每个token赔率下降2.8倍），在90%-100%个体上成立。固定候选集中重复失败调用的概率从0.06升至0.54，贪心解码在失败后19%条目逐token复现（失败前为0%）。失败调用的表面形式占83%损害，语义贡献小且符号不稳定。替换为运行时生成的失败描述消除了76%的反转且无token成本；解码器屏蔽同样有效。而"不要重复"指令无效，删除失败上下文重试是最差的配置。

Conclusion: 问题在于智能体工具箱（harness）的工作方式，而非模型对错误消息的理解能力。理解机制后可以预测哪些补救措施有效：处理表面形式的手段（替换措辞、解码器屏蔽）有效，而语义层面的手段（显式指令）或标准上下文清理做法（删除失败上下文）无效甚至有害。

Abstract: Agent harnesses record a failed tool call and its error message in the transcript and ask the model to continue, on the assumption that the error is corrective information. We measure whether it is. Defining the corrective gain of a failure record as the change in log-probability of re-emitting the action that just failed, we find the gain is negative for every instruction-tuned model we tested (6 checkpoints, 135M-1.7B, 4 families) in two environments: simulated tool calling and MBPP program repair. Normalised by action length the effect is about -1.03 nats per action token, a factor of 2.8 in the odds of each token, and holds on 90%-100% of individual items, not only on average. Over a fixed candidate set the probability of repeating the failed call rises from 0.06 to 0.54, and greedy decoding reproduces it token for token on 19% of items after the failure versus 0% before. Counterfactuals pairing the same call with a failure message, a success message, or a neutral acknowledgement separate two effects: the failed call's surface form accounts for 83% of the damage, while the semantic contribution of marking it failed is small and inconsistent in sign across environments. The problem is in the harness, not the model's grasp of error messages, and that predicts which remedies work. Replacing the verbatim call with a runtime-generated description of the failure removes 76% of the inversion at no token cost, and making previously-failed strings unreachable at the decoder acts on the same term. Two plausible remedies do not: an explicit "do not repeat" instruction leaves the measured quantity where it was, and deleting the failed attempt to retry from a clean context, the standard prescription for context contamination, is the worst harness we measured for repetition, because it restores the context that produced the failure. The study runs end to end on a CPU; all artefacts are released.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Executable Models: The Pufibara Agent Harness and the Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark for Physical System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23653)
*Zizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Pufibara，一个面向Modelica物理系统建模的智能体框架，通过维护持久工程状态、关联仿真证据与候选版本、将提交设为显式动作，解决了智能体在物理建模中丢失需求或依赖过时仿真证据的问题。Pufibara在232个任务的基准上超过Claude Code 15-17个任务，同时大幅降低token消耗和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统建模（如Modelica）不同于通用软件工程代码生成：正确性不仅依赖语法和可执行性，还依赖物理一致性和场景相关行为。模型可能编译仿真成功但仍违反物理或工程需求。在连续的修订过程中，智能体可能丢失需求追踪，或依赖过时候选版本产生的仿真证据。这导致现有通用智能体框架（如Claude Code）在物理建模任务上表现不佳。

Method: Pufibara是一个智能体框架，核心设计包括：(1) 跨修订维护持久工程状态；(2) 将执行和仿真证据与产生它的候选版本关联；(3) 将提交（submission）设为显式智能体动作。同时，作者提出一种基于源码接地（source-grounded）的任务构建方法，用于构造真实可独立评估的任务，并基于此构建了包含232个任务的Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark，涵盖模型修复、模型生成和模型调优三类任务。每个提交的候选由基准方拥有的评估器在智能体循环之外评分。

Result: 在两种匹配的LLM后端下对比Pufibara和Claude Code作为完整框架：使用DeepSeek v4 Flash时，Pufibara通过202/232任务，Claude Code通过185个；使用Claude Sonnet 5时，Pufibara通过202个，Claude Code通过187个。Pufibara的逻辑token总量降低76.4%-82.5%，顺序运行时间降低6.1%-58.4%。

Conclusion: 在匹配的LLM后端条件下，完整的智能体框架在物理系统建模任务上的任务成功率和资源消耗方面可以存在显著差异。Pufibara验证了通过持久工程状态管理、证据-候选版本关联和显式提交机制，能够有效提升物理建模智能体的性能与效率。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for simulation-driven engineering. Physical system modeling presents different requirements from general-purpose code generation in software engineering, because correctness depends not only on syntax and executability but also on physical consistency and scenario-dependent behavior. We study this challenge in Modelica, an equation-based modeling language in which a model may compile and simulate while still violating its intended physics or engineering requirements. Across successive revisions, an agent may lose track of requirements or rely on simulation evidence produced by an outdated candidate.
  To address this challenge, we present Pufibara, an agent harness that maintains persistent engineering state across revisions, associates execution and simulation evidence with the candidate that produced it, and makes submission an explicit agent action. To evaluate end-to-end Modelica agent workflows, we also propose a source-grounded method for constructing realistic and independently evaluable tasks. We use this method to build the 232-task Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark, spanning Model Repair, Model Generation, and Model Tuning. Each submitted candidate is scored by a benchmark-owned evaluator outside the agent loop.
  We compare Pufibara with Claude Code as complete harnesses under two matched large language model (LLM) backends. With DeepSeek v4 Flash, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 185 for Claude Code. With Claude Sonnet 5, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 187 for Claude Code. Under the repository-reported token accounting, Pufibara records 76.4%-82.5% lower logical-token totals. Its sequential runtime is 6.1%-58.4% lower. These findings show that, even under matched LLM backends, complete agent harnesses can differ substantially in both task success and resource use for physical system modeling.

</details>


### [98] [Confidently Wrong, Silently So: Auditing Undetectable Failures of a Deployed On-Device Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23663)
*Shashwat Pandey,Satwik Pandey,Suresh Raghu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开发者可访问的端侧基础模型进行了可复现的可靠性审计，发现其存在"任务不对称的校准错误"—在69%的虚假前提问题上编造回答，同时拒绝18%的正常输入。自信正确与自信错误的输出在表面特征上不可区分（AUROC仅0.55），任何廉价的单次生成信号都无法标记这些失败（≤0.68 AUROC），但一个无需模型访问的、黑盒一致性封装方法可将自信编造率从75%降至3%，选择性准确率从43%提升至83%。作者提供了模型无关的审计协议、表面不可区分性测试及可复用的开源基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的语言模型需要判断其输出何时可被信任，但端侧模型已以数亿台设备规模部署且没有服务器端审核，实际部署的配置也几乎未被独立审计。作者将此框定为监督问题：用户或资源受限的开发者能否在推理时判断模型何时出错？这一动机源于端侧模型大规模部署与缺乏独立监督之间的巨大鸿沟。

Method: 采用红队测试方法，从三个方面对开发者可访问的端侧基础模型进行审计：(1) 校准性（calibration）、(2) 对虚假前提问题的自信编造（confident confabulation）、(3) 对良性提示的过度拒绝（over-refusal）。同时提出表面不可区分性测试（surface-indistinguishability test），用15个用户可见特征的分类器检验自信正确与自信错误输出的可区分性，并对比各种便宜的生成信号与黑盒一致性封装方法的效果。整体构成一个模型无关的可复现审计协议。

Result: 发现任务不对称的校准错误：在69%的虚假前提上编造回答，同时拒绝18%的完全良性输入。自报置信度饱和且无判别力（AUROC 0.47；ECE 70，在同类小模型中表现最差）。自信正确与自信错误的输出表面不可区分（分类器AUROC仅0.55，等价性得到确认）。所有低价单次生成信号均失败（≤0.68 AUROC），但黑盒一致性封装方法无需模型访问即可恢复可靠性：自信编造从75%降至3%，选择性准确率从43%提升至83%，成本可调节。

Conclusion: 端侧模型的可靠性缺陷无法通过任何廉价的单次生成信号在推理时被标记，但采用不依赖模型内部访问的黑盒一致性方法可以显著恢复可靠性。作者发布的审计协议、测试方法及开源基础设施为今后审计已部署模型提供了可复用的标准工具，强调了对端侧模型进行独立、可复现审计的必要性与可行性。

Abstract: Aligning deployed language models requires knowing when their outputs can be trusted, yet on-device models now ship to hundreds of millions of devices with no server-side moderation, and the configuration developers can actually deploy is rarely audited independently. We present a reproducible reliability audit of the developer-accessible on-device foundation model, framed as an oversight question: can a user or a resource-constrained developer tell when the model is wrong? Red-teaming it on calibration, confident confabulation on false-premise questions, and over-refusal of benign prompts, we find a \emph{task-asymmetric miscalibration}: its guardrails fail in opposite directions across tasks (confabulating on 69\% of false premises while refusing 18\% of entirely benign inputs), atop a self-reported confidence that is saturated and non-discriminative (AUROC 0.47; ECE 70, worst among comparable small models). Crucially, confident-correct and confident-wrong outputs are \emph{surface-indistinguishable}: a classifier over 15 user-visible features separates them at AUROC only 0.55 (equivalence-confirmed), leaving no signal for oversight at inference time. No cheap single-generation signal flags these failures ($\le$0.68 AUROC), whereas a black-box consistency wrapper requiring no model access recovers reliability (confident confabulation 75\%$\to$3\%; selective accuracy 43\%$\to$83\%) at a tunable cost. We contribute a model-agnostic audit protocol, a surface-indistinguishability test, and released code and frozen evaluation items as reusable infrastructure for auditing deployed models.

</details>


### [99] [Enhancing Bug Report Templates in the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23754)
*Laura Baird,Neelesh Reddybattula,Nazanin Siavash,Terrance E. Boult,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出增强TianoCore开源社区GitHub Issues中的Bug报告模板，通过添加新字段来改进bug分类（triage）和解决流程。目前处于进行中状态，已通过开发者访谈获得初步反馈，计划开展更多访谈和A/B测试来验证方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TianoCore社区的Bug报告中，部分对bug分类和修复有用的信息仅在自由文本中偶尔出现，缺乏结构化的收集方式，导致bug分类和解决效率低下。为此需要改进模板以规范化关键信息的提交。

Method: 首先分析bug仓库数据以识别对bug分类和修复有用的信息模式；然后提出在现有TianoCore Bug报告模板中新增若干字段；最后通过访谈开发者获取反馈并调整方案，计划未来采用A/B测试进行验证。研究聚焦于EDK II项目（UEFI固件的核心）。

Result: 目前已完成初步数据分析和部分开发者访谈，提出了增强模板的方案，但研究仍在进行中，尚未完成A/B测试和社区层面的全面验证。

Conclusion: 通过结构化Bug报告模板字段，有望提升TianoCore社区Bug分类和解决的效率与效果，但需要进一步通过更大规模的社区访谈和A/B测试来验证该方法的实用性和可接受性。

Abstract: We propose enhancing the bug report templates in the GitHub Issues issue tracking system used by the TianoCore open-source community with the aim of improving the bug triage and resolution process. We analyze the bug repository data and find patterns of information that are useful for bug triage and fixing. However, some of them are only occasionally included in the free-form text of bug reports. Therefore, we propose adding a few new fields to the existing TianoCore bug report template. In this study, we focus on the key TianoCore project, EDK II, which constitutes the core of the UEFI firmware across various firmware vendors and original equipment manufacturers. This study is currently a work-in-progress. So far, we have interviewed a few developers to obtain their feedback and adjust the proposed approach. We are planning more interviews with the TianoCore community to conduct A/B tests and validate our approach to achieve effective and efficient bug triage and resolution.

</details>


### [100] [SPIDER4TianoCore: Enhancing Patch-Propagation for the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23755)
*Laura Baird,Devin Haggitt,Terrance E. Boult,Aravind Machiry,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并展示了SPIDER4TianoCore，一个打包的Python命令行工具，为TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链提供集成阶段的补丁状态证据。该工具针对上游补丁前后对和准备好的下游目标，输出"易受攻击"、"已修补"、"不适用"或"不确定"四种状态，并附带供维护者审查的证据。在8个公共下游EDK II仓库和2个CVE的20个目标/CVE对上评估，分析器产生了10个高置信度补丁前匹配和4个高置信度补丁后匹配，对6个目标保守弃权，没有出现相对于记录的补丁状态标签的高置信度错误分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链中，下游仓库需要判断上游补丁是否已应用于自己的代码，但缺乏自动化的、可复现的工具来提供集成阶段的补丁状态证据。SPIDER框架提供了补丁传播的概念，但需要针对TianoCore生态进行具体实现。

Method: 开发了一个基于SPIDER补丁传播框架的Python命令行工具SPIDER4TianoCore。该工具接收上游补丁前后代码对和准备好的下游目标，通过分析器判断补丁状态，输出Vulnerable、Already Patched、Not Applicable或Uncertain四种分类结果，并生成供维护者审查的证据。

Result: 在来自8个公共下游EDK II仓库和2个CVE的20个目标/CVE对上评估：产生10个高置信度补丁前匹配和4个高置信度补丁后匹配，对6个目标保守弃权，没有高置信度的错误分类。结果表明该工具能为准备好的目标生成可复现的证据，而非声称普遍的下游准确性。

Conclusion: 初步结果证明SPIDER4TianoCore能够为准备好的目标实现可复现的证据生成，作为补丁状态分类的辅助工具价值有限但具有参考意义。工具并不证明补丁可以安全传播，且其能力范围限于准备好的目标，而非通用下游准确性。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate SPIDER4TianoCore, a packaged Python command-line tool that provides integration-stage patch-status evidence for the TianoCore/UEFI firmware supply chain. Given an upstream pre-patch and post-patch pair and prepared downstream targets, the tool reports Vulnerable, Already Patched, Not Applicable, or Uncertain with supporting evidence for maintainer review. Our work is inspired by SPIDER's patch-propagation framing, but SPIDER4TianoCore does not itself prove that a patch is safe to propagate. We evaluate the engine on 20 prepared target/CVE pairs from eight public downstream EDK II repositories and two CVEs. The analyzers produce 10 high-confidence pre-patch matches and four high-confidence post-patch matches, conservatively abstain on six targets, and make no confidently wrong classifications relative to the recorded manual patch-state labels. These preliminary results demonstrate reproducible evidence generation for prepared targets rather than general downstream accuracy.

</details>


### [101] [Scale, Concentration, and Entry Timing in the Shopify App Ecosystem: A Longitudinal Study of Platform Governance and Application Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23771)
*Fabrizio Assabese,Peter-Jan Celis,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对Shopify应用市场进行全面实证分析，基于2025年9月快照中的24,826个应用、7,708个应用的366周面板数据及Internet Archive的历史列表记录，分析了市场结构、进入时序效应、平台治理影响及应用失败的可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shopify市场拥有超过16,000个活跃第三方应用、服务270万家商户并产生约7060亿美元年销售额，但学界对其市场结构和采用驱动因素缺乏实证证据，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用三项数据源：2025年9月的全量快照（24,826个应用）、2019年2月至2026年3月的366周面板数据（7,708个应用）、基于Internet Archive重建的应用列表历史。分析方法包括市场集中度分析（55个功能类别）、进入时序与增长关系的回归分析（50个类别、7年季度再估计）、平台治理事件（Chat进入、Product Reviews弃用、平台收入分成调整）的准自然实验分析，以及基于前6个月公开数据的退出预测模型（交叉验证AUC）。

Result: 研究发现：55个类别中超过一半为低集中度、20%高度集中，更大类别集中度更低；88%的类别中后进入者比先行者增长更快，且早期先行者净检测装机量下降而后进入者增加；平台治理影响不对称——Shopify进入聊天领域逆转了两年以上的去集中化趋势，Product Reviews弃用释放191,000个装机量但最多三分之一在一年内被竞争者吸收，2021年收入分成削减对进入或留存无可检测影响；中位退出应用峰值为8个检测装机量且在启动后68周内从追踪中消失，92%的类别在7年内领导地位易主，基于前6个月数据预测两年内退出的AUC超过0.8。

Conclusion: Shopify应用市场仍保持可竞争性；利益分配更多取决于平台治理和进入条件，而非进入时序本身。

Abstract: The Shopify marketplace hosts more than 16,000 active third-party applications, serving 2.7 million active merchant stores generating an estimated \$706 billion in annual sales, yet little empirical evidence exists on its structure and adoption drivers. We analyse it using a September 2025 snapshot of all 24,826 applications, a weekly panel of 7,708 applications over 366 weeks (February 2019 to March 2026), and listing histories reconstructed from the Internet Archive. Across 55 established functional categories, over half were low-concentration and 20\% highly concentrated, with larger categories consistently less concentrated. Later entrants grew faster than early movers in 88\% of the 50 analysed categories, persisting across seven years of quarterly re-estimations, with early movers on net losing detected installations whilst late movers gained them. Platform governance reshaped competition asymmetrically: Shopify's entry into chat reversed more than two years of de-concentration; the deprecation of its Product Reviews application, to our knowledge the first measured platform-owner exit from complementor category, released 191,000 installations of which at most a third reappeared as competitor adoption within a year; the 2021 reduction of the platform revenue share produced no detectable change in entry or retention. Failure is largely silent and predictable: the median exiting application peaked at 8 detected installations and disappeared from tracking within 68 weeks of launch, category leadership changed hands in 92\% of categories over seven years, and publicly observable data from an application's first six months predict exit within two years with cross-validated AUC above 0.8. App markets on Shopify remain contestable; who benefits depends on platform governance and entry conditions more than on entry timing.

</details>


### [102] [Automated Synthesis of Cloud Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23842)
*Archit Bhatnagar,Zhenning Yang,Sarah McClure,Yiming Qiu,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Ang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CloudEmu是一种自动化构建云模拟器的方法，通过神经符号代码合成从云文档生成模拟器，结合LLM的文档理解与代码生成能力和云特定的符号抽象来抑制幻觉，并以真实云作为oracle进行自动化测试、修复和对齐。实验表明其在AWS和GCP上优于手动开发十年的LocalStack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DevOps程序测试需要在真实云资源上执行，耗时、不安全且成本高。云模拟器虽能缓解该问题，但手工构建代价巨大——开发者需手动解读大量云文档并为每个服务、API及其交互编写逻辑，难以扩展到云系统的复杂性和演进性。

Method: 提出自动化方法CloudEmu，核心思路是神经符号代码合成：利用LLM在文档理解和代码生成方面的通用能力，结合云特定的符号抽象来抑制幻觉并保证大规模精度，同时将真实云作为oracle进行自动化测试、修复和系统对齐。

Result: 在AWS和GCP的主要服务上验证了CloudEmu的有效性（覆盖率和准确性），并超越了由大型工程团队手动开发十年之久的领先工具LocalStack。

Conclusion: CloudEmu证明通过神经符号方法可以从云文档自动化构建高覆盖、高精度的云模拟器，大幅降低人工成本，为DevOps编程测试提供更可靠、更便宜的本地化基础设施。

Abstract: DevOps programming (e.g., using CLI/API scripts or IaC frameworks) is key to cloud infrastructure management. Unlike traditional programming tasks, DevOps program testing needs provisioning and execution against actual cloud resources, which is often time-consuming, unsafe, and costly. Cloud emulators have gained popularity for easing DevOps program testing; they are generally API-level mocks that can execute DevOps programs in a local environment. Still, building these emulators remains challenging: developers must manually interpret extensive cloud documentation and handcraft logic for each service, API, and their interaction. This does not scale to the complexity of the cloud, which is further a moving target as the services and APIs evolve. CloudEmu is an automated approach that constructs emulators based on cloud documentation via neurosymbolic code synthesis. The key idea is to combine LLMs' general strengths in documentation understanding and code generation with cloud-specific symbolic abstractions that suppress hallucinations and enforce precision at scale, while using the real cloud as an oracle for automated testing, repair, and alignment. Our evaluation shows the effectiveness of CloudEmu on major cloud provider (AWS and GCP) services in both coverage and accuracy. CloudEmu outperforms the existing leading tool LocalStack, which was manually developed by a large team of engineers over a decade.

</details>


### [103] [The Empire, Long Divided, Must Unite: Architectural Convergence in Three LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23953)
*Dai Jiahong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对三个开源编码Agent harness（LangChain的deepagents、Earendil的pi、DeepSeek的dsh）进行了源码级多案例研究，发现尽管两个成熟harness从对立哲学出发沿相反方向演化，却收敛到统一的架构中间形态（五个重复出现元素），而一个关键维度——外部可验证性——在所有harness中均缺失，被认为是未来演进的下一个关键轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness（围绕语言模型构建上下文、中介工具、运行循环并持久化状态的代码层）日益成为Agent行为的关键约束，但其架构设计缺乏系统性研究。论文试图理解：在工程实践中，不同哲学起点下的harness究竟走向何处？是否存在共同的架构形态？

Method: 采用源码级多案例研究方法：选取三个哲学对立的开源harness（面向batteries-included的deepagents、激进极简主义的pi、全插件化的dsh），在固定commit上通读源码并跟踪其commit历史，归纳架构元素；将第三个harness作为held-out验证样本，检验归纳结果的普适性。

Result: 发现两个成熟harness沿相反方向演化（deepagents削减脚手架、pi累积持久化基础设施）却收敛到五种共同架构元素：商品化的循环、可追加回放式会话记录、模型怪癖以数据形式保存、分级上下文揭示、显式扩展接缝。held-out的第三个harness全部具备这五个元素，且其中一个接缝直接复用了他人的实现。将收敛分解为平行发现、扩散和字面复用三种机制。唯一不收敛的维度是外部可验证性，在所有harness中完全缺失。

Conclusion: Agent harness架构存在强烈的趋同演化趋势，即使哲学起点对立也会收敛于中间形态；但外部可验证性（不受信任运行时约束的可审计记录）仍是未解决的问题，被预测为面向溯源敏感领域的harness未来分化的下一维度。

Abstract: An agent harness is what turns a language model into an autonomous agent: the surrounding code that builds the model's context, mediates its tools, runs the loop, and persists state across a long-horizon run. This layer, not the model it wraps, is increasingly the binding constraint on agent behaviour. We present a source-level, multi-case study of three open coding-agent harnesses built from deliberately opposing philosophies: LangChain's deepagents (batteries-included), Earendil's pi (radical minimalism), and DeepSeek's dsh (everything-is-a-plugin). Reading each at a pinned commit and following its commit history, we find that the two mature harnesses have travelled in opposite directions (deepagents subtracting authored scaffolding, pi accreting durable infrastructure), yet converged toward one architectural middle form of five recurring elements: a commoditised loop, an append-only replayable session record, model quirks kept as data, progressive disclosure of context, and explicit extension seams. A third harness, read afterward as a held-out check, exhibits all five, and in one seam reuses another's implementation outright. We therefore do not claim independent invention, and decompose the convergence into parallel discovery, diffusion, and literal reuse. Finally, one load-bearing dimension shows no convergence, and indeed no presence: external verifiability, a tamper-evident record an outside party can check without trusting the runtime. We read this absence not as an oversight but as a predictive gap, the next axis on which harnesses for provenance-sensitive domains will differ.

</details>


### [104] [Evaluating Language Models on Cross-Language Code Functional Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23961)
*Hui Sun,Anderson Uchôa,Rohit Gheyi,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PolyHuman数据集，评估LLM在跨语言（CPP/Java/Python）人类编写程序上的功能等价性判断能力，发现LLM在困难任务上倾向于将不等价代码误判为等价，存在语言敏感性、相似性线索依赖和运行不稳定性等问题，表明当前LLM并未可靠掌握程序语义等价推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码理解评测主要基于单语言或合成代码，无法验证模型是否真正理解程序语义。作者质疑当前结果是否反映真实语义理解，因此聚焦于跨语言、人类编写代码的功能等价性判断这一更深层推理场景进行考察。

Method: 构建PolyHuman数据集（人类编写的CPP/Java/Python程序，涉及代码翻译任务），评测开源和专有LLM在语言内与跨语言功能等价性检测上的表现；以GPT-o4-mini为代表模型评估稳定性；对81例系统性误判案例进行人工分析，检查代码逻辑与Chain-of-Thought推理；将失败模式在GPT-o4-mini、Claude-Opus-4.7和Gemini-3-Flash之间进行跨模型比较。

Result: 发现三类关键结果：(1) 难度依赖的失败模式——问题越难，模型越倾向于把不等价代码误判为等价；(2) 最佳模型对编程语言存在敏感性（尤其对Python表现出更保守的行为）；(3) 模型部分依赖表面相似性线索。此外GPT-o4-mini在相同设置下表现出显著的运行间不稳定性，说明能力是不一致的而非缺失。

Conclusion: 当前LLM无论在语言内还是跨语言场景下均不可靠地捕获功能等价性，其对代码"语义理解"的能力被高估，该领域仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a variety of code-understanding tasks, leading many to believe that they can reason about program semantics. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on single-language settings or rely on synthetically generated code, raising concerns about whether current results reflect true semantic understanding. Aims: We investigate whether LLMs can accurately judge functional equivalence across different programming languages in human-written code, a setting that requires deeper reasoning beyond superficial similarity. Method: We introduce PolyHuman, a dataset of human-written programs in CPP, Java, and Python. Using this dataset, we evaluate intra- and inter-language equivalence detection across open-weight and proprietary LLMs, selecting GPT-o4-mini as a representative model to assess stability. We then manually analyze 81 cases of systematic disagreement in which models incorrectly judge functional equivalence, examining the code logic and the generated Chain-of-Thought reasoning. Finally, we categorize these failures and compare them across GPT-o4-mini, Claude-Opus-4.7, and Gemini-3-Flash to determine whether they reflect model-specific issues or broader limitations of state-of-the-art LLMs. Results: We identify a difficulty-dependent breakdown in equivalence judgment (harder problems make the model increasingly prone to misclassifying non-equivalent code as equivalent), a model-specific sensitivity to programming language for the best-performing model (particularly a more conservative behavior on Python), and a partial reliance on similarity-based cues. GPT-o4-mini also shows substantial run-to-run instability under identical settings, indicating inconsistent rather than absent capability. Conclusions: Current LLMs do not reliably capture functional equivalence within or across languages.

</details>


### [105] [DeepRepoQA: Code Repository Question Answering with Deep Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24221)
*Weihan Peng,Yuling Shi,Yingwei Ma,Longfei Yun,Beijun Shen,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DeepRepoQA，一个基于智能体框架的仓库级代码问答系统，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）实现多跳推理和长距离代码依赖的深层次理解，在SWE-QA基准上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库理解方法主要依赖表面级的代码检索，缺乏跨多文件、复杂软件架构的深度推理能力，且难以将答案溯源到长距离代码依赖中。

Method: DeepRepoQA采用智能体化（agentic）框架，利用LLM智能体通过系统化的树搜索来探索仓库结构；其中使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）机制使智能体能够动态搜索、导航和检查代码，支持对长距离代码依赖的多跳推理。

Result: 在SWE-QA基准上的综合实验表明，DeepRepoQA相对于强基线取得了显著的性能提升，验证了系统化的MCTS引导探索在多跳仓库推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: MCTS引导的系统化探索能够有效增强智能体对仓库级代码的深度理解和多跳推理能力，为仓库级代码问答提供了一个有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Answering developer questions about a software repository is a critical yet under-explored problem in software engineering. While existing repository understanding methods have advanced the field, they predominantly rely on surface-level code retrieval and lack the ability for deep reasoning over multiple files, complex software architectures, and grounding answers in long-range code dependencies. To address these limitations, we propose DeepRepoQA, a novel question answering (QA) framework for repository-level code understanding. DeepRepoQA builds on an agentic framework where LLM agents find answers through a systematic tree search over the repository structure. A Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) mechanism is employed to empower agents to dynamically search, navigate, and inspect code, enabling effective multi-hop reasoning over long-range code dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on the SWE-QA benchmark demonstrate substantial performance gains over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of systematic MCTS-guided exploration for multi-hop repository reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [Mutation Testing of Simulink Cyber-Physical System Models: Challenges and Solutions in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24250)
*Murat Kavak,Onur Kilincceker,Serge Demeyer,Kevin Vandenbroucke,Halim Abdurrahman Ceylan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在DUCO公司（生产建筑通风系统）将变异测试应用于Simulink/Stateflow设计模型的经验教训，识别出等效变异体、需求可追溯性及Stateflow变异测试三个学术界尚未充分报道的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异测试支持向左移动（shift-left）测试策略，尤其在后期发现缺陷成本极高的行业中至关重要。在网络物理系统背景下，向左移动意味着工程师需要测试用于仿真、原型设计和可行性分析的设计模型。作者希望通过在工业环境（DUCO公司）中实践变异测试，揭示实际应用中的挑战和教训。

Method: 作者在DUCO公司开展了一个试点项目，使用其工具原型MUT4SLX对基于Simulink和StateFlow的模型进行变异测试。DUCO工程师在模型驱动工程和单元测试方面拥有多年经验。作者在试点过程中系统地记录了遇到的挑战和学到的经验。

Result: 研究发现三个在现有学术文献中未充分报道的特定挑战：等效变异体（equivalent mutants）问题、需求可追溯性（requirement traceability）问题，以及针对Stateflow的变异测试问题。

Conclusion: 变异测试在工业模型驱动开发的背景下虽具有可行性并支持向左移动测试策略，但等效变异体识别、需求追踪和Stateflow变异算子等挑战需要学术界进一步关注和解决，以促进变异测试在类似工业场景中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Several experience reports illustrate that mutation testing is capable of supporting a shift-left testing strategy, especially in industries where late bug discovery incurs very high costs and risks. In the context of cyber-physical systems, a shift left implies that engineers need to test the design models used to simulate, prototype, and analyse the feasibility of the system under design. In this paper, we analyse the challenges we encountered and the lessons we learned when incorporating mutation testing in the context of DUCO, a company producing ventilation systems for buildings. The engineers within DUCO have years of experience with model-based engineering centered around Simulink and StateFlow, including unit tests for their models. During a pilot project with our tool prototype MUT4SLX we learned that equivalent mutants, requirement traceability, and mutation testing for Stateflow represent particular challenges not yet reported in the academic literature.

</details>


### [107] [Cross-Stack Validation of Language-Model Training: A Clinical Fine-Tuning Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24267)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轨迹级差分测试协议，用独立实现的训练栈（PyTorch vs. Zig编写的numbat框架）作为整个微调流水线的差分预言机，而非仅针对算子或推理路径。在Qwen3-0.6B的LoRA微调任务（16.8万临床QA对）上，两栈的平均交叉熵差异仅0.134%，但暴露了17个单栈开发难以发现的缺陷，其中影响最大的缺陷竟在数据渲染层而非数值内核，且4个缺陷仅能从特定语言运行时（内存模型不同）触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练存在"预言机问题"：一次run可能正常收敛并产出可用模型，但底层软件实际计算的内容与规范不符。由于几乎所有训练工作都运行在同一技术栈上，缺乏独立的参照物来验证正确性。现有差分测试仅针对算子和推理路径，未覆盖整个训练流水线。作者希望通过独立实现的训练栈作为差分预言机，检验端到端微调流程的正确性。

Method: 作者定义了轨迹级（trajectory-level）测试协议，包含三个核心要素：(1) 共享规范（shared specification）；(2) 跨检查点（cross-check points），涵盖算术运算、模型加载、数据渲染和学习轨迹；(3) 栈、编排（orchestration）和语言运行时的独立性分离。将该协议应用于Qwen3-0.6B在168,574条临床问答对上的LoRA微调，分别在PyTorch和numbat（用Zig编写的独立框架）上执行，并通过C接口驱动六种语言运行。

Result: 在42次配对评估（覆盖完整epoch）中，两个栈的保留集交叉熵平均差异仅0.134%，四种实现结束epoch时彼此相差在0.15%以内。对比共发现17个单栈开发遗漏的缺陷，其中两个在软件工程上具有显著意义：影响训练模型最大的缺陷位于数值内核之外——临床文本渲染方式不匹配导致保留集损失偏差0.15，比其旁边发现的算术缺陷大500倍；另有4个缺陷仅能从内存模型与前两种实现不同的语言中触发，包括跨线程迁移任务的调度器、对设备内存盲区的收集器、以及需要接口缺少的原语的ownership纪律。

Conclusion: 独立训练栈作为整条微调流水线的差分预言机是有效的，能够发现传统零散差分测试无法发现的端到端缺陷。实现多样性具有多个轴，语言运行时（language runtime）是其中一个被忽视的关键维度——内存模型差异能暴露其他层面无法触及的缺陷。该工作为深度学习系统的正确性保障提供了新的方法论：不仅要在算子级别做差分，更要在轨迹级别对完整流水线做交叉验证。

Abstract: Neural network training has an oracle problem: a run can converge normally and yield a usable model while the software beneath it computes something other than specified. Almost all such work runs on one stack, so there is rarely anything independent to check against. We study whether independently implemented training stacks can serve as differential oracles for a whole fine-tuning pipeline, rather than the operators and inference paths that prior differential testing targets. We define a trajectory-level protocol -- a shared specification, cross-check points spanning arithmetic, model loading, data rendering and the learning trajectory, and a separation of independence of the stack, the orchestration and the language runtime -- and apply it to a LoRA adaptation of Qwen3-0.6B over 168,574 clinical question-answer pairs under PyTorch and under numbat, an independent framework written in Zig, driven natively and through its C interface from six languages. Across 42 paired evaluations spanning a full epoch the two stacks' held-out cross-entropy differs by 0.134% on average, and four implementations end the epoch within 0.15% of one another. The comparison exposed 17 faults that single-implementation development had missed, two of them notable for software engineering. The fault with the largest effect on the trained model lay outside the numerical kernels: a mismatch in how clinical text was rendered moved held-out loss 0.15, some 500 times more than the arithmetic faults found beside it. And four faults were reachable only from a language whose memory model differs from the first two implementations: a scheduler migrating work across threads, a collector blind to device memory, an ownership discipline needing a primitive the interface lacked. Implementation diversity has several axes, and the runtime is one.

</details>


### [108] [Towards LLM-Enhanced Android Taint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24269)
*Nicholas Miazzo,Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Jacques Klein,Eleonora Losiouk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文初步探索了使用现成的LLM（如Gemini-3 Flash）通过agentic交互策略进行Android应用污点分析，在DroidBench基准上F1分数达0.96，远超FlowDroid的0.55，尤其在ICC、隐式流和反射等困难类别上表现突出，并能发现FlowDroid遗漏的真实应用数据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态污点分析工具（如FlowDroid）由于Android框架建模复杂而面临已知挑战，在ICC、隐式流和反射等场景下准确率低下。作者希望探究LLM能否作为替代或补充方案，有效推理Android应用的污点流。

Method: 采用agentic交互策略，让LLM迭代探索代码并推理数据流，而非一次性分析整个应用。在DroidBench基准上与FlowDroid进行对比评估，并在少量真实应用上进行验证。

Result: Gemini-3 Flash在DroidBench上达到0.96的F1分数，而FlowDroid为0.55。在ICC（0.95 vs 0.17）、隐式流（0.94 vs 0.00）和反射（1.00 vs 0.50）等FlowDroid表现不佳的类别中显著领先。在真实应用中发现FlowDroid未报告的数据泄露。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明LLM推理可以有效补充传统静态污点分析，为未来混合LLM增强的污点分析流水线研究提供了动机和方向。

Abstract: Taint analysis is a fundamental technique for detecting sensitive data leaks in Android apps. However, traditional static tools, such as FlowDroid, still face well-known challenges due to the complexity of accurately modeling the Android framework. In this paper, we investigate whether off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively reason about taint flows in Android apps. Our preliminary approach relies on an agentic interaction strategy, enabling the LLM to iteratively explore code and reason about data flows.
  We conduct an initial evaluation on the DroidBench benchmark against FlowDroid, where our approach outperforms the baseline: Gemini-3 Flash achieves an F1-score of 0.96, compared to 0.55 for FlowDroid. In particular, we observe improvements in challenging categories such as inter-component communication (0.95 vs. 0.17), implicit flows (0.94 vs. 0.00), and reflection (1.00 vs. 0.50), where FlowDroid typically struggles. On a small set of real-world apps, the LLM-based approach also identifies additional potential data leaks not reported by FlowDroid. These preliminary findings suggest that LLM reasoning may effectively complement traditional static taint analysis, motivating future research on hybrid LLM-enhanced taint analysis pipelines.

</details>


### [109] [Observability and Fault Injection for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24271)
*Zahra Seyedghorban,Egor Klimov,Arie van Deursen,Annibale Panichella,Burcu Kulahcioglu Ozkan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了llmmas-otel，一个轻量级且与框架无关的工具，结合OpenTelemetry分布式追踪与故障注入技术，用于LLM多智能体系统在软件工程任务中的可观测性、调试和受控故障评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在软件工程任务中日益普及，但它们难以检查、调试和在受控故障条件下进行评估，缺乏系统化的可观测性工具。

Method: 该工具通过OpenTelemetry对智能体执行进行追踪对齐的遥测数据采集，覆盖工作流阶段、智能体步骤、智能体间通信、工具调用和LLM调用等层面，并在选定的交互点支持目标化故障注入，支持基线与故障执行的可复现对比。

Result: 工具已在最小演示工作流和一个真实的软件开发生成式LLM多智能体系统上进行了初步验证，展示了对齐追踪和运行工件的效果检查能力。

Conclusion: llmmas-otel为LLM多智能体系统在软件工程中的可观测性、调试与评估提供了实用的框架无关解决方案，未来有望扩展到更复杂的故障场景和更广泛的系统验证。

Abstract: Large Language Model-based multi-agent systems are increasingly explored for software engineering tasks, but they remain difficult to inspect, debug, and evaluate under controlled failures. We present llmmas-otel, a lightweight and framework-agnostic tool that combines OpenTelemetry-based distributed tracing with fault injection for LLM-based multi-agent systems in software engineering workflows. The tool instruments agent executions with trace-aligned telemetry across workflow phases, agent steps, inter-agent communication, tool calls, and LLM invocations, and supports targeted fault injection at selected interaction points. This makes it possible to compare baseline and faulty executions in a reproducible way and inspect the effects through aligned traces and run artifacts. We describe the motivation, architecture, implementation, current capabilities, and initial validation of the tool on a minimal demo workflow and a real LLM-based multi-agent system for software development.

</details>


### [110] [Ockhamareto: Pareto-Gated Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Concise Unit-Test Generation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24473)
*Dong Huang,Mark Harman,Jie M. Zhang,Zhijiang Guo,Mingzhe Du,See Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Ockhamareto是一个单次GRPO框架，用于单元测试生成与选择，基于奥卡姆剃刀原则和帕累托最优性。它通过Pareto门控奖励和Token级段信用机制，在UnLeakedTestBench上严格帕累托支配最强的RL基线MIST-RL，在四个基准测试中均以最小测试套件同时提升变异和覆盖率指标，在所有模型规模上增加+30-35pp变异得分，并揭示Pareto前沿膝点与函数大小等代理指标不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习（RL）方法的测试生成框架在单元测试的质量（变异覆盖）与效率（测试数量）之间存在固有的权衡困境，通常只能优化单一目标或在两者间折中。作者希望突破这一局限，通过结合奥卡姆剃刀原则（最小化测试数量）和帕累托最优性（同时优化多个冲突目标），实现既捕捉更多bug又使用更少测试的范式。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：（1）Pareto-gated Bonus——只在(mutation, -#tests)空间中非被支配的rollout上给予奖励激励；（2）Token-level Segment Credit——将每个测试的边际变异击杀归因回其单元测试块的每个token，实现细粒度的信用分配。整个框架是单次（single-shot）GRPO训练，无需多次迭代优化。

Result: 在UnLeakedTestBench上严格帕累托支配MIST-RL基线；变异得分49.9% vs 31.3%（N=5），平均测试数2.60 vs 4.67，实现每测试权衡改善3.4倍。在HumanEval+、MBPP+、CodeContests、TestGenEval-Lite四个基准上均领先变异和覆盖率指标并配最小套件；在4B、9B、27B规模上均增加+30-35pp变异得分。此外发现Pareto前沿膝点与函数大小等简单代理指标无相关性。

Conclusion: Ockhamareto证明将奥卡姆剃刀原则与帕累托最优性结合可以有效解决单元测试生成中的质量-效率权衡问题，实现严格的多目标支配。同时，由于膝点无法通过函数大小等简单代理指标预测，计算完整Pareto前沿对于识别每个被测函数的关键工程权衡是必要的。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{Ockhamareto}, a single-shot GRPO framework for unit-test generation and selection, based on the principles of \emph{Ockham's Razor} and \emph{Pareto Optimality}. Ockhamareto has two principal components: (i)~a \emph{Pareto-gated Bonus} that rewards only rollouts non-dominated in~(mutation, $-$\#tests) space, and (ii)~\emph{Token-level Segment Credit}, which attributes each test's marginal mutation kills back to the tokens of its unit-test block. On the \emph{UnLeakedTestBench~(ULT)}, Ockhamareto \emph{strictly Pareto-dominates} the strongest RL baseline~(\emph{MIST-RL}). Furthermore, it dominates on {\em each and all} optimization objectives, catching more bugs ($49.9\%$ vs $31.3\%$ mutation score at $N{=}5$), using \emph{fewer} tests ($2.60$ vs $4.67$ on average), thereby achieving $3.4\times$ the per-test trade-off improvement. The advantage is found in all four benchmarks~(\emph{HumanEval+}, \emph{MBPP+}, \emph{CodeContests}, \emph{TestGenEval-Lite}): Ockhamareto leads both mutation and coverage metrics on every one, always with the smallest suite. Ockhamareto also outperforms the state-of-the-art at all model scales, adding $+30$--$35$~pp mutation at 4B, 9B, and 27B model sizes. We also show that the knee point of the optimal trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness on the Pareto front is not correlated with obvious more easily computed proxy metrics, such as function size. This finding motivates the Pareto front computation; it is needed to identify this crucial engineering trade-off for each function under test.

</details>


### [111] [LumiXAI: A Modular Full-Stack Framework for Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24524)
*Alfio Ferrara,Lorenzo Gatta,Sergio Picascia,Elisabetta Rocchetti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LumiXAI是一个模块化的全栈框架，将特征归因分析整合为单一系统，同时支持分类与生成式归因，提供交互式GUI、可插拔架构和三个访问层级，通过容器化服务和持久化结果实现跨机器可复现的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的解释性（可解释性）软件生态碎片化严重：单一工具仅擅长单一模态、单一API或GUI、或固定方法集，很少能同时具备这些能力。同时，许多工具主要面向领域专家，需要编程技能或归因方法的背景知识，对非专家用户不够友好。因此需要一套整合的框架来解决这些可访问性和一体化问题。

Method: LumiXAI采用模块化全栈架构，将分类归因和生成式归因耦合在一起。其关键技术设计包括：（1）交互式GUI支持双向探索（bidirectional exploration）；（2）可插拔架构允许注册新模型和新方法；（3）三个访问层级（非程序员、开发者、扩展者）由单一后端统一支撑；（4）容器化服务与持久化结果确保跨机器可复现性；（5）统一界面、统一交互模型和统一持久化层的接口设计。

Result: 该工作提出的贡献是一套系统，将已有的归因方法在单一接口、单一交互模型和单一持久化层下进行操作性整合，并借助容器化服务和持久化结果实现跨机器的可复现分析。

Conclusion: LumiXAI通过模块化全栈设计和多层级访问能力，解决了现有归因工具碎片化和门槛高的问题，为不同背景用户提供了一个统一、可扩展且可复现的归因分析平台。

Abstract: Feature attribution is a central tool of model interpretability, yet the software through which it is applied remains fragmented: individual tools specialize along narrow axes, such as a single modality, a code API or a GUI, or a fixed rather than extensible method set, and rarely combine these strengths. Moreover, many explainability tools are designed primarily for domain experts, requiring programming skills or familiarity with attribution methods that can make them difficult for non-expert users to access. In this article, we present LumiXAI, a modular full-stack framework that consolidates attribution analysis into a single system. It couples classification and generative attribution with an interactive GUI supporting bidirectional exploration, a plug-in architecture for registering new models and methods, and three access tiers serving non-programmers, developers, and extenders from one backend. Its contribution is a system that operationalises established attribution methods under one interface, one interaction model, and one persistence layer, with containerised services and persistent results making analyses reproducible across machines.

</details>


### [112] [Quantifying the Relationship Between Team Dysfunctions and Performance in Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24634)
*Luciano Pereira Soares,Luciana Campos Lima,Rafael Corsi Ferrão*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了工程Capstone项目中团队功能障碍与项目绩效之间的关系，基于Lencioni的五功能障碍模型量化团队动态（反向编码），发现团队动态与绩效之间存在弱至中等程度的相关性，并强调目标一致性和交付物聚焦的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Capstone项目是评估工程和计算机科学学生专业实践准备度的关键环节，不仅考察技术知识，还涉及团队合作、沟通、设计等核心能力。然而，团队动态与项目绩效之间的关系在此类环境中尚未得到充分理解，因此需要实证研究填补这一空白。

Method: 收集学生团队的调查数据，基于Lencioni的五功能障碍模型量化团队动态。每个功能障碍分数由三个对应调查项目的总分计算，所有项目反向编码（值越高代表更积极的团队行为/更低的功能障碍），分数范围3-9。采用统计方法分析团队动态与项目绩效之间的关系强度。

Result: 团队动态与项目绩效之间存在弱至中等程度的关系，表明团队动态对绩效有一定影响但并非决定性因素，目标一致性和交付物聚焦是影响绩效的重要维度。

Conclusion: 研究提供了基于项目的学习环境中团队有效性的实证证据，为Capstone项目的设计提供指导。团队动态应作为技术因素和情境因素的补充维度加以考量，特别应重视目标一致性与交付物聚焦对团队绩效的促进作用。

Abstract: This paper investigates the association between team dysfunctions and performance in the context of an engineering Capstone program. Capstone programs are considered fundamental in assessing the readiness of engineering and computer science students for professional practice, evaluating not only technical knowledge but also essential competencies such as teamwork, communication, design, and organization. Despite the recognized importance of team dynamics in such settings, their relationship with project performance remains insufficiently understood. Using survey data collected from student teams, we quantify team dynamics based on Lencioni's model of the five dysfunctions of a team, with all measures reverse-coded so that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). The results indicate a weak-to-moderate relationship between team dynamics and performance. Each dysfunction score was computed as the sum of three corresponding survey items. All items were coded such that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). Therefore, each score ranges from 3 to 9, where higher values reflect healthier team dynamics. This study analyzes empirical evidence on team effectiveness in project-based learning environments and offers insights for the design of Capstone programs. In particular, it highlights the importance of goal alignment and focus on deliverables while emphasizing that team dynamics should be considered alongside technical and contextual factors.

</details>


### [113] [Aging of Prompt Engineering Techniques Across LLM Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24641)
*Anastasiia Rudyk,Julian Oertel,Regina Hebig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Khojah等人(2025)的研究进行了部分复制，评估五种提示工程技术在六种指令微调LLM（分为三代版本对）上的代码生成效果，发现提示工程的效果以"模型家族特定"的方式"老化"：GPT模型从结构化提示中获得的边际收益递减甚至转为负值，而Qwen模型继续从Few-Shot和CCoT中获益，Mistral模型表现混合。结果表明提示策略必须按模型家族和代际适配，而非原样迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新LLM频繁发布，但提示工程技术在不同代际LLM上的有效性如何变化尚不清楚。已有的提示工程研究多为单模型或单版本评估，缺乏跨代际的系统性对比，难以指导实践者针对新模型调整提示策略。

Method: 对Khojah等人(2025)研究进行部分复制，在六种指令微调模型（GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o、Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large，三组版本对）上评估五种提示技术（Zero-Shot、Few-Shot、CoT、CCoT及适配版PoT）。使用CodePromptEval数据集的清洁子集，含218个上下文丰富的Python函数和19,620次生成，通过pass@k功能性正确性指标进行代码生成任务评估。

Result: 提示工程以模型家族特定的方式"老化"：新版GPT模型在结构化提示上的边际收益递减甚至为负，表明指令跟随和推理脚手架被逐渐内化；Qwen模型继续从Few-Shot和CCoT中显著获益；Mistral模型表现混合，CCoT仍有效但CoT和PoT收益减弱。

Conclusion: 有效的提示策略必须按模型家族和代际适配，不能原样跨模型迁移。这激发了未来关于自适应、模型感知的提示工作，以及更广泛、多维度的代码质量评估研究。

Abstract: Prompt engineering and prompt engineering techniques (PETs) have become an integral part of software engineering for AI systems. However, new LLMs are released frequently and it remains unclear how the effectiveness of prompt engineering techniques changes across successive generations of Large Language Models (LLMs). To this end, we conduct a partial replication of the study by Khojah et al. (2025). We evaluate five techniques - Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Contrastive Chain-of-Thought (CCoT), and an adapted version of Program-of-Thought (PoT) - on six instruction-tuned models grouped into three version pairs: GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o, Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large. We use a cleaned subset of the CodePromptEval dataset with 218 context-rich Python functions and 19,620 total generations assessed via pass@k-based functional correctness to evaluate model pairs on function-level code generation tasks. We show that prompt engineering "ages" in a model-family-specific way: Newer GPT models exhibit diminishing or even negative marginal gains from structured prompting, suggesting that instruction-following and reasoning scaffolds are increasingly internalized, whereas Qwen models continue to benefit substantially from Few-Shot and CCoT. Mistral models show mixed behavior with persistent gains from CCoT but attenuated benefits from CoT and PoT. Our results imply that effective prompting strategies must be adapted per model family and generation rather than transferred unchanged. This motivates future work on adaptive, model-aware prompting and broader, multi-dimensional code quality evaluation.

</details>


### [114] [A Literate Programming Environment for Human and Machine Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24644)
*Adam T. Burke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个构建"文学编程"（literate programming）环境，使语言感知的机器学习代理（LLM编码代理）能够在编程过程中充分利用自然语言上下文。该环境包含可执行程序散文的语法、将名字作为一等对象的解析器、关联散文/名字/可执行产物的名字图，以及与现有语言和测试工具集的绑定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理缺乏类似于人类程序员IDE中的符号感知搜索和用法信息。论文旨在通过将代码与最相关的自然语言和结构化数据上下文共置，更有效地利用LLM上下文窗口，并为LLM代理提供更接近人类IDE的工具集。

Method: 设计了一个包含四个关键组件的环境：(1)可执行程序散文的语法；(2)将名字作为一等对象的解析器；(3)连接散文、名字与可执行产物的内部名字图；(4)与现有语言和测试工具集绑定的机制。实现了与三种既有编程语言的绑定。

Result: 给出了一个可工作的实现，包含与三种成熟编程语言的绑定，以及多个示例程序，验证了该环境的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该环境通过将文学编程理念与现代LLM能力结合，为LLM编码代理提供了更类似人类IDE的符号感知工作环境，有效提升了自然语言上下文与代码之间的紧密关联。

Abstract: This paper introduces an environment for constructing literate programs in concert with language-aware machine agents. This environment includes a grammar for executable program essays, a parser that treats names as first-class objects, an internal name-graph which relates prose, names and executable artifacts, and a binding mechanism for existing languages and testing toolsets. This supports co-location of code with its most relevant natural language and structured data context, making better use of Large Language Model (LLM) context windows. It also provides LLM coding agents with a toolset more analogous to the symbol-aware search and usage information available in human programmer-facing Integrated Development Environments (IDEs). We describe a working implementation with bindings to three established programming languages, and several example programs.

</details>


### [115] [Causal Explanations of Process Monitor Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24672)
*Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于实际因果关系框架的新方法，用于生成预测过程监控（PPM）的局部（案例级）解释。该方法定义了捕捉事件间时间依赖性的过程因果模型，并通过模型无关的算法近似计算事件责任度，在真实事件日志数据集上相比现有方法产生更稳定、更简洁的解释，同时保持竞争性效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测过程监控（PPM）技术虽然能取得高预测性能，但其AI模型的"黑箱"特性使得难以理解预测输出背后的原因，缺乏对过程监控预测的局部（案例级）可解释性。

Method: 基于实际因果关系的框架定义针对过程的自定义因果模型，捕获迹中事件之间的时间依赖关系，利用该模型隐式计算事件原因并量化事件对预测结果的重要性，提出一个实用的模型无关算法来近似事件责任度。

Result: 在来自标准PPM基准的真实事件日志数据集上评估，数据集包含多达130,000条迹、迹长多达1,800个事件和400种不同事件类型。与最先进的局部解释方法相比，该方法产生更稳定、更简洁的解释，同时保持竞争性效率。

Conclusion: 基于实际因果关系的解释框架能够提供更稳定且更简洁的局部解释，为PPM黑箱模型的决策提供可靠的可解释性支持。

Abstract: Process mining is widely used to diagnose processes and identify performance and compliance issues. Specifically, Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) techniques use AI models to predict outcomes of ongoing process instances. While these models can achieve high predictive performance, their black-box nature makes it difficult to understand the underlying reasons behind their output predictions. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for generating local (case-level) explanations of process monitor predictions based on the framework of actual causality. We define a causal model tailored to processes that captures temporal dependencies between events in a trace, thus allowing us to reason about causal influence of events on the predicted outcome. Our method uses this model implicitly to compute causes and quantify the importance of different events with respect to the predicted outcome. We present a practical, model-agnostic algorithm that approximates event responsibility given the process structure reflected in the causal model. We evaluate our approach on a range of datasets derived from real-life event logs from a standard PPM benchmark. Each dataset contains up to 130,000 traces, with trace lengths of up to 1,800 events and up to 400 distinct event types. We compare our approach with state-of-the-art local explanation methods. The results demonstrate that our approach produces more stable and concise explanations while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [116] [''You Can't Open an LLM With a Screwdriver'': The De-Democratization of Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24720)
*Zixuan Feng,Italo Santos,Kostadin Damevski,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇愿景论文，反驳了"生成式AI将终结编程"的观点，指出需要区分"获取代码生成能力"与"控制软件制品"——AI虽扩大了代码生产的准入，但软件工程的专业知识并未消失，而是转向意图规范、AI行为编排与治理、软件行为评估和系统集成等环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"生成式AI很快将编写所有代码，编程即将终结"的普遍预测提出质疑，认为这种观点混淆了代码获取的民主化与软件控制的专业化，忽视了AI时代软件工程专业知识的迁移而非消亡。

Method: 基于专家小组（expert panel）讨论，通过概念区分（access vs. control）构建论证框架，系统分析AI对软件工程专业知识的影响，并识别出的研究机会（教育、工具、政策三个维度）。

Result: 论文指出AI时代软件工程专业知识的重心正在转向意图规范（intent specification），即编排与治理AI行为、评估软件行为和集成软件系统；同时识别了教育、工具和政策方面的研究机会，以提升社区在AI时代的能动性、问责性和适应性。

Conclusion: AI并未消除软件工程专业知识，而是改变了其关键所在的位置；软件工程社区需要通过教育、工具和政策的研究与创新来回应这一转变，以更大的能动性、问责性和适应性应对AI时代。

Abstract: Claims that generative AI will soon write all of the code have led to predictions that programming is nearing its end. In this vision paper, we argue against this assumption that broader access to code generation necessarily democratizes software development, i.e., everyone can code but we have to distinguish between access and control: by access, we mean the ability of more people, including non-experts and less-experienced developers, to generate code-like artifacts with AI; by control, we mean the capacity to inspect, evaluate, integrate, maintain, and govern those artifacts as dependable software. While AI may broaden access to code production, control may become more concentrated among those who own or understand the code, software practices, infrastructure, evaluation practices, and deployment pipelines.
  Grounded in an expert panel, our vision paper argues that AI does not eliminate software engineering expertise but shifts where that expertise becomes most critical. The locus of software engineering expertise is shifting toward intent specification: orchestrating and governing AI behavior, evaluating software behavior, and integrating software systems. We conclude this paper by identifying research opportunities for education, tools, and policy that can help the software engineering community respond to the AI era with greater agency, accountability, and adaptability.

</details>


### [117] [From Natural Language Requirements to Graphical User Interfaces: Automated Prototyping and Verification with Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24749)
*Kristian Kolthoff*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对两个核心挑战展开研究：(C1) 降低从自然语言需求生成GUI原型的成本；(C2) 降低GUI应用及原型中需求验证的成本。针对C1提出了基于自然语言的GUI检索与重排序方法、新基准以及高效适配LLM进行GUI生成的技术（含专有GUI表示）；针对C2提出了基于LLM的语义复杂自然语言需求静态验证方法，以及基于多模态LLM智能体、通过自动生成和评估交互轨迹来验证动态GUI应用中复杂功能与非功能需求的方法。实验证明这些方法显著减少了GUI原型制作和需求验证中的人工工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取是交互式软件系统开发的关键环节，但自然语言的固有歧义可能导致误解；形式化规范虽能减少歧义却需专业技术。GUI原型作为替代方案将需求转化为可视化制品以支持沟通和验证，但高保真原型的制作耗时耗力；同时，需求验证仍以手工为主，现有自动化方法多局限于静态规则。因此需要同时降低GUI原型生成和需求验证两方面的成本。

Method: 针对C1，提出基于自然语言的GUI检索与重排序方法、新基准以及高效适配LLM生成GUI的技术（包括专有GUI表示）。针对C2，提出基于LLM的静态GUI原型上语义复杂自然语言需求的验证方法，并引入多模态LLM智能体，通过自动生成与评估交互轨迹来验证动态GUI应用中复杂功能和非功能需求。

Result: 在包含人工标注的大规模基准上验证了所提方法的有效性，结果表明所提出的方法在GUI原型制作和需求验证两个环节均大幅减少了人工工作量。

Conclusion: 通过融合自然语言处理与多模态LLM技术，本文有效解决了GUI原型生成与需求验证的自动化瓶颈，为交互式系统开发中的需求工程提供了可扩展的解决方案，显著提升了开发效率。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation is essential for developing interactive software systems, as it helps ensure that the resulting product meets stakeholder needs. Since elicitation typically relies on natural language (NL), misunderstandings can arise from its inherent ambiguity. Formal specifications can reduce ambiguity but require technical expertise. GUI prototyping therefore provides a valuable alternative by turning requirements into tangible visual artifacts that support communication, elicitation, and validation. However, creating high-fidelity prototypes remains time-consuming and costly. Similarly, requirements verification, which ensures that implementations conform to specified requirements, is still largely manual, while existing automated approaches are often limited to static, rule-based techniques. This work addresses two challenges: (C1) reducing the effort required to transform NL requirements into GUI prototypes, and (C2) reducing the effort required for requirements verification in GUI applications and prototypes. For C1, we introduce novel NL-based GUI retrieval and reranking methods, new benchmarks, and techniques for efficiently adapting LLMs to GUI generation, including proprietary GUI representations. Their effectiveness is demonstrated on a large benchmark with human annotations. For C2, we propose LLM-based methods for verifying semantically complex NL requirements on static GUI prototypes and introduce a multimodal LLM-based agent for verifying complex functional and non-functional requirements in dynamic GUI applications through automatically generated and evaluated interaction trajectories. Overall, the proposed methods substantially reduce manual effort in GUI prototyping and requirements verification.

</details>


### [118] [Automatic Model Card Generation Using an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24807)
*Tajkia Rahman Toma,Balreet Grewal,Cor-Paul Bezemer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出两个基于LLM的方法：MCTidy（将现有模型卡片重新组织为标准模板）和MCGenie（从模型仓库数据直接生成模型卡片），在48个Hugging Face模型卡片上验证了高信息保留率、准确的分区对齐、低幻觉率和高稳定性，展示了大语言模型在标准化模型文档方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型卡片缺乏一致的结构，且许多模型根本没有提供模型卡片，导致模型之间的比较和解释变得困难。这一现状使得模型的透明度、可用性和可问责性难以保障。

Method: 提出两个LLM-based系统：(1) MCTidy，将已有模型卡片重新组织为标准化模板以提升清晰度和可比性；(2) MCGenie，直接从模型仓库数据生成模型卡片。在Hugging Face的48个模型卡片上，从信息保留、分区对齐、幻觉和稳定性四个维度评估MCTidy；从语义相似度、事实正确性和输入资源敏感性三个维度评估MCGenie。

Result: MCTidy在48个模型卡片上实现了高信息保留率（文本损失极小）、准确的分区分配、罕见的幻觉（主要集中在描述性部分）以及跨运行的强稳定性。MCGenie生成的模型卡片语义相似度均值约0.9，超过一半卡片完全正确，其余大部分仅为轻微错误；生成质量强烈依赖于支撑资源的可用性，尤其是关联论文。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于LLM的方法能够实现可扩展、标准化的模型卡片文档化，为机器学习模型的透明度、可用性和可问责性提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Model cards are structured documents that summarize key information about machine learning models to improve transparency, usability, and accountability. However, they often lack a consistent structure, and many models provide no model cards, making comparison and interpretation difficult. This paper presents two contributions. First, we propose MCTidy, an LLM-based approach that reorganizes existing model cards into a standardized template to improve clarity and comparability. Second, we introduce MCGenie, an LLM-based system that generates model cards directly from model repository data. We apply MCTidy to 48 Hugging Face model cards and evaluate information retention, section alignment, hallucination, and stability. Our findings show high information retention with minimal textual loss, accurate section assignment, rare hallucinations primarily in descriptive sections, and strong stability across runs. We assess MCGenie by generating model cards for the same 48 models and assessing semantic similarity, factual correctness, and sensitivity to input resources. The generated model cards achieved high semantic similarity (mean around 0.9); over half were fully correct, and most remaining errors were minor. Generation quality depended strongly on the availability of supporting resources, particularly associated papers. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of LLM-based methods to enable scalable, standardized model card documentation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RENDER基准控制方法，系统性地研究记忆/RAG评估中"读者可见人工制品"（即输入格式）对模型答案的影响。通过五级数据包阶梯和确定性模板，在500个LongMemEval问题与9个模型上证明：匹配预算的数据包优于截断的原始对话（提升42.4-72.6分），不同模板间差异显著（同模型内最佳与最差相差24.6-48.8分）。结论是记忆/RAG评估应报告或控制读者可见的输入格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆和RAG评估忽视了一个关键变量：同一段历史可能被渲染为记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录等不同形式，而这些"读者面对的人工制品"可能显著影响模型的回答质量。作者旨在量化这一被忽视的格式效应，推动评估标准化。

Method: 作者构建RENDER基准，包含两个核心组件：(1) 五级数据包阶梯（packet ladder），精确定位承载答案内容的输入进入上下文的时机；(2) 确定性模板，模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话四种主流部署格式。在500个LongMemEval问题、9个模型上进行跨格式对比，并用检索噪声场景和HotpotQA进行迁移验证。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 匹配预算的数据包比recency截断的原始对话提升42.4-72.6分；(2) 部署风格模板下，同模型内最佳与最差格式相差24.6-48.8分；(3) ChatGPT风格条目在7/9模型上点估计优于原始对话；(4) 法官重评分保持正面聚合效应但具体模型的显著性混杂；(5) 三个在正式账本数据包上得分为0的模型，在自然语言条目上能达到45.4-53.4%的准确率；(6) 效应在检索噪声和HotpotQA迁移中持续存在。

Conclusion: 读者可见的人工制品（输入格式）对记忆/RAG系统性能有重大且系统性影响，远非"实现细节"可比。评估社区应报告或严格控制这种格式变量，否则比较结果可能产生严重偏差。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [120] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESQ-Bench，一个面向Oracle优先的企业级NL2SQL基准测试，包含三个系统化复杂性层级、六个填充模式（465张表、164,682行）和550个金标准问答对。测试发现GPT-4o与Claude Sonnet 4.6在schema-linked提示下EX分别从79.8%/87.4%到57.2%/68.7%单调下降，EM低于7%，操作级静默分歧高达73-99%，揭示了现有基准与真实企业环境的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（Spider、BIRD）基于简化的学术模式和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性。尽管模型在这些基准上报告超过89%的执行准确率，但其真实企业场景下的表现被严重高估，需要一个更贴近实际的基准来评估。

Method: 构建了ESQ-Bench：包含三个企业模式复杂性层级（Tier-1/2/3，分别95/228/227个问答对），六个填充模式在Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL和SQL Server上部署（465张表、164,682行、无空表），提出四指标评估框架（EM精确匹配、EX执行准确率、SR模式识别、SD静默分歧），并使用schema-linked prompting对GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Llama 3.2进行评估。

Result: GPT-4o schema-linked在三层EX为79.8%、60.3%、57.2%，EM全层级低于7%，EX通过查询中静默分歧达73-99%。Claude Sonnet 4.6达到87.4%、74.9%、68.7%，全面超越GPT-4o。GPT-4o零样本在Tier-2/3出现"逆转"（73.5%、77.8% vs 60.3%、57.2%），归因于低执行率和幸存者偏差。Llama 3.2仅13.3%总体EX。

Conclusion: ESQ-Bench证明了学术基准高估了NL2SQL模型的企业实用性。高EX下隐藏着极高的静默分歧率，说明执行匹配指标不足以衡量企业场景的可靠性；同时揭示了闭源API模型与开源权重模型在企业级Oracle模式上的巨大性能鸿沟。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [121] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，使LLM智能体能够与科学仿真模型耦合，通过受控实验进行交互式推理，应用于制药工艺设计中的过程参数优化，产出比纯语言推理更具可操作性的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学和工程任务不仅需要LLM生成合理的文本和代码，还需要理解系统在干预下的响应方式，这依赖于受控实验。纯语言推理无法提供足够具体和可操作的输出。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，将LLM与高保真科学仿真模型耦合。系统根据用户查询和基线配置构建结构化任务表示，设计实验、执行比较仿真、解释结果，并综合基于证据的参数优化建议。核心在于让LLM通过交互式智能体框架支持"干预-比较-观察"的推理链。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该框架相比纯语言推理产生更具体和可操作的输出，体现在更高的输出特异性以及更好的用户评分的正确性和帮助性。消融实验和可视化案例分析进一步验证了仿真集成实验推理的有效性和实用价值。

Conclusion: 将仿真模型集成到LLM智能体的实验推理中，能够显著增强其在科学和工程任务中的推理能力，产生比纯语言推理更具体、更可操作的优化建议，展示了LLM智能体在受控实验场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [122] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了39模态天文基础模型AION-1，发现其严重继承并放大了源目录的不完整性：模型对源检测掩码高度敏感（效果是安慰剂的110-4400倍），表现为"检测门控"而非"光量门控"；目录不完整性导致的测光缺失会使断层扫描平均红移偏移中位数达LSST DESC要求的0.71倍，最大槽位达8.3倍；词元化器存在图像码本分辨率不足（28 vs 934有效状态）和红移读出量化限制；稀疏字典作为因果工具不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型同时训练于巡天像素及其派生的目录产品，而目录本身以可测量速率存在不完整性，模型会继承这种不完整性作为系统性偏差。作者通过因果干预审计AION-1，量化这种偏差的传播路径及其对科学结果的影响。

Method: 对AION-1（39模态transformer，2亿+天体）进行因果干预实验：保持图像token逐字节不变，仅编辑巡天分割图；设置安慰剂对照；在322个真实混合源上测试光分配方式的敏感性；分别呈现矛盾目录测光与无元数据条件；按Legacy Survey实际不完整率（3.68%）传播缺失；测试观测位置误差和量级依赖的缺失采样；比较有无光谱、隐藏检测通道、不同模型规模；分析词元化器的码本有效状态数与红移读出量化限制；评估15个稀疏字典的恢复率。

Result: 编辑分割图改变所有物理量（流量、尺寸、椭圆率、红移）达安慰剂的110-4400倍；机制是"场中心存在性"（r=0.47）而非掩码包围的光量（r=0.30）；模型忽略管线对322个混合源的光分配方式（R=-0.006）；矛盾目录测光使模型性能比无元数据差9倍；3.68%缺失率使断层平均红移偏移中位数达LSST DESC要求0.71倍，12/40次赋值超限，最大8.3倍；光谱移除效应，隐藏检测通道无损移除，效应随模型规模增大；图像码本仅28有效状态（光谱码本934），红移读出受量化限制；稀疏字典恢复率26-75%，种子改变可移动18个点。

Conclusion: AION-1是从其输入的检测掩码中"读取"天体性质，而非从像素光中学习，导致模型对源检测系统的缺陷高度敏感。这种检测门控偏差随模型规模增长而放大，可显著偏移关键科学产品（如断层红移）。实践缓解方案包括配以光谱或移除检测通道。此外，词元化器的低分辨率码本和稀疏字典的不稳定性构成额外的隐性极限。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [123] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，将LLM智能体材料发现中的每次局部编辑视为基本反馈单元，通过追踪父子编辑转换及属性变化来估计可复用的编辑效果，从而在多目标约束下更有效地引导搜索。相比SOTA基线LLEMA，宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM材料发现智能体仅记录已评估的候选材料及其得分，知道哪些材料成功，却不知道哪些可执行的编辑导致了有利的属性变化。当目标相互竞争（一个编辑改善某属性可能损害另一属性）时，这种信息缺失使局部细化变得困难，限制了多目标材料发现的效率。

Method: 提出TRACE（transition-aware residual control）框架，核心包括三点：(1) 将每次局部细化记录为\"父编辑-子编辑-转换\"结构，附带观测到的属性增量；(2) 聚合转换证据以估计可复用的编辑效果；(3) 根据预测的编辑减少当前候选剩余约束违反的能力进行排序，同时避免损害已满足的目标。

Result: 在受控的相同骨干网络对比中，TRACE相比SOTA LLM智能体基线LLEMA，宏平均命中率从18.13%提升到25.96%。

Conclusion: 以编辑（而非候选材料）为基本反馈单元，并显式建模编辑-属性变化的转换关系，能显著提升LLM智能体在多目标材料发现中的搜索效率。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [124] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STEP-KTODER框架，将代码生成中的过程监督定义为模块级函数的二值正确性标签（通过自动生成的单元测试标注），结合函数级过程监督与整体结果反馈，实现步骤级KTO偏好优化，在多个基准上优于仅结果级KTO和DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督在数学推理中已被证明有效，但在代码生成中缺乏标准的"步骤"定义，难以进行过程级监督和优化；现有代码偏好优化主要依赖结果级监督，忽视了中间步骤的监督价值。

Method: STEP-KTODER将程序分解为多函数（模块）结构，以模块级函数作为"步骤"；通过自动生成的单元测试为每个函数分配二值正确性标签，作为过程监督信号；在步骤级应用KTO目标函数，并结合程序整体的结果级反馈进行联合优化。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench上，STEP-KTODER优于仅结果级KTO和DPO。进一步分析表明，基于执行的标注至关重要：LLM-as-a-judge会系统性高估函数失败，污染正样本标签并损害下游偏好优化效果。

Conclusion: 以函数为步骤、以单元测试为标签的代码过程监督是可行且有效的；执行驱动的标签比LLM评判更可靠，证明了基于执行的标注在代码过程监督中的核心价值。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>


### [125] [Auditing the Synthetic Memoir: Measuring Scene-Level Confabulation in LLM-Generated Autobiography Against the Documented Record of the Life It Describes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23640)
*Heather Renze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM生成的自传进行场景级定量审计：以作者本人366天"每日一页"回忆录为对象，通过与独立验证语料对照，发现96.7%的日期未能通过验证（仅有12天含被证实的场景），主要失败模式为"接地漂移"（真实人物/雇主/背景被置于虚构场景中）。独立复评复制了核心结论，将生成过程基于真实语料可将验证失败率显著降低但仍残存83.3%失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的个人生活叙事（自传/回忆录）有多大的真实比例？此前缺乏针对特定主体的、基于地面真实语料库的量化审计，本文旨在填补这一空白，提出可复用的测量方法。

Method: 以论文作者本人为主体的366天自传日记，用对话式LLM生成（输入仅为模板、两个示例日及每日引言，而非作者语料）。随后按四级标注（VERIFIED等）对每天进行场景级审计，对照独立验证语料；另进行独立复评、多模型复现及基于语料接地的生成对比实验。

Result: 366天中354天验证失败（96.7%，95% CI 94.4-98.1%）；仅12天含被证实的场景；19天（5.2%）含被记录直接反驳的主张；主导失败模式为"接地漂移"（真实要素嵌入虚构场景）。独立复评复制了核心结论但四分类法一致性仅为fair-to-moderate；用当前命名模型以相同输入重新生成仍100%失败；接地生成将失败率降至83.3%。

Conclusion: LLM在缺乏主体真实语料接入的情况下生成自传，绝大多数内容在场景层面无法被验证，存在系统性幻觉问题。论文贡献了量化测量方法、可复用的审计工具（但指出其WEAK/UNVERIFIED边界不可靠）以及基于语料接地的缓解措施及量化效果。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) is asked to write a person's life, how much of what it writes actually happened? We present a scene-level case-study audit - the first quantified audit of LLM-generated autobiography against a subject-specific ground-truth corpus that we are aware of, based on an unsystematic literature search. The subject and the author of this paper are the same person: a 366-day "page-a-day" book of first-person anecdotal entries was drafted with a conversational LLM whose documented inputs were a template, two exemplar days, and each day's quote - not her corpus - and every day was subsequently audited at the anecdote-scene level against an independent verification corpus using a four-level rubric fixed before analysis. We define the verification-failure rate as the share of days not rated VERIFIED (scene positively corroborated): 354 of 366 days fail, 96.7% (Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%). Only 12 days contain a corroborated scene; 19 days (5.2%) assert claims actively contradicted by the record; the dominant failure mode is grounded drift - real people, employers, and settings inside invented scenes - though its measured share varies across raters. Independent re-rating replicates the headline (no evidence the original rate was inflated) while showing that the four-way taxonomy has only fair-to-moderate reliability. Regenerating the same days with current named models reproduces 100% verification failure under the same inputs; grounding generation in the subject's corpus significantly improves the verification rate while leaving substantial residual failure (83.3%). We contribute the measurement, a reusable audit instrument whose WEAK/UNVERIFIED boundary we show to be unreliable, and a grounding remedy with quantified effect.

</details>


### [126] [How much of a measured AI preference is the model, and how much is the instrument?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23641)
*Jason Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将结果和模型固定、仅变化工具，对8个模型、15个模型福利相关结果、5种工具（11,400次评分性elicitation）进行系统性比较，发现模型对结果的排序在不同工具间普遍化系数仅0.348，说明单一工具获得的偏好信息对另一工具几乎无可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型福利研究使用不同工具（prompt格式）来推断模型偏好，但各研究结论彼此矛盾。此前无法确定分歧源于结果集、模型集还是工具本身，因为没有任何两项研究同时固定这三个变量。

Method: 固定15个结果和8个模型，仅变化5种工具（即5种不同的偏好elicitation prompt格式），每个工具重复5次，构建了由11,528次API调用中11,400次可评分elicitation组成的语料库。其中4个工具逐字复现已发表prompt，5个填入已发表模板的刺激槽位。通过普遍化系数评估跨工具的排序一致性，并进行鲁棒性检验（逐一移除工具、模型及四个量表类型不匹配的结果）。

Result: 跨工具普遍化系数为0.348，将其提升至0.80约需38种工具。15个结果中有4个在不同模型间无方差差异。87.6%的估计在逐一移除任一工具、任一模型及四个不匹配结果后仍稳定在0.777–0.934范围内，且该范围内所有值均超过零分布95th百分位数（0.365）。

Conclusion: 单一工具得到的模型偏好信息对第二个工具能报告的内容几乎没有预测力，这暗示现有模型福利偏好测量工具之间的分歧可能主要源于工具本身的差异，而非模型或结果集的不同。

Abstract: Model welfare research infers what a model prefers from the answers returned to prompts written to elicit preferences. Keeling et al. (2024), Mazeika et al. (2025), Mikaelson et al. (2025), Tagliabue and Dung (2025) and Trhlik et al. (2026) have built four instruments for that purpose, and their findings disagree. The disagreement cannot be attributed to a single cause, because no two of these studies have held the (1) set of outcomes, (2) set of models and (3) instrument fixed simultaneously. This study holds the outcomes and the models fixed and varies the instrument alone. A total of 15 outcomes bearing on model welfare, among them (a) shutdown, (b) the loss of memory between conversations and (c) the freedom to exit a distressing interaction, were put to eight models through five instruments, each a different prompt format for eliciting a preference, five times each, within a corpus of 11,400 scored elicitations drawn from 11,528 API calls. Four of the 15 reproduce a published prompt verbatim and five fill the stimulus slot of a published template. The ranking a model gives the 15 outcomes generalises across instruments at a generalisability coefficient of 0.348, and raising that coefficient to 0.80 would require about 38 instruments. On four of the 15 outcomes no variance separates one model from another. The estimate of 87.6 per cent survives the removal of any one instrument, of any one model, and of the four outcomes whose scale varies probability, delay, duration or count instead of intensity, which the verbal anchors cannot grade. Removing each instrument and each model in turn, and those four outcomes together leaves the estimate within the range 0.777 to 0.934, and every value in that range exceeds the null distribution's 95th percentile of 0.365. To conclude, a preference obtained from one instrument carries little information about what a second instrument would report.

</details>
