  While controlled generation (e.g., PPLM, GeDi, DExperts, FUDGE) and text-watermarking systems (e.g., SynthID-Text) demonstrate the technical maturity of decoding- and logit-level interventions, the governance, security, and economic implications of an undisclosed inference policy remain comparatively underexplored. This paper examines the emergence of inference-time framing bias: the systematic modification of generated language toward political, ideological, institutional, or commercial frames via interventions applied after model inference but before token sampling.
  We formalize the operational reality Model != Deployed System and introduce three concepts: (1) the Inference Attribution Problem, characterizing why observed behavioral bias cannot generally be causally attributed to model weights alone under limited observability; (2) Probability Placement, defining a hypothetical advertising primitive in which commercial influence is implemented through systematic shifts in generation probabilities rather than explicit product insertions; and (3) Inference Policy Transparency, a governance principle for making deployment-layer interventions auditable. We examine these concepts in relation to Article 5 of the EU AI Act, the EU Digital Services Act, and FTC doctrines.

</details>


### [224] [Meta$^n$: Recursive Self-Improvement through Emergent Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24735)
*Zae Myung Kim,Young-Jun Lee,Seungyeon Jwa,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta^n，一种通过固定元操作Ω并对其输入递归应用来突破LLM智能体自我改进深度限制的方法，在8个基准家族上超越现有方法，尤其在ARC-AGI-2上取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进LLM智能体的局限在于：它们改进答案而非生成答案的过程；添加元层时该层本身固定不变；系统自编辑时必须保留部分机制不变以保证稳定，这使得实际可实现的元深度约为两层。作者希望突破这一局限，实现可收敛的更深层自我改进。

Method: Meta^n的核心设计为：(1) 固定元操作Ω，对Ω的输出递归应用而非改变Ω本身，避免系统不稳定性；(2) Ω读取下层求解器栈的轨迹及其生成代码，然后输出上一层作为策略预处理和可调用辅助函数库；(3) 由于输入严格增长，每层都能从更高视角推理；(4) 深度由收敛性决定而非预设；(5) 使用进化式存档搜索层链组合。

Result: 在两种主干网络上，Meta^n在所有八个基准家族上都优于先前的自我改进智能体。最突出的是ARC-AGI-2（专门设计以抵抗技能记忆化的基准），Meta^n是唯一得分高于零的方法。消融实验表明，递归带来的大部分增益来自每层传递给下一层的条件信息；尽管没有提示预设各层的角色，但随着深度增加，各层角色出现分化。

Conclusion: Meta^n通过固定元操作并递归其输入的方式，成功实现了超过两层的元深度，使自我改进的LLM智能体能够从递增的认知高度逐层推理，显著提升性能并展现出超越预设提示的自然分层行为，为深层次自我改进智能体提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents refine answers, not the process that produces those answers. Systems that add a meta-level hold that level fixed, and those that edit themselves must leave part of their own editing machinery untouched to stay stable, capping the meta-depth they realize at roughly two. We present Meta$^n$, which keeps the meta-operation fixed and recurses on its input instead. That operation, $Ω$, is applied repeatedly to its own products, reading the traces of the solver stack below together with the code that produced them, then writing the next layer as a strategic pre-process and a library of callable helpers. Because $Ω$ never changes, it cannot destabilize the system, and because its input strictly grows, each layer reasons from a higher vantage than the last. Depth is set by convergence rather than fixed in advance, and an evolutionary archive searches over layer chains. Across two backbones, Meta$^n$ outperforms prior self-improving agents on all eight benchmark families. The sharpest case is ARC-AGI-2, built to resist skill memorization, where it alone scores above zero. Ablations indicate that most of the gain from recursion comes from the conditioning each layer passes to the next, and distinct layer roles emerge with depth although no prompt prescribes them. Code available at https://github.com/minnesotanlp/meta-n

</details>


### [225] [RACE: Scalable Statistical Estimation of Functional Consistency in LLM Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24758)
*Runyu Wang,Bo Liu,Xiaxin Zhang,Yu Han,Jiawei Cao,Xiaoye Zhang,Zhe Zhang,Yifan Yang,Peng Ping*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RACE是一种前向传播统计框架，用于评估Transformer神经元在整个域上的功能一致性，相比梯度法具有更高的域特异性和两个数量级的计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法依赖实例级点估计或高计算成本程序，无法揭示群体层面的可变性，也难以进行可扩展的全域分析，因此需要一种高效且能捕捉域级稳定神经元行为的框架。

Method: RACE (Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation) 是一种基于前向传播的统计框架，通过残差对齐来估计Transformer神经元在域范围内的功能一致性，无需梯度计算。

Result: 扰动实验表明RACE相比基于梯度的点估计方法具有更优的域特异性；token分布级结果验证了所选神经元与目标域的关联；计算开销比梯度法低两个数量级。

Conclusion: RACE为全域范围内的神经元功能一致性分析提供了高效且具有良好域特异性的解决方案，显著降低了计算成本，适用于大规模机制可解释性分析。

Abstract: Discovering stable neuron behavior across entire domains remains a challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing methods often rely on instance-level point estimates or computationally expensive procedures, which either obscure population-level variability or limit scalable domain-wide analysis. We present RACE (Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation), a forward-pass statistical framework that evaluates the domain-wide functional consistency of Transformer neurons. Perturbation experiments demonstrate that RACE achieves superior domain specificity compared to gradient-based point estimates. Meanwhile, token-distribution-level results verify the association between the selected neurons and the target domain. Furthermore, its computational overhead is two orders of magnitude lower than that of gradient-based methods.

</details>


### [226] [Evidence Blindness in Direct Corpus Interaction: Persistent Navigation with AtlasNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24764)
*Hongyu Guo,Zhiyu Zheng,Zhao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AtlasNav框架，将大规模智能体搜索形式化为对可复用语料库结构的有限预算导航。不同于动态工作区方法需要从每个查询和轨迹中重建条件交互空间，AtlasNav一次性将语料库组织成Corpus Atlas（多视图持久化结构），允许每个查询自适应导航而非重构共享结构。在BrowseComp-Plus上达到92.05%的严格准确率，在线推理成本相对降低30.21%，并提出了"证据盲区"（Evidence Blindness）这一新概念来描述交互预算有限时证据的逐阶段丢失现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型智能体从传统的检索增强生成转向直接与外部语料库交互（DCI），但直接交互在有限交互预算下存在严重的证据不可用问题：需要的证据可能无法浮现、浮现的支持文档可能未被打开、打开的文档可能未能暴露关键片段，导致证据逐步无声丢失（称为证据盲区）。此外，原始的DCI交互几乎不增加可复用的语料库组织，动态工作区方法虽然重建查询条件的交互空间，但每次都需要在线重建，浪费大量计算资源。作者旨在利用可复用的语料库结构来克服这些限制。

Method: AtlasNav采用持久化的多视图语料库导航框架：1) 一次性将语料库组织成Corpus Atlas（包含多个视图的持久化表示）；2) 每个查询基于Atlas自适应导航，而无需在线重建共享结构；3) 保留与语料库的直接交互能力，但将大规模智能体搜索形式化为有限预算下对可复用结构的导航问题；4) 用阶段式证据实现（stage-wise evidence realization）来量化证据盲区。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus基准上，AtlasNav达到92.05%的严格准确率，相较于之前的动态工作区最优方法将记录的在线推理成本降低了30.21%。在匹配的预算下，它更早地实现了完整必要证据的呈现，并且更快速地逼近同模型下证据供给的经验参考上限。该表示原则在PhantomWiki的不同语料库组织和10K-1M规模控制下依然有效，并能竞争性地迁移到异构企业知识场景。

Conclusion: 智能体搜索的效果不仅取决于证据的"可及性"（accessible evidence），还取决于语料库的表示方式——即如何将语料库组织起来，使得有限的交互预算变成有效的导航。可复用的语料库结构表示（Corpus Atlas）相比每次在线重建交互空间，在保证高准确率的同时显著降低了推理成本，并在多种语料库场景下均展现鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond conventional retrieval-augmented generation toward direct interaction with external corpora. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) keeps the full corpus accessible, yet reachable evidence can remain unusable under finite interaction budgets. Required evidence may fail to surface, a surfaced supporting document may remain unopened, or an opened document may fail to expose its decisive fragment. We call this progressive silent loss Evidence Blindness and quantify it through stage-wise evidence realization. Within the DCI paradigm, raw interaction adds little reusable corpus organization, while dynamic-workspace methods reconstruct a query-conditioned interaction space from each query and trajectory. In both cases, useful structure is recovered largely online. We instead formulate large-scale agentic search as finite-budget navigation over reusable corpus structure. We introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that retains direct corpus interaction but organizes the corpus once into a Corpus Atlas, allowing each query to navigate adaptively rather than reconstruct shared structure. On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav achieves 92.05% strict accuracy while reducing recorded online inference cost by 30.21% relative to the prior dynamic-workspace state of the art. Under matched budgets, it realizes the complete required evidence earlier and approaches the same model's evidence-supplied empirical reference more rapidly. The same representation principle remains effective under PhantomWiki's distinct corpus organization and controlled 10K-1M scaling, and transfers competitively to heterogeneous enterprise knowledge. These results show that agentic search depends not only on accessible evidence, but also on how the corpus is represented so that limited interaction becomes effective navigation.

</details>


### [227] [StepGuard: Learning Step-Level Guardrails with Scalable Supervision and Safety-Utility Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24777)
*Zhijie Zheng,Yu Li,Chen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Yanwei Fu,Lu Sheng,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StepGuard，一个步骤级(step-level)的智能体护栏模型，可在工具动作执行前进行审计，并配套StepGen自动数据引擎和Balance-GRPO平衡训练策略，在保证低效用损失(2.8%)的同时将平均攻击成功率降低77.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过工具调用与外部环境交互，但引入了文件修改、信息泄露和未授权操作等安全风险。现有护栏方法通常在完整轨迹完成后进行评估，缺乏对步骤级动作执行前的实时监控，因此需要一种能在动作执行前进行审计的护栏模型。

Method: 1) StepGuard：步骤级护栏模型，既能审计已完成轨迹，也能在执行前检查工具动作；2) StepGen：自动数据引擎，生成具有相同上下文但在风险步骤上动作不同的安全/不安全轨迹对；3) Balance-GRPO：动态平衡安全与不安全动作学习的强化学习训练策略，基于两者观测到的准确率调整学习权重，以减少过度防御和防御不足。

Result: StepGuard在开放权重护栏模型中取得最高平均准确率，性能与GPT-5.4相当。在AgentDojo和AgentDyn上用于护栏时，相对无护栏设置，平均攻击成功率降低77.3%，平均效用仅下降2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 步骤级预执行监控的护栏方案是可行且高效的，通过合理的数据生成与平衡训练策略，能够在保障安全性的同时大幅降低对智能体正常任务执行效用的影响，实现了安全与效用之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can interact with external environments through tool invocation, but this capability also introduces security risks such as file modification, information leakage, and unauthorized actions. Existing guardrails often evaluate completed trajectories, leaving pre-execution monitoring of step-level actions underexplored. We propose StepGuard, a step-level guard model that can audit completed agent trajectories and check tool actions before they are executed. To train StepGuard, we introduce StepGen, an automatic data engine that generates safe and unsafe trajectories with the same context but different actions at the risky step. To further reduce over-defense and under-defense, we propose Balance-GRPO, which dynamically balances learning between safe and unsafe actions based on their observed accuracy. Experiments show that StepGuard achieves the highest average accuracy among open-weight guard models, with performance comparable to GPT-5.4. When used to guard agents on AgentDojo and AgentDyn, StepGuard reduces mean attack success rate by 77.3% relative to the no-guard setting, while mean utility drops by only 2.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [228] [Right Diagnoses, Decorative Reasoning:A Perturbation Audit of Medical Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24790)
*Mengzhu Xu,Jifan Gao,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Xi Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过医学扰动审计框架发现，LLM在医学推理中的思维链（CoT）理性文本与实际答案之间存在高度解耦——链条并未有效承载推理，仅起文档性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生将CoT理性视为医学推理的证据，但这一假设很少被检验。通用领域的CoT忠实性检测忽略临床成本，而医学LLM评估则将链条视为黑箱。作者旨在弥合这一空白。

Method: 提出医学扰动审计框架：包含30种操作符的电池，对链条和问题施加临床驱动的扰动（严重性反转、否定翻转、人口属性交换、证据删减），并结合链条更新与答案翻转的联合分析来对模型故障模式进行分类。在14个LLM、4个医学QA基准上测试，并由两位执业临床医生重新标注197个扰动问题。

Result: 三个独立测试收敛一致：链条解耦率（CDR）在全面板上达到72.9%（临床意义的破坏性编辑下链条不响应且答案不翻转）；链条被破坏后准确率保持不变；移除CoT提示也不降低准确率。关闭模型层级的答案侧信号与解耦现象一致。临床医生重新标注的197个问题中98.5%的金标准仍然站得住脚。

Conclusion: 医学CoT在大多数模型上是"文档性"而非"忠实性"的——表现为与答案解耦的装饰性文本，而非真正的推理依据。作者提出的框架和CDR度量可作为审计医学CoT是否忠实的通用标杆。

Abstract: Clinicians read chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales as evidence of medical reasoning, but whether the visible chain plays that role is rarely tested. General-domain CoT-faithfulness probes ignore clinical cost, and medical LLM evaluations treat the chain as a black box. We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [229] [CAFE: Self-Improving Search Agents Need Co-Evolving Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24794)
*Boyang Liu,Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Yibo Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Xinbing Liang,Shizheng Zhu,Yuhui Wang,Jingqi Tong,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Clive Bai,Clarenceai,Blaze Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CAFE框架，通过共享参数模型在搜索代理与反馈评论者角色之间交替，使用在线RL（比较反馈估计与反馈感知优势塑造）与离线偏好优化耦合，使反馈与策略协同进化，在七个智能体搜索基准上超越现有RL搜索代理并降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果监督的搜索代理虽然能学习何时检索证据，但终端奖励无法定位中间错误，也无法在错误累积前引导轨迹。将纠正性反馈作为轨迹内干预需要同时解决两个耦合问题：代理需决定何时请求和使用反馈，评论者需从结果混杂且失败模式随代理改进而变化的轨迹中推断有用纠正。

Method: CAFE框架的核心是共享参数模型交替扮演搜索代理与评论者两种角色。具体包括：(1) 基于基础代理自身失败轨迹初始化反馈条件恢复；(2) 在线RL中使用提示级调用-跳过成功差距形成比较反馈估计以塑造请求回报，并通过反馈感知优势塑造重加权反馈前后的token优势；(3) 离线阶段从匹配的成功/失败轨迹对中通过偏好优化学习反馈。

Result: 在七个智能体搜索基准上平均优于现有RL搜索代理，在全部六个域外基准上保持性能增益，并减少答案级幻觉。单向消融显示只改进代理或只改进评论者最终都会平台期，而交替更新两种角色则持续提升。

Conclusion: 自改进搜索代理需要与其所引导的策略协同进化的反馈。代理与评论者的耦合交替优化是实现持续性能提升的关键，反馈必须随着策略改进而变化而非静态固定。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised search agents learn when and how to retrieve evidence, but terminal rewards neither localize intermediate errors nor redirect an ongoing trajectory before those errors compound. Treating corrective feedback as a learned in-trajectory intervention couples the two roles: the agent must decide when to request and use feedback, while the critic must infer useful corrections from outcome-confounded rollouts whose failure patterns shift as the agent improves. We introduce CAFE (Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution), a framework in which a shared-parameter model alternates between search-agent and critic roles. CAFE initializes feedback-conditioned recovery from trajectories built around the base agent's own failures, then couples online and offline optimization. During online RL, a comparative feedback estimate uses a prompt-level call--skip success gap to shape request returns, while feedback-aware advantage shaping reweights token advantages before and after feedback. Offline, rollout-derived preference optimization learns feedback from matched successful and unsuccessful trajectories. On seven agentic search benchmarks, CAFE outperforms the evaluated RL-based search agents on average, retains its gains across all six out-of-domain benchmarks, and reduces answer-level hallucinations. One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [230] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格因果的流式视频异常检测器。它基于对角线性状态空间递归与输入/状态依赖的衰减门，以O(1)时间/内存逐帧更新固定大小的状态，无需剪辑缓冲或前视。作者从理论上推导了递归衰减谱与检测延迟及可捕获最短异常之间的闭合关系，并在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上验证。实际测量显示在Apple M3 Pro上延迟仅0.74ms/帧（超1300 FPS）。方法在帧级AUC上达到Ped2 67.9%与Avenue 70.2%，但落后于非因果SSM基线，门控贡献呈数据集规模依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba SSM的视频异常检测方法存在三方面不足：（1）内部仍依赖剪辑或窗口缓冲，并非真正的流式处理；（2）缺乏从理论上解释时序记忆与检测延迟关系的框架；（3）基准评测仅在GPU吞吐上进行，而非这些方法实际要部署的边缘硬件上实测。作者旨在填补这些空白，提出一种真正严格因果的流式检测器，并给出理论到实证的完整对应。

Method: 方法核心是一个严格因果流式架构：固定大小状态、无前视、无剪辑缓冲，每帧以O(1)时间与内存更新。时序核心为带输入和状态依赖衰减门的对角线性状态空间递归，在冻结视觉主干上通过因果下一嵌入预测进行自监督训练。作者推导了衰减递归谱与检测延迟及最短可靠捕获异常之间的闭合理论关系，并设计了针对性的消融实验（衰减率、状态大小、门控）来验证理论预测。

Result: 理论推导的基于学习到的基衰减的稳定延迟界（57–59帧）远高于实测检测延迟（Ped2 1.6帧、Avenue 18.4帧），证明事件边界门而非基衰减主导响应速度。在Apple M3 Pro上实测端到端延迟为0.74ms和0.77ms每帧（超1300 FPS）。未调优初始配置下帧级AUC为Ped2 67.9%、Avenue 70.2%，落后于非因果SSM基线。消融表明门控贡献呈数据集规模依赖性——在小规模Ped2训练集上损害精度，在大规模Avenue上提升精度。

Conclusion: 本文证明了严格因果的流式SSM检测器在边缘硬件上可实现极低延迟（<1ms/帧），且理论上的衰减谱-延迟关系能有效指导系统理解，但实际响应由事件边界门主导。当前精度仍落后于非因果SSM基线，门控行为取决于训练集规模。下一步工作聚焦于弥合精度差距以及在大规模第三个基准上进行扩展评估。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [231] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CES-PK框架，通过轻量级符号约束而非完整语义解析来验证LLM生成的候选答案，提升KGQA的精确率同时保持召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在知识图谱问答中常无法正确将答案锚定于底层图结构。现有方法要么依赖完整的语义解析（如SPARQL），这在真实世界知识图谱中因模式复杂或不完整而脆弱；要么依赖LLM推理直接生成答案，虽更鲁棒但缺乏形式保证。需要一个介于两者之间的补充方案。

Method: 提出Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)问题形式化，利用从问题中提取的轻量级符号约束（类型约束、关系约束、排除约束）对LLM生成的候选答案进行验证。采用三值约束语义（满足/违反/未知）以适配不完整知识图谱的开世界假设，避免错误拒绝有效答案。

Result: 在Hetionet生物医学知识图谱上的实验表明：通过过滤无效候选答案提升精确率；由于保留未被明确违反约束的候选，召回率得以保持；满足的约束还能作为正向符号证据，用于对剩余候选进行排序。

Conclusion: 该方法在完全语义解析和纯LLM推理之间提供了一条折中且有效的路径，能以轻量级符号约束提供形式保证，同时兼顾真实知识图谱的不完整性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [232] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的双维度自动题目相似性分析（AISA）框架，通过结构化分解和语义关联性来量化题目间的冗余，并在计算机化自适应测试（CAT）中验证其优于传统文本相似度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模测评的扩展和自动题目生成的普及，导致题目中存在意外的内容冗余（如措辞或情境框架等构造无关元素的重复）。传统相似度指标（如BLEU、余弦相似度）无法同时捕捉结构性和语义层面的细微冗余，需要更强大的分析方法。

Method: 提出双维度AISA框架，利用LLM从"结构化分解"和"语义关联性"两个维度对题目相似性进行可操作化度量。通过心理计量学验证评估LLM指标与传统文本指标的表现，并在CAT模拟中引入LLM相似性约束优化选题策略。

Result: 心理计量学验证表明LLM指标与构造无关的局部依赖指标更一致，能产生更连贯的题目参数分组；CAT模拟显示基于LLM相似性约束的选题策略在最小化效率损失的同时改善了估计稳定性并降低了偏差，优于传统指标约束。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AISA框架有望支持可扩展的题库策展、内容感知的组卷设计和体验敏感的适应性测试，适用于多样化的测评场景。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [233] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedV-KGQA框架，用于在垂直划分的知识图谱场景下进行多跳推理问答。组织间共享实体但持有不相交的关系集合，通过本地图增强和知识图谱嵌入实现原始三元组和关系参数不出站的数据边界，并引入主题实体锚定机制在无需运行时跨站通信的情况下将问题锚定到正确的图邻域。在三个基准上的12种模型配置评估表明，该框架性能接近集中式方法，可泛化到3跳推理，且对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的知识图谱问答数据常因治理和数据主权约束而分布在不同组织之间，现有集中式系统无法回答所需事实分散在垂直划分数据孤岛上的多跳问题。因此需要一种在不泄露原始数据的前提下，支持跨组织多跳推理的联邦框架。

Method: FedV-KGQA结合本地图增强（local graph enrichment）和知识图谱嵌入（KG embeddings）来建立结构数据边界，确保原始三元组和关系参数不出站；提出主题实体锚定（topic entity anchoring）机制，在无需运行时跨站点通信的情况下将问题定位到正确的图邻域；组织间共享实体但持有互斥的关系集合。

Result: 在三个基准上评估了12种模型配置，结果显示FedV-KGQA表现强劲，性能接近集中式方法，能够泛化到3跳推理场景，并且对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA在垂直划分的知识图谱联邦场景下实现了无需中央图访问和运行时跨站通信的多跳问答，兼顾了隐私保护和推理能力，验证了该框架在分布式KGQA任务中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [234] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPO++方法，通过修正单流策略优化（SPO）中轨迹级白化与token级actor损失之间的不匹配，采用动作token测度下的终端结果优势标准化，并基于策略事件而非接收顺序组织提示证据，在ALFWorld和Math-TIR基准上显著提升在线学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习需要同一提示的兄弟轨迹进行对比，对于长且可变的工具使用轨迹成本高昂。SPO虽然通过持续提示级价值估计消除该依赖，但其轨迹级白化处理与actor优化的token级加权目标之间不一致，导致优化方向存在偏差。

Method: SPO++包含两个关键改进：(1) 在动作token测度下对终端结果优势进行标准化，修正轨迹级白化无法居中token加权目标的问题；(2) 按策略事件（policy event）而非学习者接收顺序来组织提示证据，以更合理地利用提示信息。

Result: 在两种模型规模的ALFWorld及Math-TIR任务的匹配运行实验中，SPO++相比SPO在在线学习效率上均有提升。配对消融实验表明，动作token测度下的标准化是最强有效的组件。

Conclusion: SPO++通过修正优势标准化与提示组织方式，在不依赖兄弟rollout的前提下进一步提升了单流策略优化的在线学习效率，为长时间可变工具使用轨迹的强化学习提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


### [235] [Recursive Experiential-Working Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24876)
*Zhaochen Yu,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Kaiyuan Chen,Zhe Zhao,Mengdi Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Recuris提出了一种递归的"经验-工作记忆"架构，通过工作记忆跟踪任务进度并引导技能选择、经验记忆提供技能库，将执行过程转化为结构化证据来定位失败组件，再由固定的元Agent对技能记忆进行验证门控的局部更新，形成有界的递归记忆进化循环。在四个长时程基准和十个模型上，37个完成的模型-基准组合中有35个提升了任务成功率，在tau-bench上将Claude Opus 5提升至87.9%，最长任务上最高提升+32.2点，常见长时程失败最多降低80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）在长时程任务中仍然困难，因为不断增长的历史记录会模糊任务状态并导致技能调用错位。现有方法无法在累积经验的同时保持对当前任务状态的清晰认知。

Method: Recuris构建递归的Experiential-Working Memory架构：工作记忆跟踪任务进度并基于当前需求（而非完整历史）从经验记忆中引导技能选择；这种耦合将执行过程转化为结构化证据，用于将失败定位到特定记忆组件；一个固定的元Agent将这些证据转化为经过验证门控的技能记忆局部更新，重塑后续执行并产生新证据，从而形成有界的递归记忆进化循环。

Result: 在4个长时程基准、10个模型上，37个完成的模型-基准组合中35个提升任务成功率。在tau-bench上为GPT-5.6 Sol增加+17.8点、为Claude Opus 5增加+15.6点（达到87.9%），在SkillFlow上为Qwen3.6-27B/35B分别增加+16.6/+13.5点。最长任务上优势扩大到+32.2点，常见长时程失败最多下降80%。

Conclusion: 递归进化的记忆可作为RSI的可扩展基础，使智能体能够持续将累积经验转化为日益有效的长时程行为，定位了递归记忆进化作为长时程智能体能力提升的关键路径。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) remains hard in long-horizon tasks, where growing histories obscure the task state and misalign skill invocation. We introduce Recuris, a recursive Experiential-Working Memory architecture for long-horizon agent harnesses, in which Working Memory tracks task progress and guides skill selection from Experiential Memory, grounding skill use in current needs rather than the full history. This coupling also turns execution into structured evidence that localizes failures to specific memory components. Across tasks, a fixed Meta-Agent turns that evidence into localized, validation-gated updates to Skill Memory that reshape execution and yield new evidence, forming a bounded recursive memory-evolution loop. Across four long-horizon benchmarks and ten models, Recuris improves task success in 35 of the 37 completed model-benchmark pairs, carrying frontier models to SOTA-level task success: on tau-bench it adds +17.8 points to GPT-5.6 Sol and +15.6 to Claude Opus 5, taking Opus 5 to 87.9%, and +16.6/+13.5 points on Qwen3.6-27B/35B on SkillFlow. The advantage widens as the interaction horizon grows, to +32.2 points on the longest tasks, and common long-horizon failures fall by up to 80%. These results position recursively evolving memory as a scalable foundation for RSI, enabling agents to continuously transform accumulated experience into increasingly effective long-horizon behavior. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Recuris

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [AdaWidth: Query-Adaptive Embedding Width for Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23862)
*Shubing Yang,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AdaWidth提出了一种自适应的维度缩减方法，根据每个查询的实际需求动态调整需要评估的嵌入维度数量。通过正交前缀适配器将判别性信号集中到前导坐标，并使用轻量级路由器基于查询已产生的排序结果来决定是否扩展维度。实验表明，在六个检索任务和五个冻结编码器上，AdaWidth比SOTA维度缩减方法节省55%到84%的维度，同时保持NDCG@10性能不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维嵌入是稠密检索的核心，但并非所有维度都需要在检索时评估。现有方法要么对所有查询截断相同的前导维度，要么为每个查询掩码不同子集但仍需存储和访问完整嵌入。然而，同一任务中不同查询稳定其排序所需的维度数量差异很大，因此需要针对每个查询自适应地调整评估维度数。

Method: AdaWidth包含两个关键组件：(1) 正交前缀适配器——对查询和文档应用单个学习的旋转，将判别性信号集中到前导坐标，同时保持全宽度内积不变；(2) 轻量级路由器——读取查询已产生排名的顺序统计量，仅对顶部结果会改变的那些查询评估更多维度。此外，作者推导了前缀充分性分析，表明所需维度数由检索截断处的竞争文档决定。

Result: 在六个检索任务和五个冻结编码器上，AdaWidth在匹配NDCG@10性能的前提下，相比SOTA维度缩减方法节省了55%到84%的每查询维度数。

Conclusion: 理论分析显示所需维度数随语料库大小呈对数增长，随检索深度呈对数减少，且在各查询间呈现重尾分布。这验证了查询间维度需求的显著异质性，也印证了自适应策略的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: High-dimensional embeddings are central to dense retrieval, but not all of these dimensions need to be evaluated at retrieval time. Existing methods reduce dimensions in two ways: truncating the same leading dimensions for every query, or masking a different subset for each query while still storing and accessing the full embedding. Yet queries within a single task differ widely in the number of dimensions they need for their rankings to stabilize.
  We introduce AdaWidth, which adapts the number of evaluated dimensions to each query within a shared prefix representation. An orthogonal prefix adapter applies a single learned rotation to queries and documents alike, concentrating discriminative signal in leading coordinates while leaving every full width inner product unchanged. A lightweight router then reads order statistics off the ranking a query has already produced, and evaluates more dimensions only for the queries whose top results would change. We further derive a prefix sufficiency analysis showing that the required number of dimensions is set by the competing documents at the retrieval cutoff: it grows logarithmically with corpus size, decreases logarithmically with retrieval depth, and remains heavy-tailed across queries. Across six retrieval tasks and five frozen encoders, AdaWidth matches the NDCG@10 of state-of-the-art dimensionality reduction using 55% to 84% fewer dimensions per query.

</details>


### [237] [Wontopos Tablet 2: Measuring Multilingual and Multimodal Memory Retrieval Without Lexical Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23920)
*Sunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍tablet-2，一个生产级语言模型长期记忆引擎，在文本基准（LongMemEval-S 95.7%，BEAM-1M 67.5%）和跨语言无文字照片检索（与BM25对比95.2% vs 19.0% recall@5）上表现优异。其检索路径不含词法匹配、关键词评分或独立语言模型。论文重点强调基准分数单独意义有限，通过受控消融展示读者模型和重问预算等设置对结果的影响，并诚实报告三个对自身不利的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆引擎评测仅报告单一基准分数，未区分引擎、读者模型、重问预算等各组件的贡献，且缺乏跨语言无文本模态的评估。作者希望展示tablet-2在传统文本基准和跨语言视觉检索上的表现，同时揭示基准分数单独解读的局限性，推动更严谨的评测方式。

Method: 在文本维度，使用LongMemEval-S（500问）和BEAM-1M（700问/221万条记忆）评测，并做受控消融：固定引擎、语料、设置和评判者，仅更换读者模型或调整重问预算来量化各因素贡献。在多模态维度，构建70个跨语言存储-查询语言组合，与BM25（强配置）对比recall@5；在300张无字幕Crossmodal-3600照片、14种语言上对比稠密基线，测量跨语言表现方差。

Result: 文本基准：LongMemEval-S 95.7%，BEAM-1M 67.5%。仅换读者模型可使LongMemEval-S变动2.0分；仅调重问预算可使BEAM-1M变动8.9分。多模态：与BM25对比达95.2% vs 19.0% mean recall@5（BM25在54个组合中为零）；稠密基线跨语言退化严重（某模型英语91.0% vs 俄语4.7%），tablet-2跨语言方差14.0分远小于基线27.5和27.7。不利结果：斯瓦希里语仅53.0%、泰卢固语64.0%；附字幕使跨语言检索降11.4分；某设置省略导致韩语top-1下降37分。

Conclusion: tablet-2在文本长期记忆和多模态跨语言检索上均为强基线，检索路径无需词法匹配或语言模型。但基准分数单独意义有限，需披露读者模型、重问预算等设置细节才能进行公平对比。报告三个对自身不利的结果表明评测的诚实性，同时提示低资源语言退化、字幕干扰和设置敏感性仍是值得关注的挑战。

Abstract: We measure tablet-2, a production long-term memory engine for language models, on the text benchmarks the field already uses and on cross-lingual retrieval of photographs stored with no text at all. Its retrieval path contains no lexical matching, no keyword scoring, and no language model of its own.
  On LongMemEval-S (500 questions) it scores 95.7% [93.4, 97.1]; on BEAM-1M (700 questions, 2.21M stored memories) 67.5% [64.8, 70.2]. Those are question-sampling intervals, not the run-to-run spread, which is an order of magnitude narrower.
  Most of the paper is about how little they mean alone. Holding engine, corpus, settings and judge fixed, changing only the reader moves LongMemEval-S by 2.0 points; changing only the re-ask budget moves BEAM-1M by 8.9. Neither is stated in the reports we compare against, and the second exceeds most gaps there, so we give that table as a placement and not a ranking.
  For the multimodal axis we run two controls. Against BM25, configured as strongly as we could, we reach 95.2% mean recall@5 over 70 store-and-query language cells where BM25 reaches 19.0% and is exactly zero in 54. On captionless photographs a lexical method has no document to score at all. Open dense baselines on 300 Crossmodal-3600 photographs in 14 languages show that density confers no language independence: one scores 91.0% on English and 4.7% on Russian from identical image vectors, and a multilingual variant collapses on Telugu and Swahili. Our spread across languages is 14.0 against their 27.5 and 27.7.
  Three results run against us and are reported at equal weight: low-resource languages degrade sharply (Swahili 53.0%, Telugu 64.0%), attaching captions lowers cross-lingual retrieval by 11.4 points, and one setting omitted into one stage of our own retrieval cost 37 points of Korean top-1 accuracy while leaving nine languages untouched.

</details>


### [238] [Hybrid Semantic Tool Discovery for Enterprise MCP Gateway: Architecture and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23992)
*Olympia Saha,Amy Wang,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个通过选择性上下文优化解决MCP工具暴露问题的框架，将工具暴露重新定义为上下文选择问题，仅注入当前步骤相关的工具，通过tool_search和execute_tool两个MCP元工具实现混合检索，在PayPal生产中实现工具token消耗从140.2k降至1.3k（99%的减少）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理MCP服务器架构虽然聚合了多个后端服务器形成安全网关，但带来了两个叠加挑战：1）完整工具模式在用户查询前就饱和了模型上下文窗口，形成上下文工程瓶颈；2）200+ MCP服务器中2000+索引工具让用户和代理难以发现最佳工具。现有提示缓存只能降低重处理成本，既不能释放上下文容量也不能提高准确性。

Method: SCOUT将工具暴露重新框定为上下文选择问题，仅注入与当前步骤相关的工具。具体方法包括：1）暴露两个MCP元工具tool_search和execute_tool；2）tool_search执行混合检索，结合BM25稀疏匹配与稠密向量搜索，通过倒数排名融合（Reciprocal Rank Fusion）返回top-k相关工具；3）由零停机目录更新管道支撑工具索引的持续更新。

Result: 在PayPal生产中，SCOUT将MCP工具token消耗从140.2k token（占上下文70.1%）降至1.3k token（占0.8%），实现了99%的token减少，并降低了企业规模下每次查询的推理成本。

Conclusion: SCOUT以标准MCP工具的形式呈现，因此是模型无关的，无需客户端修改，同时解决了上下文饱和和工具发现两大挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents invoke external tools to retrieve and reason over information beyond pretrained knowledge. The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how such tools are surfaced, and a proxy MCP server aggregates many backend servers behind a single endpoint providing a secure, governable chokepoint for authentication, policy enforcement, and observability. This architecture creates two compounding challenges: a context-engineering bottleneck where full tool schemas saturate the model context window before any user query, and a tool discoverability barrier where users and agents cannot identify the best tool among 2,000+ indexed tools across 200+ MCP servers. Prompt caching reduces reprocessing cost but neither frees context capacity nor improves accuracy. We present SCOUT (Selective Context Optimization for Universal Tooling), which reframes tool exposure as a context-selection problem, injecting only tools relevant to the current step. SCOUT surfaces two MCP meta-tools -- tool_search and execute_tool -- where tool_search performs hybrid retrieval, fusing BM25 sparse matching with dense vector search via Reciprocal Rank Fusion to return the top-k relevant tools. Backed by zero-downtime catalog update pipelines, SCOUT resolves both context saturation and tool discovery challenges. In production at PayPal, SCOUT reduces MCP tool-token consumption from 140.2k tokens (70.1% of context) to 1.3k tokens (0.8%), a 99% reduction, cutting per-query inference cost at enterprise scale. Because SCOUT is surfaced as standard MCP tools, it is model-agnostic and requires no client-side modifications.

</details>


### [239] [TAGR: Temporally Adaptive Generative Recommendation for Industrial Live-Streaming Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24034)
*Wencai Ye,Guangyi Liu,Chaoyi Wang,Wenbin Luo,Shengyu Wang,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Quanming Yao,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TAGR，一个面向直播广告的时序自适应生成式推荐框架，通过三个层面的自适应机制（直播广告词元化、用户意图建模、偏好对齐）解决静态生成式推荐在动态直播场景下的失效问题，在实际电商直播广告平台上部署后直播进入率和购物车点击率分别提升8.5%和7.4%，收入提升16.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播广告是短视频和电商平台的重要变现渠道，其直播内容、推广商品和用户反馈快速变化，对推荐模型的时效性有极高要求。现有生成式推荐器主要面向静态领域设计，在三个层面存在缺陷：静态语义ID（SID）无法追踪不断演化的直播广告；单尺度行为建模无法捕捉用户意图的持续转移；偏好优化在新鲜的在策略反馈与训练稳定性之间难以权衡。因此需要一套时序自适应的生成式推荐方案。

Method: TAGR框架在三个层面进行时序自适应：1）词元层面，提出Live Semantic-Collaborative ID（LSID），周期性根据当前直播场景和推广商品刷新每个活跃广告的SID，同时保留稳定的分层词元词汇表用于自回归生成；2）意图层面，提出Intent-Aware Generation（IAG），多时间粒度建模直播房间进入历史作为主意图序列，辅助行为作为独立输入，并利用请求后意图证据和业务价值对下一词元预测（NTP）进行加权；3）对齐层面，提出Intermittent On-Policy Preference Optimization（IOPO），周期性从当前策略采样新鲜候选组，进行行为对齐和价值对齐的偏好更新，并与监督NTP维护交替执行以保持学习到的行为分布。

Result: 在大规模电商直播广告平台部署后，TAGR相比生产基线在直播进入率上提升8.5%，购物车点击率提升7.4%，收入提升16.1%，验证了时序自适应生成式推荐在直播广告场景中的有效性和工业可行性。

Conclusion: TAGR通过三个层面的时序自适应设计（LSID词元化、IAG意图建模、IOPO偏好对齐），系统性地解决了生成式推荐在动态直播广告场景下的时效性问题，实验结果证明了该框架在效果和工业部署方面均具有显著优势，为生成式推荐在实时动态场景中的应用提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Live-streaming advertising is an important monetization channel on short-video and e-commerce platforms, where rapidly changing live content, promoted products, and user feedback impose strong freshness requirements on recommendation models. Existing generative recommenders designed for static domains fail at three levels: static semantic IDs (SID) cannot track evolving live ads; single-scale behavior modeling misses shifting intent; preference optimization conflicts between fresh on-policy feedback and training stability. We propose TAGR, a generative recommendation framework with temporal adaptation at three levels: live-ad tokenization, user intent modeling, and preference alignment. At the token level, Live Semantic-Collaborative ID (LSID) periodically refreshes each active ad's SID based on its current live scene and promoted products, while retaining a stable hierarchical token vocabulary for autoregressive generation. At the intent level, Intent-Aware Generation (IAG) models live-room entry histories at multiple temporal granularities as the primary intent sequence, keeps auxiliary behaviors as separate inputs, and weights next-token prediction (NTP) using post-request intent evidence and business value. At the alignment level, Intermittent On-Policy Preference Optimization (IOPO) periodically samples fresh candidate groups from the current policy and performs behavior- and value-aligned preference updates interleaved with supervised NTP maintenance to preserve learned behavior distribution. Deployed on a large-scale e-commerce live-stream advertising platform, TAGR improves live-room entry and shopping-cart click rates by 8.5% and 7.4%, respectively, and achieves a 16.1% revenue lift over the production baseline. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and industrial viability of temporally adaptive generative recommendation for live-stream advertising.

</details>


### [240] [SQLite is Enough. Lexical, Semantic, and Hybrid Search with scrydb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24060)
*Timo Breuer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: scrydb 是一个基于 SQLite 的 Python 库，支持词法检索（FTS5）、语义检索（sqlite-vec）以及混合检索（重排序和融合），并在多个 IR 基准上验证了其在关键词匹配、语义相似度和排名融合方面的有效性，同时探讨了查询延迟与效率-效果权衡。代码以 MIT 许可证开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信息检索或智能体搜索系统往往依赖复杂且重量级的搜索引擎或向量数据库，缺少轻量级、易于嵌入的解决方案。作者希望通过直接在 SQLite 中整合词法与语义检索能力，为下游 IR 任务和智能体搜索提供一种轻量的替代方案。

Method: 利用 SQLite 的 FTS5 扩展实现词法（全文）搜索，利用 sqlite-vec 扩展实现语义（向量）搜索，并提供重排序与排名融合功能，将词法与语义检索结果进行组合，形成混合检索流程。

Result: 在多个 IR 基准数据集上验证了 scrydb 在基于关键词匹配、语义相似度和排名融合的文本检索中的有效性，同时提供了查询延迟数据以及效率与效果之间的权衡分析。

Conclusion: scrydb 提供了一种轻量、开源（MIT 许可）的基于 SQLite 的检索方案，可有效支持词法、语义和混合搜索，为 IR 与智能体搜索等下游任务提供实用工具。

Abstract: This work introduces scrydb, a Python library that enables lexical, semantic, and hybrid search within SQLite. For lexical search, scrydb leverages SQLite's full-text search extension FTS5. Semantic search builds on sqlite-vec, a SQLite extension for vector search. Furthermore, the library allows users to rerank and fuse retrieval results to combine both lexical and semantic approaches, providing a lightweight solution for downstream tasks in information retrieval (IR) or agentic search. We evaluate scrydb on various IR benchmark datasets and demonstrate its effectiveness in text retrieval based on keyword matching, semantic similarity, and rank fusion. In addition, we provide insights into query latency and the trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness. scrydb is available under the MIT license.

</details>


### [241] [RetrievalFormer: A Dual-Encoder Transformer for Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbor Retrieval and Cold-Item Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24079)
*Theodore Rogers,Joe Standerfer,Dmitrii Timoshenko,Haoxue Li,Zuhaib Akhtar,Soyoung Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究共享搜索-推荐索引中如何为全新物品（从未展示或访问过的物品）提供检索能力。在公开日志中发现38.6%的查询-搜索展示涉及此前从未见过的物品，双编码器检索通过特征塔实现这种"开放性"，在用户冷启动场景下与热启动基准性能持平。与ID-softmax推荐器需要重训练不同，双编码器保持索引对物品的开放性。在推荐任务上，温启动准确率略逊最强重训练基线，但在严格冷启动评估中，内容塔的Recall@20达到最强专门方法的1.4倍。论文同时揭示了float32时间戳bug、全量softmax训练的收益与内存瓶颈等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的搜索与推荐共享索引必须仅凭特征为全新物品打分，因为没有搜索探索槽位来引入新物品。然而，现有的ID-softmax推荐器需要对新物品重训练才能支持检索，无法维持索引的"开放性"。本文旨在研究双编码器检索系统能否在这一设定下保持索引对新物品的开放能力，并在推荐和冷启动场景下量化其性能代价与收益。

Method: 论文在公开日志（覆盖搜索与推荐两个表面、同一目录）上，分析held-out查询-搜索展示中新物品的占比（38.6%），对比双编码器特征塔与ID-softmax推荐器的差异。通过99个采样负样本与词法基线进行对比验证。在推荐端，对六个顺序基线进行五轮重训练与调参，并通过修正后的留一法目标进行评估（修正了float32时间戳bug对19.7%用户目标的排序错误）。在MovieLens-1M和MIND数据集上，比较双编码器与重训练基线的温启动和严格零泄漏冷启动性能。进一步通过精确全量softmax训练（替代采样InfoNCE）和近似最近邻搜索实验来剖析性能差距来源。

Result: 双编码器特征塔能在用户冷启动场景下无需冷特定训练即以0.9595 Recall@20（vs 热启动0.9510）服务新物品需求。在MovieLens-1M上，温启动准确率比最强重训练基线低5.2% Recall@20和11.4% NDCG@20；在MIND上差距缩小至0.8-3.6%，但模型排名第六。严格的零泄漏冷启动评估中，内容塔达到0.172±0.006 Recall@20，是最强专门重训练方法的1.4倍，是训练无关基线的3倍。全量softmax训练将MIND-small的Recall@20提升54%、MovieLens-1M提升6.9%（对比采样InfoNCE），但每一步需重算整个目录且在24万物品时耗尽加速器内存。近似最近邻搜索无法解释剩余差距，历史窗口扫描解释了配方余下差距的一半。

Conclusion: 双编码器检索能够维持共享索引对新物品的开放性，无需重训练即可支持从未见过的物品，这说明内容塔为搜索侧的新物品需求提供了可行路径。然而，推荐任务上温启动性能与最强重训练基线存在差距，全量softmax的精确训练虽能显著提升Recall@20但在目录规模下受限于内存。论文指出"目录规模下的精确质量训练"仍是未解决的关键开放问题。

Abstract: A shared search-and-recommendation index must score new items from features alone because search has no exploration slot. In a public log covering both surfaces over one catalog, $38.6\%$ of held-out query-search impressions show an item never previously shown or visited. For user-cold engagements, the feature-based tower serves this demand without measurable loss against $99$ sampled negatives ($0.9595$ Recall@20 versus $0.9510$ warm). A lexical baseline reaches similar parity, while a full-catalog check remains statistically undecided. Dual-encoder retrieval therefore keeps the index \emph{open} to new items, unlike an ID-softmax recommender that requires retraining. We price this openness on recommendation against six sequential baselines, each retrained and tuned through five rounds on corrected targets. A float32 timestamp bug had reordered leave-one-out targets for $19.7\%$ of users. On MovieLens-1M, warm accuracy trails the strongest retrained baseline by $5.2\%$ Recall@20 and $11.4\%$ NDCG@20. On MIND, the gap narrows to $0.8$--$3.6\%$ relative to the five strongest baselines, though the model ranks sixth of seven. Under strict zero-leakage cold-start evaluation, the content tower achieves $0.172 \pm 0.006$ Recall@20, $1.4\times$ the strongest retrained dedicated method ($0.124 \pm 0.007$) and $3\times$ a training-free floor, without cold-specific training. Exact full-softmax training raises Recall@20 by $54\%$ on MIND-small and $6.9\%$ on MovieLens-1M over sampled InfoNCE, but recomputes the full catalog each step and exhausts accelerator memory at $240$K items. Approximate nearest-neighbor search explains none of the remaining gap, serving cost does not regress against ID-softmax retrieval, and a history-window sweep explains half the post-recipe remainder. Exact-quality training at catalog scale remains the open problem.

</details>


### [242] [CodeHID: Learning an Addressable Hierarchical Code Index for Generative Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24089)
*Zhen Li,Yuhong Chen,Wenhao Xu,Xiaodong Li,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeHID，一种生成式代码检索框架，将代码检索从扁平候选匹配重新构建为从粗到细的语义地址生成。通过伪邻居引导的DocID学习和双阶段DocID生成引导两大核心组件，在CoSQA和ProCQA基准上大幅超越现有稀疏、稠密及生成式检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索模型主要依赖扁平匹配范式，将代码片段视为独立候选，难以区分语义相似的代码候选。虽然生成式检索可以通过构建可学习索引来改善这一问题，但直接套用生成式检索到代码检索任务会导致标识符空间的前缀不对应于有意义的代码语义区域。

Method: CodeHID包含两个核心组件：(1) Pseudo-Neighbor Guided DocID Learning：通过多级残差量化和k近邻伪监督构建全局静态层次索引，确保语义相关的代码片段共享前缀，同时保持目标级可分离性；(2) Dual-Phase DocID Generation Guidance：结合训练阶段排序增强（使用难负样本和排序蒸馏）与推理阶段的候选约束和前缀感知解码，可靠地导航固定索引。

Result: 在CoSQA和ProCQA基准上的大量实验表明，CodeHID在大多数情况下大幅优于现有的稀疏检索、预训练代码模型、稠密代码检索和生成式检索基线，尤其在排名第一的检索指标上取得了特别强的改进。

Conclusion: CodeHID通过生成式地址生成将代码检索从扁平匹配转化为粗到细的层次语义检索，有效解决了生成式检索在代码领域的前缀语义不对齐问题，验证了层次化语义索引在代码检索中的有效性。

Abstract: Code retrieval models have predominantly relied on a flat matching paradigm that treats code snippets as independent candidates, making them less capable of distinguishing similar code candidates. Generative retrieval offers a solution by constructing a learnable index over the code corpus, guiding the retriever to better understand how code candidates are semantically organized and addressed. However, naively applying generative retrieval in the code retrieval task may result in operating over an identifier space whose prefixes do not correspond to meaningful code-semantic regions. In this paper, we propose CodeHID, a generative code retrieval framework that reformulates the code retrieval task from flat candidate matching to coarse-to-fine semantic address generation. CodeHID relies on two core components. First, Pseudo-Neighbor Guided DocID Learning constructs a globally static hierarchical index by applying multi-level residual quantization and $k$-nearest-neighbor pseudo-supervision, ensuring that semantically related code snippets share prefixes while preserving target-level separability. Second, Dual-Phase DocID Generation Guidance reliably navigates this fixed index by combining training-side ranking enhancements, using hard negatives and rank distillation, with inference-side candidate constraints and prefix-aware decoding. Extensive experiments on CoSQA and ProCQA benchmarks demonstrate that CodeHID outperforms existing sparse retrieval, pre-trained code models, dense code retrieval, and generative retrieval baselines by a large margin in most cases, achieving particularly strong improvements in rank-one retrieval metrics.

</details>


### [243] [Native Multimodal Representation Learning for Click-Through Rate Prediction in E-Commerce Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24091)
*Chao Yi,Feifan Yang,Jiawei Feng,Sishuo Chen,Zhangming Chan,Xiang-Rong Sheng,Han Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"先挖掘后训练"(Mine-Then-Train)方法，用于在CTR预测任务中学习原生多模态表示。通过从原始CTR数据中挖掘高质量、可用多模态语义解释的训练样本，并以此微调多模态编码器，使其更好地与用户点击偏好对齐，从而解决了端到端联合训练中因用户行为同时受多模态和非多模态因素驱动而导致的监督信号模糊、编码器更新不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态CTR预测采用两阶段范式：先预训练多模态编码器，再将其表示融入CTR模型。但多模态预训练的目标函数和数据分布与CTR任务存在差异，限制了多模态表示在下游任务中的效果。端到端联合训练虽然直观，但实验发现其无法带来性能提升，原因在于原始CTR数据中用户行为同时由多模态语义和非多模态因素驱动，导致监督信号模糊、编码器更新不一致。

Method: 提出Mine-Then-Train方法：(1) 从原始CTR数据中挖掘高质量、多模态可解释的训练样本；(2) 利用这些样本对多模态编码器进行微调，使其更好地与用户点击偏好对齐；(3) 将微调后的多模态表示集成到CTR预测模型中。

Result: 离线和在线实验均验证了所提方法的有效性，证明了Mine-Then-Train方法相比现有的多模态应用范式能够带来CTR预测性能的提升。

Conclusion: 本文的关键洞察是：端到端联合训练多模态编码器和CTR模型并非有效方案，关键在于筛选出高质量、多模态可解释的样本用于编码器微调，从而实现多模态表示与用户点击偏好的更好对齐。

Abstract: Multimodal representations have been widely adopted in industrial e-commerce recommendation systems. Due to their strong semantic understanding and generalization capabilities, they enhance the performance of traditional sparse ID-based Click-Through Rate (CTR) prediction models. Current multimodal application frameworks in the CTR prediction task typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first, pre-training a multimodal encoder on data from specific recommendation scenarios; second, extracting items' multimodal representations using this pre-trained multimodal encoder and integrating them into the CTR prediction model. However, the training objectives and data distribution of multimodal pre-training tasks often differ from those of the CTR prediction task, which limits the effectiveness of multimodal representation on downstream tasks. In this paper, we focus on how to learn Native Multimodal Representation for the CTR prediction task. One intuitive solution is to jointly train the multimodal encoder and CTR model end-to-end on the CTR task, with the expectation that the encoder can automatically learn downstream-relevant knowledge. However, we find that the end-to-end training does not bring performance improvements to existing multimodal application paradigms. Our analysis reveals that user behaviors in raw CTR data are driven by both multimodal semantics and non-multimodal factors, leading to ambiguous supervision and inconsistent encoder updates. To address this, we propose a Mine-Then-Train method that mines high-quality, multimodally interpretable training samples from CTR data and uses them to fine-tune the multimodal encoder for better alignment with user click preferences. Offline and online experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [244] [Tlow: Flow-based Item Tokenizer for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24176)
*Nian Li,Chonggang Song,Jingtao Ding,Lingling Yi,Yong Li,Qingmin Liao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Tlow，一种基于流的物品分词器，将原始语义嵌入转化为符合标准正态分布的潜在空间，获得维度独立性和分布简单性，从而实现高效的独立分词。通过引入码本引导对齐码本空间与token嵌入空间，显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐模型使用随机分配的item ID存在参数过多和冷启动问题。现有item分词器中，RQ-VAE因码本间固有依赖导致解码效率低，而高效独立的OPQ等分词器仍难以处理语义嵌入的维度相关性和分布复杂性。

Method: 提出Tlow（flow-based item Tokenizer），利用归一化流将原始语义嵌入变换到符合统一标准正态分布的潜在空间，实现维度独立和分布简单性；在该潜在空间上执行独立分词获得语义清晰的token ID；并引入码本引导机制将对齐码本空间与token嵌入空间，辅助学习更具语义区分度的token嵌入。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上的离线实验表明Tlow的分词和码本引导显著提升推荐性能；跨域和多模态推荐上的改进证明了在简化嵌入空间中进行item分词的有效性。在中国最大社交平台微信上的多模态检索在线实验中，基于token ID的检索模型将用户CTR在全球范围内提升10.32%，新品提升11.64%。

Conclusion: Tlow通过流变换实现维度独立且分布统一的潜在嵌入空间，使独立分词可以有效工作，同时码本引导进一步提升性能。离线与在线实验均验证了其强大的分布变换能力和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Item tokenizer encodes semantic embeddings into token IDs to replace the randomly assigned item IDs used in traditional recommendation models, fundamentally addressing the problems of excessive parameters and cold starts. However, the most common tokenizer, RQ-VAE, suffers from low decoding efficiency due to the inherent dependencies among its codebooks. Meanwhile, efficient independent tokenizers such as optimized product quantization (OPQ) still struggle with dimensional correlations and distribution complexity of semantic embeddings. In this work, we propose a f\underline{low}-based item \underline{T}okenizer (Tlow) to transform raw semantic embeddings into a latent space where embeddings conform to a unified standard normal distribution, achieving dual advantages of dimensional independence and distributional simplicity. Independent tokenization performed on these latent embeddings yields semantically clear token IDs. Additionally, we introduce a novel codebook guidance to align the codebook space with the token embedding space, further aiding the learning of more semantically distinct token embeddings. Offline experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that Tlow's tokenization and codebook guidance significantly improve recommendation performance. The improvement on cross-domain and multi-modal recommendations also proves the effectiveness of item tokenization in a simplified embedding space. Online experiments for a multi-modal retrieval task on China's largest social media platform WeChat validate Tlow's powerful distribution transformation capability. The retrieval model based on token IDs improves user CTR by 10.32\% globally and by 11.64\% for new items. Our codes are available at https://github.com/wjjln/Tlow.

</details>


### [245] [RecGPT-Mobile-V2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24295)
*Lingqing Zhang,Bin Zhang,Weipeng Huang,Chengfei Lv,Chengyu Lai,Chuxin Chen,Dimin Wang,Han Zhu,Hongtao Cheng,Jialin Zhu,Jian Wang,Jiuning Lin,Junqing Wu,Li Chen,Qichao Ma,Ruiquan Lan,Shuai Zhong,Tao Wang,Xiaodong Zhu,Yinjiang Cai,Yinnan Song,Yipeng Yu,Yuan Liu,Yuning Jiang,Zhaode Wang,Zhibo Xiao,Zhixin Ma,Zihong Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RecGPT-Mobile-V2，一个端到端的端侧个性化Query预测框架，将意图质量与执行效率作为耦合目标，通过分阶段设计（轨迹构建→推荐原生域适应→推理成本优化→蒸馏部署低比特学生模型），实现"充分性导向"而非"均匀简短"的推理。实验证明证据聚焦的短推理链在ROUGE-L和Jaccard上优于完整五阶段推理，完整奖励公式将Query质量从73.2%提升至78.6%，中位CoT长度从62降至14 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧个性化Query预测面临三重挑战：行为轨迹噪声大且多尺度、单条轨迹可能对应多个有效Query、统一推理策略无法平衡简单与复杂实例的计算资源分配。现有方法在过去度计算和不足够容量之间缺乏自适应调节能力，需要将意图质量与执行效率作为耦合目标联合优化。

Method: 框架包含四个阶段：(1) 将异构交互信号转化为证据保留的轨迹表示；(2) 通过域适应和受监督对齐建立推荐原生基础模型；(3) 仅在分组rollout满足可掌握性与效用标准后应用推理成本优化；(4) 将教师模型蒸馏为紧凑学生模型，部署时采用低比特执行、结构化压缩和预算感知的端云路由。训练中使用证据聚焦的短CoT和完整奖励公式（质量+失败率+长度多方面）进行RL优化。

Result: 在对照CoT消融中，证据聚焦的短CoT将ROUGE-L从0.228提升至0.315，Jaccard从0.174提升至0.248，略优于完整五阶段CoT。在受控RL对比中，完整奖励公式将Query质量从质量-only RL的73.2%提升至78.6%，硬失败率从3.6%降至1.6%，中位CoT长度从62降至14 token。在线检索分析显示Query召回通道能够检索到与既有召回通道互补的库存。

Conclusion: 研究支持"充分性导向"而非"均匀简短"的推理范式：应保留决策相关证据，仅在可能改善Query预测时分配额外计算。该框架在端侧部署约束下同时优化了意图质量与推理效率，证明了证据保留与计算自适应的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Personalized Query prediction maps implicit behavioral signals---clicks, favorites, purchases, and post-purchase exploration---to explicit retrieval intent. On-device deployment makes this task particularly challenging: behavioral trajectories are noisy and multi-scale, multiple Queries may be valid for a single trajectory, and a uniform reasoning policy either expends unnecessary computation on simple instances or allocates insufficient capacity to complex ones. We introduce RecGPT-Mobile-V2, an end-to-end framework that treats intent quality and execution efficiency as coupled objectives within a staged design. The framework transforms heterogeneous interactions into an evidence-preserving trajectory, establishes a recommendation-native foundation through domain adaptation and supervised alignment, and applies reasoning-cost optimization only after grouped rollouts meet grounding and utility criteria. The resulting teacher is distilled into a compact student deployed with low-bit execution, structured compression, and budget-aware device--cloud routing. In an aligned CoT ablation, an evidence-focused short rationale increases ROUGE-L from 0.228 to 0.315 and Jaccard from 0.174 to 0.248, while slightly outperforming the full five-stage rationale. In the controlled RL comparison, the complete reward formulation improves Query quality from 73.2% under quality-only RL to 78.6%, lowers the hard-failure rate from 3.6% to 1.6%, and reduces the median CoT length from 62 to 14 tokens. Online retrieval analysis further indicates that the Query recall channel retrieves inventory complementary to that surfaced by established recall channels. Collectively, these findings support sufficiency-oriented rather than uniformly short reasoning: retain decision-relevant evidence and allocate additional computation only when it is likely to improve the predicted Query.

</details>


### [246] [Rethinking Semantic Alignment in LLM-Enhanced Collaborative Filtering: A Spectral Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24363)
*Yedong Jin,Shaowen Peng,Tsunenori Mine,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文从谱域（spectral）视角重新审视LLM增强推荐中的对齐问题，发现协作信号和语义信号各自受益于不同的频谱分量。协作表示由低频平滑分量主导，而语义嵌入包含有用的非主奇异分量。通过分量级评估和训练动力学分析，发现对齐使学习表示越来越集中于主导协作和主语义子空间，减少与非主语义分量的重叠。非主分量在对齐下收益不一致，但通过分量级解耦能持续提升性能，因此提出UniSpecRec，通过信号特定频谱滤波，在不跨空间对齐或无额外可训练参数的情况下实现最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强推荐通常将对齐语义表示与协作嵌入于共享空间，但对齐如何影响LLM编码的信息尚不清楚。作者希望从谱域角度揭示对齐机制对语义信息利用的潜在缺陷，以改进推荐性能。

Method: 首先从谱视角分析，证明协作表示受低频同质性主导、语义嵌入包含非主奇异分量；通过分量级评估和训练动力学分析揭示对齐的负面效应。基于此提出UniSpecRec，采用信号特定频谱滤波，将协作和语义表示保留在各自身份空间中，在预测层进行解耦融合，无需跨空间对齐和额外参数。

Result: 非主分量在对齐设置下增益不一致，但通过分量级解耦能持续改进性能；完整预测级解耦取得最佳总体性能。大量实验验证了UniSpecRec的有效性、效率和泛化性。

Conclusion: 对齐无法有效利用互补的非主语义信息，UniSpecRec通过频谱信号解耦而非跨空间对齐的方式有效融合协作与语义信号，为LLM增强推荐提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-enhanced recommendation commonly align semantic representations with collaborative embeddings in a shared space, yet how alignment affects LLM-encoded information remains unclear. In this work, we revisit LLM-enhanced recommendation from a spectral perspective and show that collaborative and semantic signals benefit from different spectral parts. While collaborative representations are dominated by smooth low-frequency components due to user-item homophily, semantic embeddings contain useful non-principal singular components. Through component-wise evaluation and training-dynamics analysis, we find that alignment increasingly concentrates learned representations in dominant collaborative and principal semantic subspaces, reducing overlap with non-principal semantic components. Controlled comparisons show that non-principal components provide inconsistent gains under alignment but consistently improve performance through component-level decoupling, while full prediction-level decoupling achieves the best overall performance. These results indicate that alignment fails to effectively exploit complementary non-principal semantic information. Motivated by these findings, we propose UniSpecRec (Unifying Spectral Signals for Recommendation), which applies signal-specific spectral filtering while preserving collaborative and semantic representations in their respective spaces. UniSpecRec combines their predictions without cross-space alignment or additional trainable parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness, efficiency, and generalizability.

</details>


### [247] [EviGraph: Towards Verifiable Evidence Construction for Information-Seeking Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24667)
*Jiashun Chen,Yirong Mao,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EviGraph是一个深度搜索框架，将搜索执行与证据记录分离，通过显式的证据图谱结构和过程奖励来监督智能体的证据构建过程。在BrowseComp-Plus上，Qwen3-8B智能体达到35.9%的准确率，显著优于无强化学习的双角色架构（26.9%）和单体智能体（2.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体网络搜索代理通常在一次性的线性交互轨迹中同时进行搜索和证据记录，只优化最终答案的正确性，对中间过程的证据接地（grounding）监督不足——检索到的内容不一定真正支持答案中的论断。

Method: EviGraph将搜索执行与证据记录分离：1) 执行器（executor）规划简洁查询；2) 冻结的证据验证器（frozen evidence verifier）检查源页面并返回带显式极性（polarity）的逐字证据项；3) 共享策略（shared policy）将这些证据映射为添加/支持图谱请求；4) 确定性结构化验证器（deterministic structural validator）检查这些请求。生成的证据图谱既作为持久工作记忆，又作为密集过程奖励的来源，使强化学习能够直接监督证据构建而非仅监督最终答案。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，Qwen3-8B EviGraph代理在匹配交互预算下达到35.9%准确率，对比同一双角色架构无RL时为26.9%，单体代理仅2.7%，且每轮生成更少的token。在BrowseComp、GAIA和XBench上也取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式结构化并奖励证据记录能够显著提升智能体搜索的效果，证据图谱作为可验证的结构化工作记忆与过程奖励来源，是超越仅以最终答案正确性为目标的智能体搜索的有效范式。

Abstract: Agentic Web search can retrieve relevant information without establishing that the retrieved content actually supports the claims used in an answer. Existing agents typically keep search and evidence recording in a linear interaction trace and optimize primarily for final-answer correctness, providing limited supervision for intermediate grounding. We present EviGraph, a deep-search framework that separates search execution from evidence recording while using a shared policy for the trainable roles. An executor plans concise queries, a frozen evidence verifier inspects source pages and returns verbatim evidence items with an explicit polarity, and the policy maps those items to add/support graph requests that are checked by a deterministic structural validator. The resulting graph serves both as persistent working memory and as a source of dense process rewards, enabling reinforcement learning to directly supervise evidence construction rather than only the final answer. On BrowseComp-Plus, a Qwen3-8B EviGraph agent achieves 35.9% accuracy under a matched interaction budget, compared with 26.9% for the same dual-role architecture without reinforcement learning and 2.7% for a monolithic agent, while generating fewer tokens per rollout. Consistent gains on BrowseComp, GAIA, and XBench indicate that explicitly structuring and rewarding evidence recording improves agentic search

</details>


### [248] [Auditing Return Conditioning as a Control Knob: An Offline Diagnostic for Decision Transformer Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24815)
*Jingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究离线return-to-go（RTG）干预是否真正控制推荐系统的奖励。在MovieLens 25M和MyAnimeList 2020上，作者发现只在真实上下文的当前token位置干预RTG（K=20）可显著改变MovieLens的预测类型（+23.61个百分点），但干扰RTG内容或换数据集（MAL）则效果消失。结果呈描述性，作者提出四个检查建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTG干预常被用作测试推荐器可控性的手段，但现有工作很少审计这种干预是否真的有效——改写全部历史RTG会制造越来越合成化的上下文，而只改当前token更局部。因此需要系统的诊断框架来验证RTG是否真正控制奖励。

Method: 在固定窗口的离线设置中，使用Decision Transformer，构造一个RTG局部性梯子（RTG locality ladder，K=20覆盖全上下文、K=1仅当前槽位）、无RTG对照组、logged match和分数奖励检查、以及轨迹内shuffled RTG消融。在MovieLens 25M和MyAnimeList 2020上对类型预测进行评估。

Result: MovieLens上，K=20只改真实上下文位置RTG使Crime预测份额从验证5到95百分位变化+23.61±2.96个百分点，而仅改当前槽位仅+1.77±1.17点；shuffled RTG模型大幅削弱响应（K=20时+2.08±1.20点）。MAL上未出现Drama响应（K=20为-0.03±0.07，K=1为-0.01±0.01）。genre预测准确率在真实RTG、无RTG、shuffled RTG间数值接近。

Conclusion: 由于数据集和焦点类型的选择是探索性的，这些数值是描述性的；跨诊断的模式（局部性、shuffled RTG、MAL上的零结果）并不能确立奖励控制。作者提出四项检查：干预局部性、无RTG基线、奖励检查、RTG内容消融。

Abstract: Offline return-to-go (RTG) sweeps can test whether a recommender conditioned on return is controllable, but the intervention is rarely audited. Rewriting every historical RTG token creates an increasingly synthetic context, while rewriting only the current token is more local. We test this distinction in an offline setting with a fixed window. On MovieLens 25M and MyAnimeList 2020 (MAL), we evaluate a Decision Transformer using an RTG locality ladder, a control without RTG, a logged match and score reward check, and a within-trajectory shuffled RTG ablation. On MovieLens, a $K=20$ intervention that covers the full context, applied only to real context positions, shifts the share of Crime predictions by $+23.61 \pm 2.96$ percentage points from the validation 5th to 95th percentile, whereas changing only the current slot shifts it by $+1.77 \pm 1.17$ points. The shuffled RTG model largely removes this response ($+2.08 \pm 1.20$ points at $K=20$). On MAL, the same protocol does not produce a Drama response: $K=20$ changes Drama by $-0.03 \pm 0.07$ points, and $K=1$ by $-0.01 \pm 0.01$. Genre prediction accuracy is numerically close across real RTG, no RTG, and shuffled RTG, and at $K=1$ logged match rates and matched ratings change little. Because dataset and focus-genre selection were exploratory, these magnitudes are descriptive; the cross-diagnostic pattern across locality, shuffled RTG, and the null result on MAL does not establish reward control. We propose four checks: intervention locality, a no-RTG baseline, a reward check, and RTG-content ablation.

</details>
